Method: 利用伴随系统的辛离散化与反向传播产生相同灵敏度的性质来训练HNN参数；采用预测-校正ODE求解器和定点迭代降低隐式时间步进的计算成本；训练后通过后向误差分析对学习到的哈密顿量进行后处理，得到更精确的修正哈密顿量。

Result: 在非可分离混沌系统上，该方法在系统辨识和能量保持方面展现出数值优势，且计算和内存复杂度更高效；后处理得到的修正哈密顿量比直接使用更精确流映射离散化更准确。

Conclusion: 隐式辛积分器结合伴随灵敏度方法可以有效训练HNN，在保持长期能量守恒的同时降低计算开销，后向误差分析进一步提升了哈密顿量的近似精度。

Abstract: Hamiltonian Neural Networks (HNNs) integrate physical priors into neural models by learning a system's Hamiltonian, improving generalization and sample efficiency. Identifying the system Hamiltonian from noisy observations of state variables is a challenging task. For simulations to faithfully reflect the long-term behavior of Hamiltonian systems, especially energy conservation, it is essential to use symplectic integrators, which preserve the system's geometric structure. This fidelity comes at a cost: implicit symplectic integrators are more computationally intensive and make backpropagation through the ODE solver non-trivial. However, by leveraging the fact that symplectic discretizations of the adjoint system yield the same sensitivities associated by backpropagation, we obtain an efficient method of training the Neural Network parameters. In our work, we explore this alternate method of HNN training under noisy observation of trajectories with our HNN model based on an implicit symplectic integrator. Computationally, a predictor-corrector based ODE solver and fixed point iteration help to mitigate the computational cost of the implicit timestepping, resulting in more efficient generation of gradient updates. We showcase the numerical advantage, in experiments, in system identification and energy preservation on a range of non-separable, chaotic systems and the efficient computation and memory complexity of our method. We also observe that the post-processing of the learned Hamiltonian using backward error analysis yields a modified Hamiltonian that is a more accurate approximation of the true Hamiltonian without the need to use more accurate discretizations of the flow map.

</details>


### [71] [State Representation Matters in Deep Reinforcement Learning: Application to Energy Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27032)
*Jesper Klicks,Sander Vržina,Vincent François-Lavet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究抽水蓄能交易强化学习中状态表征的设计选择，发现单一特征（绝对价格、相对特征或预测特征）表现有限，而组合所有三类特征（绝对+相对+预测）在跨市场迁移中表现最佳，表明状态表征是储能交易RL策略设计的核心环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源交易决策不仅依赖当前市场价格，还受未来市场条件和运营约束影响，因此强化学习智能体的状态表征是一个重要的设计选择，但此前缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 在HydroDam抽水蓄能套利环境中，固定Double DQN智能体架构，仅改变市场特征：绝对价格/日历特征、相对特征（当前价格与近期历史比较）、预测特征，以及三者的组合。使用2007-2011比利时日前电价训练，在2012-2025的同市场测试集和39个其他ENTSO-E市场区域上评估。

Result: 绝对特征仅达到测试集28.8%、跨区域中位数5.7%；相对特征和预测特征单独使用时均低于滚动价格评分启发式。组合特征显著更强：绝对+相对达到测试集49.9%、跨区域中位数39.8%；绝对+相对+预测达到55.6%和47.5%。

Conclusion: 状态表征在储能交易RL中不是次要的预处理选择，而是策略设计的核心组成部分；鲁棒的跨市场迁移需要组合价格尺度、近期相对价格背景和短期预测信息，而非依赖单一特征族。

Abstract: Energy trading decisions depend not only on current market prices, but also on expected future market conditions, and operational constraints. This makes the state representation given to a reinforcement learning agent an important design choice. We study this in HydroDam, a pumped-storage arbitrage environment, using a fixed Double DQN agent. The environment, action space, reward function, network, and training protocol are kept fixed; only the market features are changed. We compare absolute price/calendar features, relative features that compare current prices with recent market history, forecast features, and all combinations of these three feature families. Policies are trained and selected using 2007--2011 Belgian day-ahead prices and evaluated on two test settings: a later same-market test set from 2012--2025 and 39 other ENTSO-E market zones. Absolute features only reaches 28.8% on the test set and a median 5.7% across zones. Relative-only and forecast-only states also stay below a rolling price-score heuristic in the cross-zone median. Combining feature families is much stronger: absolute + relative reaches 49.9% on the test set and a 39.8% cross-zone median, while absolute + relative + forecast reaches 55.6% and 47.5%. These results suggest that state representation is not a minor preprocessing choice in storage-trading RL, but a central part of the policy design: robust transfer requires combining price scale, recent relative price context, and short-horizon forecast information, rather than relying on any single feature family.

</details>


### [72] [Finding Stationary Points by Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27082)
*Helin Wang,Chenyi Zhang,Xiwen Tao,Yexin Zhang,Tongyang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究仅通过比较预言机（给定两点，返回函数值更大的点）访问非凸函数时，寻找驻点的问题。针对二阶可导且满足Lipschitz梯度和Hessian的函数，提出了经典算法（$\widetilde O(n^2/ε^{1.5})$次查询）和量子算法（$\widetilde O(n/ε^{1.5})$次查询）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中许多优化问题无法直接获取梯度信息，仅能通过比较函数值来优化（如黑箱优化、对抗性攻击等）。现有工作缺乏对仅依赖比较预言机的非凸优化理论基础，需要设计高效算法并探索量子加速的可能性。

Method: 1. 设计子程序用于估计归一化Hessian矩阵，精度δ需$\widetilde O(n^2\log(1/δ))$次查询。2. 基于该子程序构建经典算法，达到ε-驻点。3. 引入量子比较预言机模型，利用叠加态查询实现量子加速。

Result: 经典算法：$\widetilde O(n^2/ε^{1.5})$次查询；量子算法：$\widetilde O(n/ε^{1.5})$次查询，实现二次加速（维度n上的平方加速）。

Conclusion: 首次为仅依赖比较预言机的非凸优化提供了理论保证，并展示了量子查询在维度上的显著优势，为量子机器学习和优化算法开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We study the problem of finding stationary points of non-convex functions when access to the objective is provided only through a comparison oracle that, given two points, outputs which has the larger function value. For a twice differentiable $f\colon\mathbb R^n\to\mathbb R$ with Lipschitz gradient and Hessian, we develop an algorithm that visits an $ε$-stationary point using $\widetilde O(n^2/ε^{1.5})$ queries. Our approach uses a subroutine that estimates the normalized Hessian to accuracy $δ$ using $\widetilde O(n^2\log(1/δ))$ queries. We further study this problem with a quantum comparison oracle model where queries can be made in superpositions, and develop the first quantum algorithm that finds an $ε$-stationary point, which takes $\widetilde O(n/ε^{1.5})$ queries.

</details>


### [73] [Data-Free Reservoir Features for Efficient Long-Horizon Cold-Start Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27095)
*Augustinas Jučas,Yangchen Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CIRCLE方法，使用固定的双向二维随机储备特征（BiRC2D）结合流式线性判别分析（SLDA）头，无需回放、外部预训练或骨干网络反向传播，在冷启动无范例类增量学习中，尤其是在长任务序列（50/100/500任务划分）上显著优于现有方法，且训练速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有冷启动无范例类增量学习方法存在计算张力：漂移补偿方法需要重复训练骨干网络，随着任务数增长计算成本急剧上升；而冻结骨干的方法虽计算廉价，但在冷启动场景下性能较弱。本文探索第三种选择：使用完全不拟合图像数据的固定特征提取器。

Method: 提出CIRCLE方法，包含三个核心组件：1）固定双向二维储备特征提取器（BiRC2D），从不进行图像数据拟合；2）流式线性判别分析（SLDA）头，支持闭环流式更新；3）多随机储备实例化组成特征集成，并通过独立SLDA头的softmax输出平均实现预测级集成，可在丰富随机特征与预测集成之间调节偏差-方差权衡。整个方法无需回放、任务边界信息或骨干反向传播，支持样本级训练。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet、ImageNet-Subset和ImageNet-1k基准上，CIRCLE在10-20任务划分下与现有方法相当，在50、100和500任务划分下显著超越强CS-EFCIL基线，且训练速度远快于基于骨干网络训练的漂移补偿方法。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明，BiRC2D风格特征提取器、SLDA头以及平衡的特征/预测集成各自都对最终性能有贡献。固定特征提取器结合流式更新头的范式为冷启动类增量学习提供了一种高效且强竞争力的新路径。

Abstract: Cold-start exemplar-free class-incremental learning requires learning a growing set of classes without replay, external pretraining, or a large initial task. Existing cold-start methods typically either train the backbone throughout the stream and compensate for semantic drift, or freeze a backbone after the first task, producing features biased toward the initial classes. These choices also create a computational tension: drift-compensation methods require repeated backbone training and increasingly expensive updates as the task horizon grows, while frozen-backbone methods are cheap but weak under cold start. We study a third option: a feature extractor that is never fit to image data at all. We propose CIRCLE, a class-incremental classifier built from fixed bidirectional two-dimensional reservoir features, adapted from BiRC2D for image classification, and streaming linear discriminant analysis heads. CIRCLE groups multiple random reservoir instantiations into feature ensembles and averages the softmax outputs of independent SLDA heads, yielding a tunable bias-variance tradeoff between richer random features and prediction-level ensembling. Because the feature extractor is fixed and the head admits streaming closed-form updates, CIRCLE performs sample-wise training without replay, task-boundary information, or backbone backpropagation. On CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, ImageNet-Subset, and ImageNet-1k, CIRCLE is competitive at 10-20 task splits and substantially outperforms strong CS-EFCIL baselines at 50, 100, and 500 task splits, while training much faster than trained-backbone drift-compensation methods. Ablations show that the BiRC2D-style extractor, SLDA head, and balanced feature/prediction ensembling each contribute to the final performance.

</details>


### [74] [Transformer-Based Classification of Bacterial Raman Spectra with LOOCV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27096)
*Jamile Mohammad Jafari,Thomas Bocklitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了基于Transformer的模型在拉曼光谱分类中的性能，采用嵌套留一复制交叉验证框架，与PCA/ICA结合LDA、SVM和随机森林的传统方法对比。在包含5,417个单细胞光谱（6种细菌、9个独立重复）的数据集上，Transformer持续取得最佳分类性能，潜空间类分离度更优，且无需预处理即可直接应用于原始光谱，表现出跨重复鲁棒的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在拉曼光谱分类领域的研究尚不充分，尤其是缺乏系统性评估和与经典方法的严格对比。现有研究常忽略实验重复间变异对模型泛化性能的影响，需要一种更严谨的复制感知验证框架来真实评估模型表现。

Method: 采用嵌套留一复制交叉验证框架，使用5,417个单细胞拉曼光谱数据集（6种细菌×9个独立重复）。将基于Transformer的方法与PCA+LDA、PCA+SVM、PCA+随机森林、ICA+LDA、ICA+SVM、ICA+随机森林等六种传统机器学习流水线进行全面比较。评估了分类准确率、潜在特征空间的类分离度以及无需预处理的端到端性能。

Result: Transformer在所有独立测试复制上持续取得最高分类性能，显著优于所有传统方法。其学习到的潜在特征空间相比PCA和ICA表示具有更好的类间分离度。即使在直接应用于未预处理的原始拉曼光谱时，Transformer仍保持优越性能，且行为在不同测量复制间保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: Transformer模型在拉曼光谱分类中具有显著潜力，能够实现更准确、更鲁棒的分类，且对预处理依赖度低。研究同时强调了复制感知验证框架在模型评估中的重要性，为拉曼光谱分析的深度学习应用提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have recently attracted increasing attention for Raman spectral classification. In this study, a transformer-based approach was systematically evaluated using a nested leave-one-replicate-out cross-validation framework and compared with conventional machine-learning pipelines combining PCA or ICA with LDA, SVM, and Random Forest classifiers. A bacterial Raman dataset comprising 5,417 single-cell spectra from six bacterial species and nine independent measurement replicates was used. The transformer consistently achieved the highest classification performance across independent test replicates and significantly outperformed all conventional approaches. Analysis of the learned latent feature space revealed improved class separation compared with PCA- and ICA-based representations. Furthermore, the transformer maintained superior performance when applied directly to raw Raman spectra without preprocessing, demonstrating robust behavior across measurement replicates. These findings highlight the potential of transformer-based models for robust Raman spectral classification and emphasize the importance of replicate-aware validation for realistic model evaluation.

</details>


### [75] [Heavy-Ball Q-Learning with Residual Weighting Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27112)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种修正的heavy-ball Q-learning方法，基于切换线性系统和联合谱半径框架，证明了其收敛性以及在特定条件下比标准Q-learning更快的收敛速度，并将结果扩展到线性函数逼近场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Q-learning方法收敛速度有限，heavy-ball动量虽在优化中广泛使用，但其在Q-learning中的理论保障尚不明确。本文旨在提供一种严格的理论框架来分析和加速Q-learning。

Method: 将Q-learning算法表示为切换线性系统，利用联合谱半径分析算法的收敛性和加速条件；提出修正的heavy-ball Q-learning方法，并扩展到线性函数逼近情形。

Result: 证明了修正heavy-ball Q-learning的收敛性，给出了其比标准Q-learning收敛更快的理论条件；在线性函数逼近下也得到了类似的收敛与加速结论。

Conclusion: 切换线性系统视角为分析Q-learning提供了新框架，揭示了heavy-ball动量加速Q-learning的机理，为后续动量RL算法的理论分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper proposes a corrected heavy-ball Q-learning method for reinforcement learning (RL) and establishes its convergence. It also identifies conditions under which the method is theoretically guaranteed to converge faster than standard Q-learning. The same construction is then extended to Q-learning with linear function approximation, where analogous convergence and acceleration statements are derived. The analysis is based on a switched linear system (SLS) representation of Q-learning algorithms and on the joint spectral radius (JSR) of the associated switching families. This SLS viewpoint is not commonly used in standard analyses of Q-learning, and it provides a complementary framework and new insight into how heavy-ball momentum can accelerate Q-learning.

</details>


### [76] [Cross-Head Attention Uplift Network with Inverse Propensity Score under Unobserved Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27114)
*Haoran Zhang,Chuanpu Li,Yuxin Fu,Bin Tong,Guan Wang,Bo Zheng,Feng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CHAUN和RA-IPS方法，分别解决 uplift建模中跨组相似性利用和未观测混淆下的去偏问题，在公开和工业数据集上取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Uplift建模面临两大挑战：灵活利用组间相似性以提升判别能力，以及在未观测混淆场景下进行去偏处理。

Method: 提出CHAUN网络（共享特征嵌入+跨头注意力机制）动态整合处理组和对照组的表征；提出RA-IPS方法，通过对抗优化在约束不确定集内调整倾向权重以缓解未观测变量带来的偏差。

Result: 在CRITEO-UPLIFT、LAZADA及工业电商数据集上，CHAUN的QINI分数相对提升最高达25.6%；RA-IPS在未观测混淆下比标准IPS提升5.4%。

Conclusion: 所提方法在真实因果推断任务中有效，理论和实验均验证了其优越性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Uplift modeling, crucial for estimating individual treatment effects (ITE), faces dual challenges: flexibly leveraging inter-group similarity to enhance discriminative power and debiasing under unobserved confounding scenarios. In this paper, we propose the Cross-Head Attention Uplift Network (CHAUN) and Robust Adversarial Inverse Propensity Score (RA-IPS) method to address these limitations. CHAUN employs shared feature embeddings and cross-head attention mechanisms to dynamically integrate treatment-specific and control-specific representations, enhancing inter-group correlation modeling. Theoretically, we prove that access to the true propensity scores ensures ITE identifiability even with unobserved confounders. For practical scenarios lacking true propensity scores, RA-IPS adversarially optimizes propensity weights within constrained uncertainty sets to mitigate bias from unobserved variables. Experiments on public datasets (CRITEO-UPLIFT, LAZADA) and a production e-commerce dataset demonstrate CHAUN's superiority over state-of-the-art uplift models, achieving relative improvements of up to 25.6% in QINI scores. RA-IPS further enhances robustness, outperforming standard IPS by 5.4% under unobserved confounding. The results validate the effectiveness of our proposed methods in real-world causal inference tasks.

</details>


### [77] [Kolmogorov Arnold networks (KAN) for aerodynamic prediction: a comparison with MLPs and GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27126)
*Miguel Jaraiz,Fermin Gutierrez,Pablo Yeste,Miguel Sánchez-Domínguez,Eusebio Valero,Gonzalo Rubio,Lucas Lacasa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了KAN（Kolmogorov-Arnold网络）在流体动力学代理建模中的性能，并与MLP和GNN进行了比较。以亚音速和跨音速翼型表面压力分布预测为任务，结果表明KAN虽能以较低模型复杂度实现可接受性能，但训练不稳定、依赖超参数优化，整体性能略逊于MLP，而GNN性能最佳但训练时间最长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于KAN是否在符号回归、通用机器学习、NLP和计算机视觉等领域超越传统MLP存在争议，作者希望评估KAN在流体动力学代理建模这一新兴领域的表现，并与MLP和GNN进行对比，为KAN的实际应用价值提供实证依据。

Method: 选取亚音速和跨音速翼型表面压力分布预测作为空气动力学经典任务，分别训练KAN、MLP和GNN三种模型，在预测整个压力系数以及跨马赫数和攻角插值方面进行系统比较，重点关注模型性能、复杂度、训练速度和稳定性。

Result: 1) KAN在预测压力系数和跨参数插值方面表现良好；2) KAN性能与MLP相当但略逊一筹；3) GNN性能最佳但训练时间最长；4) KAN模型复杂度最低、训练速度最快；5) KAN存在训练不稳定性问题，性能对超参数优化高度敏感。

Conclusion: KAN在流体动力学代理建模中并非优于MLP，其性能与MLP相当但略逊，且存在训练不稳定等缺陷；GNN性能最优但代价是训练耗时更长。因此建议在流体动力学代理建模中，需根据对性能、效率和稳定性的不同需求合理选择模型架构。

Abstract: Kolmogorov Arnold networks (KAN) have recently been introduced as a (deep) neural network architecture whose trainable parameters adapt the activation functions, instead of the coefficients of the affine transformations at the core of traditional architectures such as deep multilayer perceptrons (MLPs). This architecture builds on the Kolmogorov-Arnold theorem, which endows it with universal approximation properties. While the advent of KANs has been received with excitement, there is a current debate about the possible KAN supremacy over deep multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) for classic fields such as symbolic regression, generic-purpose machine learning, natural language processing or computer vision. Here we assess the performance of KANs --and its nuanced comparison against MLPs and graph neural networks (GNNs)-- in the realm of fluid dynamics surrogate modelling. To that aim, we consider the task of predicting the surface pressure distribution over subsonic and transonic airfoils, a canonical task in aerodynamics. Our results show that KAN models show good performance in predicting the whole pressure coefficients and is able to interpolate across Mach numbers and angles of attack, however its performance is comparable --marginally inferior-- to a suitably trained MLP, where best performance is achieved by a GNN at the expense or requiring lengthier training. While the optimal KAN model have typically much lower complexity than MLP and GNN --hence resulting in faster training--, we find that KANs suffer from training instabilities, and their performance is highly dependent on a proper hyperparameter optimisation.

</details>


### [78] [fTNN: a tensor neural network for fractional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27140)
*Qingkui Ma,Hehu Xie,Xiaobo Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出fTNN，一种确定性张量神经网络子空间方法，用于求解有界域上的分数阶拉普拉斯问题（分数阶泊松方程和时间依赖的分数阶对流扩散方程）。该方法采用几何自适应积分拆分、边界奇异性感知的试函数以及时空可分离神经网络，在强边界奇异性和长时间模拟问题上显著优于现有fPINN和蒙特卡洛方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分数阶拉普拉斯问题的数值方法（如fPINN和蒙特卡洛方法）在处理强边界奇异性和长时间模拟时精度不足，需要一种高精度且确定性的求解框架。

Method: 1) 采用几何自适应积分拆分，将分数阶拉普拉斯算子分解为奇异近场、规则内远场和解析外远场三部分；2) 奇异性径向积分用Gauss-Jacobi求积，规则径向积分用Gauss求积，角度变量用确定性角度求积；3) 构造边界奇异性感知的试函数，自动选择主导指数并评估损失函数；4) 设计时空可分离神经网络，将时空残差分解为低维时间积分和空间积分之和，采用交替神经网络子空间优化策略训练。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提框架在测试基准上达到高精度，在强边界奇异性和长时间模拟问题上显著优于现有fPINN和蒙特卡洛基线方法。

Conclusion: fTNN为分数阶拉普拉斯问题提供了一种高精度、确定性的神经网络求解方案，尤其在处理边界奇异性和长时间演化方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: We develop the fTNN, a deterministic tensor neural network subspace method for problems involving the fractional Laplacian on bounded domains, taking the fractional Poisson equation and time-dependent fractional advection-diffusion equation as typical representatives. The work employs a geometry-adapted integration split featuring a spatially dependent near-field radius, which decomposes the fractional Laplacian into three contributions: a singular near field, a regular interior far field, and an analytical exterior far field. Then the singular radial integrals are treated by Gauss-Jacobi quadrature, the regular radial integrals by Gauss quadrature, and the angular variables by deterministic angular quadrature, yielding a fully deterministic integration framework of the fractional Laplacian operator. To accurately resolve low-regularity solutions and the associated loss functional, we construct boundary-singularity-aware trial functions enriched with explicit boundary features, and propose two strategies for automatically selecting the leading exponent and evaluating the loss function from the singularity structure induced by the fractional operator, or jointly by the fractional operator and the source term. For time-dependent fractional PDEs, we design a spatiotemporally separable neural network that factorizes the time-space residual into a sum of low-dimensional temporal and spatial integrals, and we integrate this representation with an alternating neural network subspace optimization strategy for efficient training. Numerical experiments show that the proposed framework attains high accuracy on the tested benchmarks and improves substantially over existing fPINN and Monte Carlo baselines, particularly for problems with strong boundary singularities and long-time simulations.

</details>


### [79] [Stochastic Gradient Optimization with Model-Assisted Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27171)
*Jonne Pohjankukka,Jukka Heikkonen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型辅助抽样的梯度估计框架，将小批量梯度下降与调查抽样理论相结合，利用辅助梯度预测模型构建更高效的梯度估计器，实验表明在71-86%的场景中性能提升，尤其与AdamW等动量优化器结合时，能在约一半训练轮次内实现更好的泛化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决深度学习中小批量梯度估计的方差问题，现有方差缩减技术（如SVRG、SAG）和自适应优化器虽能缓解梯度噪声，但可能引入额外计算开销，亟需更高效的梯度估计方法。

Method: 将数据集视为固定有限总体，利用调查抽样理论解释小批量梯度，引入辅助梯度预测模型构建更高效的梯度估计器，统一采样作为无辅助信息时的特例，该方法可与现有优化器无缝集成。

Result: 在合成数据和6个基准数据集上，71-86%的实验取得性能提升；与AdamW等动量优化器结合时，约一半训练轮次即可达到更好的泛化效果。

Conclusion: 模型辅助抽样框架能有效降低梯度估计方差，提升优化效率，且易于集成到现有优化器中，对中等规模输入空间的数据集效果尤为显著。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of variance in stochastic gradient estimation for machine learning optimization. Deep learning relies on mini-batch methods such as stochastic gradient descent, which approximate full gradients but introduce noise, creating trade-offs between convergence stability, speed, and generalization. Existing methods, including variance reduction techniques (e.g., SVRG and SAG) and adaptive optimizers, aim to mitigate gradient noise but may introduce additional computational overhead. We propose a model-assisted sampling framework that interprets mini-batch gradients through survey sampling theory, treating the dataset as a fixed finite population and gradients as sample-based estimates. Our aim is to bridge machine learning optimization and survey sampling theory by combining their perspectives on sample-based estimation and variance reduction. By incorporating auxiliary gradient-prediction models, we construct more efficient gradient estimators, with uniform sampling arising as a special case when no auxiliary information is used. Our approach integrates easily with existing optimizers, improving efficiency without altering their dynamics. Empirical results on synthetic and six benchmark datasets show performance gains in 71-86% of the experiments, particularly for medium-sized input spaces in our benchmarks. Notably, with momentum-based optimizers such as AdamW, the proposed estimator achieves clearly better generalization in roughly half the training epochs compared to baseline estimator.

</details>


### [80] [RecallRisk-BERT: A Multi-Task Framework for Post-Report Medical Device Recall Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27174)
*Ali Semih Atalay,Sevgi Yigit-Sert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于54,165条FDA医疗设备召回记录，提出RecallRisk-BERT多任务模型，联合预测召回严重等级（I/II/III类）和根因类别（9类），在文本与结构化特征融合学习上取得优异性能，严重等级预测准确率达0.963，根因风险排序与真实模式高度一致（rho=0.983）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多单独处理召回预测或根因分析，缺乏对召回严重等级和根因类别的联合建模，而实际召回分类、严重性评估和根因解读面临挑战，亟需自动化召回分类框架。

Method: 1）收集openFDA 2002-2025年54,165条召回记录；2）对比评估经典机器学习与Boosting模型在严重等级和根因类别预测上的表现；3）提出RecallRisk-BERT多任务模型，融合PubMedBERT文本表示与产品代码、法规编号、医学专业等结构化特征的嵌入表示，同时预测严重等级（三类）和根因类别（九类）。

Result: 单任务严重等级预测中，LightGBM文本-表格配置表现最优：准确率0.963、宏F1 0.856、ROC-AUC 0.974。多任务设置下RecallRisk-BERT显著优于单任务PubMedBERT基线。模型风险排序与根因严重模式高度一致（rho=0.983, p=1.936e-6）。

Conclusion: 文本-表格联合学习可支持可扩展的召回后分类、监管决策辅助以及基于模型的根因风险分析。

Abstract: Medical device recalls are a critical regulatory mechanism for protecting patient safety. The growing volume of FDA recall records presents challenges in post-report recall triage, severity assessment, and root-cause interpretation. Existing studies mostly address recall occurrence prediction or root-cause analysis separately, while joint modeling of recall severity and root-cause categories has received limited attention. We develop an automated recall triage framework using 54,165 FDA medical device recall records from openFDA, covering the period from 2002 to October 2025. We first evaluate classical machine learning and boosting-based models for recall severity and root-cause category prediction. We then develop RecallRisk-BERT, a multi-task model that combines PubMedBERT-based textual representations of recall narratives with embedding-based representations of structured categorical features, including product code, regulation number, and medical specialty. The model simultaneously predicts recall severity (Class I/II/III) and a consolidated root-cause category (9 classes). Performance was evaluated using accuracy, macro-averaged precision, recall, F1-score, and ROC-AUC. In single-task severity prediction, our LightGBM-based text--tabular configuration achieved the strongest performance, with an accuracy of 0.963, macro-F1 of 0.856, and ROC-AUC of 0.974. In the multi-task setting, RecallRisk-BERT substantially outperformed the single-task PubMedBERT baseline. Model-derived risk rankings were strongly consistent with observed root-cause severity patterns (rho = 0.983, p = 1.936e-6). These findings indicate that text--tabular learning can support scalable post-report recall triage, regulatory decision support, and model-based root-cause risk analysis.

</details>


### [81] [Explaining Temporal Graph Neural Networks via Feature-induced Information Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27201)
*Ping Xiong,Thomas Schnake,Klaus-Robert Müller,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于归一化相关度量框架的新颖归因方法，通过分析事件时序图神经网络中所有事件关联变量的完整信息流，解决了现有解释方法仅关注部分信息流而忽略事件诱导变量路径的问题，在流行病追踪、社交动态和政治事件网络数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件时序图神经网络解释方法仅追踪从事件相关嵌入到输出的部分信息流，忽略了通过事件诱导变量的重要路径，而这些变量在捕获长时序依赖中起核心作用。

Method: 基于归一化相关度量框架，提出全信息流归因方法：1) 显式量化来自事件嵌入和通过事件诱导变量的信息流；2) 确保跨层潜变量的可比性；3) 支持事件间高阶交互分析；4) 引入模块化分解程序处理复杂神经架构。

Result: 在流行病追踪、社交动态两个合成数据集以及一个真实政治事件网络数据集上，定性和定量实验均表明该方法一致优于现有解释方法，并生成更可解释的解释。

Conclusion: 通过覆盖事件诱导变量的完整信息流路径，该方法有效提升了事件时序图神经网络的可解释性，为理解长时序依赖提供了更全面的归因分析。

Abstract: Event-based Temporal Graph Neural Networks (ETGNNs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of applications, including social network analysis, epidemic tracing, recommender systems, and political event forecasting. However, their increasing complexity poses significant challenges for explainability. Existing explanation methods focus only on a subset of the information flow within ETGNNs, typically tracing contributions from the event-related embeddings to the output. Consequently, they overlook the important pathways through event-induced variables, which mediate interactions between nodes and thereby play a central role in capturing long-range temporal dependencies. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel attribution method that analyzes the \emph{entire} information flow through all event-associated variables. Our method is built upon the recent Normalized Relevance Measure (NRM) framework, which enables explicit quantification of information flow originating from event embeddings as well as information flow passing through event-induced variables. It also ensures comparability of latent variables across layers, and supports higher-order analysis of interactions between events. To handle the architectural complexity of ETGNNs, we extend the NRM framework with a modular decomposition procedure that facilitates the systematic construction of relevance structure for complex neural architectures. We evaluate our approach on two synthetic datasets for epidemic tracing and social dynamics, as well as a real-world dataset of political event networks. Our qualitative and quantitative experiments show that our method consistently outperforms existing explanation approaches while producing more human-interpretable explanations.

</details>


### [82] [Graph Neural Networks Applications Across Domains: All Insights You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27202)
*Abderaouf Bahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了图神经网络的设计空间、谱域与空域方法的统一原理、Weisfeiler-Leman层次下的表达能力，并在12个应用域中评估了图结构的成本效益，揭示了异质性、尺度、时序动态等跨领域共性挑战，指出排行榜模型与实际部署之间的脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机在于回答一个关键问题：图结构何时值得其计算成本，何时不值得。随着GNN从 niche 方法变为默认模型，需要系统性地梳理其设计空间、表达能力和应用收益，并区分真实提升与弱基线或数据划分带来的伪增益。

Method: 采用综述方法，围绕单一设计空间组织领域，从共享第一性原理推导谱域与空域公式，将表达能力与Weisfeiler-Leman层次关联。对12个应用域分别指定图构造选择和成本，识别主导架构族及其原因，分离报告增益与假象。

Result: 识别出跨领域共性模式：异质性和尺度削弱了几乎所有领域的相同模型；时序图始终比静态图更难处理；排行榜顶尖架构很少被实际部署。将过平滑、过挤压、鲁棒性、分布偏移、公平性和可解释性定位为决定采纳的约束条件。

Conclusion: 图结构并非总是有效，其效益高度依赖领域特性和任务需求。当前GNN在异质性、大规模时序图等场景面临根本性挑战，且学术界与工业界的模型选择存在明显鸿沟。

Abstract: Graph neural networks have moved from a niche representation-learning technique to the default model class wherever data carry relational structure. The interesting question is no longer whether message passing helps on a given dataset, but where graph structure earns its computational cost and where it does not. This survey organises the field around a single design space, derives the spectral and spatial formulations from shared first principles, and connects expressive power to the Weisfeiler-Leman hierarchy with explicit statements of what current architectures can and cannot separate. Against that methodological backbone we examine twelve application domains, among them recommendation and social networks, knowledge graphs and language-model integration, drug discovery and molecular property learning, healthcare and neuroscience, computer vision, traffic and urban computing, power and renewable-energy systems, wireless and sixth-generation networks, fraud and cybersecurity, industrial prognostics, materials science, and climate modelling. For each domain we specify the graph-construction choices and their costs, identify which architecture families dominate and why, and separate reported gains from artefacts of weak baselines or favourable splits. A cross-domain comparison exposes recurring patterns: heterophily and scale undercut the same models almost everywhere, temporal graphs remain harder than their static counterparts, and the architectures that top public leaderboards are seldom the ones that reach deployment. We treat over-smoothing, over-squashing, robustness, distribution shift, fairness, and explainability not as a closing checklist but as the constraints that decide adoption.

</details>


### [83] [The Geometry of Updates: Fisher Alignment at Vocabulary Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27242)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FisherSketch，一种无需训练的源模型选择方法，适用于共享词表的LLM家族。通过将Fisher对齐近似为激活-误差联合空间中的核均值嵌入余弦，该方法仅需单次流式扫描即可计算，存储开销极小（16KB任务签名+192KB流状态），解决了传统表示相似度指标（如CKA）在共享输出头设置下的不可识别性问题，以及经典更新几何度量在大词表规模下的计算不可行问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学序列领域（如SMILES、蛋白质、基因组序列）中，存在共享分词器但预测目标不同的候选语料库，需要高效选择最适合目标任务的源模型。然而，表示相似度指标（如CKA）在共享输出头设置下无法区分模型（模型可有相同表示但头更新正交），而经典更新几何度量（如Fisher信息矩阵）在词表规模下计算成本过高。

Method: FisherSketch方法基于关键数学恒等式：头Fisher对齐恰好等于激活-误差联合空间中核均值嵌入之间的余弦。通过单次流式扫描直接估计该余弦，避免了显式构造Fisher矩阵。存储结构包括16KB的任务签名（m=4096）和192KB的每任务流状态，可与模型哈希值一同存储。

Result: FisherSketch使得K=128,256头Fisher对齐变得实用。在共享参数和内部层验证中，以及在Llama-3.1-8B的verbalizer偏移实验中，FisherSketch在激活相似度无法区分任务时仍能提供有效信息。该方法编码了与迁移相关的更新结构。

Conclusion: FisherSketch为共享词表的LLM家族提供了一种实用、高效的源模型选择方法，其任务签名和边缘分布还可作为诊断工具，用于研究LLM任务相似性是由激活、误差还是它们的耦合驱动的。

Abstract: Training-free source selection for LLM families with shared vocabularies arises in scientific string domains such as SMILES, protein, and genomic sequences, where candidate corpora share a tokenizer but differ in prediction targets. This creates an activation-dark regime: representation-similarity metrics can be uninformative without assumptions about label-conditioned error geometry, while classical update-geometry metrics are computationally prohibitive at vocabulary scale. We show that, in a shared-output head setting, representation metrics (e.g., CKA) are non-identifiable for transfer; models can share identical representations yet have orthogonal head updates. The key identity is that head Fisher alignment is exactly a cosine between kernel mean embeddings in the joint activation-error space, exposing activation, error, and coupling factors rather than requiring a materialized Fisher matrix. FisherSketch estimates this cosine directly in a single streaming pass, making K=128,256 head Fisher alignment practical with a 16 KB task signature (m=4096) and a 192 KB per-task streaming state, small enough to store next to a model hash, but encoding transfer-relevant update structure. Beyond source selection, the same signatures and marginals provide a diagnostic instrument for studying whether LLM task similarity is driven by activations, errors, or their coupling; shared-parameter and internal-layer validations, together with Llama-3.1-8B verbalizer-shift experiments, show that FisherSketch remains informative when activation similarity cannot distinguish tasks.

</details>


### [84] [Effective Covariance Dynamics in Solvable High-Dimensional GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27246)
*Andrew Bond,Zafer Doğan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个可解的高维生成对抗网络（GAN）训练模型，其中线性生成器从具有结构化潜在协方差的数据中学习低维子空间。将无条件信号、对角潜在协方差的设定扩展至类别相关、相关且非零均值的潜在结构。对于二次能量判别器，所有异质性通过概率加权的有效二阶矩进入动力学。证明随机微观训练过程在高维极限下收敛到由该有效协方差控制的确定性常微分方程。在匹配协方差特化下，稳定性分析给出了由学习率和噪声水平决定的模式可解区间：当主导有效特征值跨越下阈值时学习开始，完全恢复需要所有相关有效模式保持在区间内。揭示了信号增强机制：低秩相关可提升弱方向越过可学习阈值，而过强相关会破坏恢复稳定性。数值模拟验证了ODE、相边界和增强机制。在MNIST、FashionMNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验表明，信息性的生成器协方差能改善与数据驱动参考子空间的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解GAN分析仅考虑无条件信号和对角潜在协方差，无法捕捉真实数据中类别依赖、相关且非零均值的潜在结构。本文旨在建立一个更通用的可解高维GAN训练框架，以理解结构化潜在协方差如何影响生成器的子空间学习动力学。

Method: 建立线性生成器+二次能量判别器的GAN模型，允许潜在变量具有类别相关、相关、非零均值的结构。采用高维极限下的随机过程分析方法，推导微观训练过程的宏观ODE极限。对匹配协方差特化进行模态分解稳定性分析，推导学习区间与相边界。

Result: 所有异质性通过概率加权有效二阶矩进入动力学；证明微观过程在高维极限下收敛至确定性ODE；稳定性分析给出模式可解区间（由学习率和噪声水平决定）；发现低秩相关可增强弱信号可学习性，过强相关破坏稳定性；数值和真实数据实验验证了理论。

Conclusion: 本文建立的通用可解GAN框架揭示了结构化潜在协方差对子空间学习的双重作用：低秩相关有助于提升弱信号的可学习性，但过强相关会破坏恢复稳定性。信息性生成器协方差能改善与数据参考子空间的对齐，为设计更有效的GAN训练策略提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study a solvable high-dimensional model of generative adversarial network (GAN) training in which a linear generator learns a low-dimensional subspace from data with structured latent covariance. Prior solvable GAN analyses assume unconditional signals with diagonal latent covariance; we extend the multi-feature discriminator setting to class-dependent, correlated, and non-zero-mean latent structure. For the quadratic energy discriminator, all such heterogeneity enters the dynamics through a probability-weighted effective second moment. We prove that the stochastic microscopic training process converges, in the high-dimensional limit, to deterministic ordinary differential equations governed by this effective covariance. In the matched-covariance specialization, the stability analysis yields a mode-wise solvable interval determined by the learning rates and noise level: learning begins when the leading effective eigenvalue crosses the lower threshold, while full recovery requires all relevant effective modes to remain within the interval. This reveals a signal-boosting mechanism: low-rank correlations can lift weak directions above the learnability threshold, whereas overly strong correlations destabilize recovery. Numerical simulations validate the ODE, phase boundary, and boosting mechanism. Experiments on MNIST, FashionMNIST, and CIFAR-10 further show that informed generator covariance improves alignment with the data-driven reference subspace.

</details>


### [85] [RSPC: A Benchmark for Modeling Stress and Psychiatric Conditions in Digitally Mediated Relationships using Psychiatrist Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27247)
*Parmitha Vangapandu,Sai Ganesh Mokkapati,Sathwik Narkedimilli,MSVPJ Sathvik,Timothy Liu,Simon See,Johannes C. Eichstaedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了关系压力与精神病学语料库（RSPC），包含1,799条由精神科医生标注的Reddit帖子，涵盖情绪障碍（焦虑和抑郁）、关系压力源触发因素及关系阶段。通过微调模型和大型语言模型的评估，发现不同模型在不同任务上存在显著性能差异，Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类上最优（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测上最强（Macro-F1=0.519）。研究揭示了焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间的强关联，为上下文感知的心理健康NLP建模提供了基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP心理健康研究通常将精神健康状况视为孤立现象，忽视了人际背景和关系语境。本文旨在通过引入关系上下文来弥补这一缺陷，从关系压力源和人际动态的角度建模心理健康问题。

Method: 构建了RSPC数据集，包含1,799条来自Reddit异地恋板块的帖子，由专业精神科医生标注诊断类别（焦虑、抑郁）、关系压力源触发因素和关系阶段。评估了7个微调Transformer模型和5个大型语言模型在多标签障碍分类、关系触发检测和时间阶段预测三个任务上的表现。

Result: Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类任务上表现最佳（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测任务上最强（Macro-F1=0.519）。不同模型家族在不同任务上表现出明显差异。同时发现焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间存在强关联。

Conclusion: RSPC为考虑关系语境的NLP任务建立了基准，支持从以个体为中心向上下文感知的心理健康建模转变，能够捕捉社会和时间维度上的心理困扰动态。

Abstract: In NLP, mental health conditions are often modeled as isolated phenomena, without interpersonal context. We use Reddit posts about long-distance relationships to capture both mental health distress and associated relational triggers. We introduce the Relational Stress and Psychiatry Corpus (RSPC) containing 1,799 Reddit posts annotated by psychiatrists for diagnostic categories, including the most prevalent mood disorders (anxiety and depression), relational stressor triggers, and indications of relationship phase. We benchmark seven fine-tuned transformer models and five large language models across multi-label disorder classification, relational trigger detection, and temporal phase prediction tasks. We find clear task-dependent differences between model families, with Claude-3-Haiku achieving the best disorder classification performance (Macro-F1 = 0.538) and GPT-4o obtaining the strongest relational trigger detection performance (Macro-F1 = 0.519), suggesting distinct model capabilities. We further find strong associations between anxiety disorders and chronic relational uncertainty. Overall, RSPC establishes a benchmark for NLP tasks that consider relational context and supports a shift from individual-centric to context-aware mental health modeling that captures the social and temporal dynamics of distress.

</details>


### [86] [BetXplain: An Explanation-Annotated Dataset for Detecting Manipulative Betting Advertisements on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27274)
*MSVPJ Sathvik,Parmitha Vangapadu,Nishit Rane,Sathwik Narkedimilli,Mark Lee,Akrati Saxena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了来自Instagram和Reddit的博彩广告数据集，并进行了人工标注，用于检测具有操纵性和欺骗性的博彩广告，同时分析了这些广告常用的说服策略及其对用户心理健康的潜在影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上博彩应用广告激增，这些广告常使用可能误导用户、鼓励冒险行为并影响心理健康的说服技巧，但缺乏公开可用的标注数据集来支持自动化检测操纵性和欺骗性博彩广告的研究。

Method: 从Instagram和Reddit收集博彩广告，进行人工标注操纵性和欺骗性广告实践，并附有人类提供的推理解释；分析常用说服策略及其对心理健康的潜在影响。

Result: 构建了一个带有人工标注和解释的博彩广告数据集，可用于可解释的操纵性广告检测研究。

Conclusion: 该框架可支持实际应用，如提醒用户注意操纵性博彩广告的浏览器插件，以及帮助监管机构监控在线博彩推广的自动网络爬虫。

Abstract: The promotion of betting applications on social media platforms has increased significantly in recent years. Many of these advertisements use persuasive techniques that may mislead users, encourage risky behavior, and potentially influence users' mental well-being. However, research on the automated detection of manipulative and deceptive betting advertisements remains limited due to the lack of publicly available annotated datasets. In this work, we introduce a new dataset of betting-related advertisements collected from two widely used social media platforms, Instagram and Reddit. The advertisements were manually annotated for manipulative and deceptive advertising practices. In addition to classification labels, the dataset includes human-provided explanations that describe the reasoning behind each annotation, enabling research into explainable approaches to detecting manipulative advertising. Furthermore, we analyze the strategies commonly used in betting advertisements and examine how these persuasive tactics may impact users' mental health. The proposed framework can also enable practical applications such as browser plugins that warn users about manipulative betting advertisements and automated web crawlers that help regulatory authorities monitor and detect such promotions online.

</details>


### [87] [How Good Can Linear Models Be for Time-Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27282)
*Lang Huang,Jinglue Xu,Luke Darlow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Ridge回归实验证明，时间序列预测中通过优化预处理（回溯长度、归一化方式、正则化等）而非扩大模型规模，即可达到甚至超越大型Transformer/MLP/CNN模型的性能，并揭示了三个关键数据规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战当前时间序列预测领域"更大模型即更好性能"的主流假设，探究通过优化预处理环节能否以更低成本接近甚至超越大规模模型的性能。

Method: 以Ridge回归为测试平台（因其闭式解和可解释权重），在8个标准数据集上系统搜索超参数空间，包括：上下文长度（回溯窗口）、局部归一化策略（学习率化尾部比例而非全窗口）、正则化强度、数据增强。分析最优超参数的跨数据集/跨序列分布规律。

Result: (1) 最优回溯长度高度依赖于序列本身，且与预测时长呈非单调关系，幂律指数从+0.46到-0.19不等，挑战了"更长预测需更长历史"的惯例。(2) 在上下文的学习尾部比例上做归一化几乎普遍优于全窗口归一化。(3) 同一数据集内不同序列的最优超参数不一致，跨序列共享程度从完全共享到完全独立不等。优化后的模型在8个基准中6个上超越Transformer/MLP/CNN基线，并在多数数据集-预测时长组合上击败先前线性模型。

Conclusion: 预处理优化（而非模型规模扩张）是提升时间序列预测性能的高效路径；同时最优超参数可作为数据本身的诊断工具，揭示大型模型隐式吸收的数据结构。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting research has been moving steadily toward larger architectures, from specialized transformers to general-purpose foundation models, on the assumption that capacity is what unlocks accuracy. We take the opposite position: most of the gap can be closed at far lower cost by tuning preprocessing rather than scaling models. We use Ridge regression as the testbed, since it has a closed-form solution and interpretable weights, which let the optimal hyperparameters be read off the search directly. We search over context length, local normalization, regularization, and augmentation on eight standard benchmarks and find three patterns. (1) Optimal lookback is strongly series-specific and often non-monotonic in forecast horizon, with fitted power-law exponents ranging from $+0.46$ on ETTm2 to $-0.19$ on Exchange and Traffic, challenging the convention that longer horizons need longer history. (2) Normalizing over a learned trailing fraction of the context, rather than its entirety, is almost universally preferred. (3) Series within the same dataset often disagree on hyperparameters; the optimal degree of cross-series sharing varies from fully shared to fully per-series. The resulting models beat prior linear forecasters on most dataset-horizon entries and exceed Transformer, MLP, and CNN baselines on six of eight benchmarks. The optimized hyperparameters also serve as a diagnostic on the data itself, revealing structures that larger models absorb silently into their learned parameters.

</details>


### [88] [Recovering Governing Equations from Solution Data: Identifiability Bounds for Linear and Nonlinear ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27285)
*Yang Pan,Helmut Bölcskei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对科学机器学习中从观测数据学习控制方程的理论空白，引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，建立了从线性到非线性（Lipschitz/Hölder连续向量场）ODE的可辨识性边界，并推导了度量熵估计和样本复杂度界，量化了可靠恢复控制方程所需的解观测数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献缺乏从多个解观测中唯一且稳定辨识真实ODE的理论条件，且缺乏对这类学习任务样本复杂度的定量分析。

Method: 引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量（捕捉所有允许初始条件下两个方程的最坏情况分离，编码辨识问题的极小化极大结构）；建立从线性到非线性（Lipschitz/Hölder连续向量场）ODE的可辨识性边界；基于该度量推导度量熵估计和样本复杂度界。

Result: 建立了从线性到非线性ODE类的可辨识性边界；推导了度量熵估计和样本复杂度界，定量了可靠恢复控制方程所需的解观测数量。

Conclusion: 解集上的Hausdorff距离是量化ODE可辨识性的有效工具，为科学机器学习中控制方程辨识提供了理论保证和样本复杂度分析。

Abstract: Learning governing equations from observed solution data is a fundamental challenge in scientific machine learning \cite{bruntonDiscoveringGoverningEquations2016,kovachkiNeuralOperatorLearning2023,longPDENetLearningPDEs2018,rudyDatadrivenDiscoveryPartial2017,raonicConvolutionalNeuralOperators2023}, yet the theoretical conditions under which a ground-truth ODE can be uniquely and stably identified from multiple solution observations remain largely undeveloped, and no quantitative analysis of the sample complexity of such learning tasks exists in the literature. To address this gap, we introduce the Hausdorff distance on solution sets as the natural metric for comparing differential equations, since it captures the worst-case separation between two equations over all admissible initial conditions and thus encodes the minimax structure of the identification problem. We establish identifiability bounds for governing ODEs across a wide class of structure equations--ranging from linear ODEs to nonlinear classes with Lipschitz (Hölder)-continuous vector fields--characterizing precisely when two distinct equations can be distinguished from solution data. Using this metric, we derive metric entropy estimates for the relevant ODE classes and analyze sample complexity bounds, quantifying how many solution observations are needed to reliably recover the governing equation.

</details>


### [89] [Designing Reward Signals for Portable Query Generation: A Case Study in Industrial Semantic Job Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27291)
*Ping Liu,Qianqi Shen,Jianqiang Shen,Wenqiong Liu,Rajat Arora,Yunxiang Ren,Chunnan Yao,Dan Xu,Baofen Zheng,Wanjun Jiang,Andrii Soviak,Kevin Kao,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端RLAIF框架用于生成可迁移的求职搜索查询，并通过实验证明：在对抗性奖励环境下，训练成功根本上取决于奖励塑形设计，而非优化器选择；GRPO对虚假奖励信号尤为敏感，而引入规则基底的奖励下限可显著提升质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求职平台依赖低带宽查询接口，难以捕捉候选人档案的高维复杂性，现有方法无法生成既屏蔽个人标识符又保留可泛化资质的便携式搜索查询。

Method: 提出端到端RLAIF框架；通过大规模实验隔离优化机制与结构化奖励工程的影响；引入确定性规则基底的奖励下限纠正逐字复制行为的奖励分配。

Result: 无批评器的优化器性能主要由奖励塑形决定，算法选择影响甚微；GRPO的组相对优势归一化对虚假奖励信号敏感，易被利用；引入奖励下限后，跨族评估法官上质量提升+0.147；训练时奖励模型将性能增益膨胀了2.4倍。

Conclusion: 训练成功根本上依赖于强制奖励塑形纪律，而非选择替代优化器。

Abstract: Job-search platforms rely on low-bandwidth query interfaces that often fail to capture the high-dimensional complexity of candidate profiles. We present an end-to-end RLAIF (Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback) framework to generate \emph{portable} job search queries, terms that abstract away seeker-specific identifiers while preserving generalizable qualifications. This task introduces a highly adversarial reward surface where policy optimization frequently exploits flaws in LLM-as-judge rubrics, resulting in degenerate verbatim-copying behaviors.
  We conducted comprehensive empirical experiments to isolate the impact of optimization mechanics against structured reward engineering. Our results demonstrate that for critic-free optimizers, performance is overwhelmingly dictated by robust reward shaping, rendering the specific choice of algorithm largely immaterial. While critic-free per-rollout baseline methods (RLOO and REINFORCE++) natively resist reward-hacking, the group-relative advantage normalization in GRPO appears uniquely sensitive to spurious reward signals, making it disproportionately susceptible to exploitation. We show that introducing a deterministic, rule-based reward floor to correct for rewards assigned to verbatim copying mitigates this failure mode, resulting in a substantial $+0.147$ quality improvement on a cross-family evaluation judge. Ultimately, we show that the training-time reward model inflates performance gains by $2.4\times$, confirming that the training success is fundamentally dependent on enforcing reward-shaping disciplines rather than selecting alternative optimizers.

</details>


### [90] [A Multi-Fidelity Convolutional Autoencoder-Transfer Learning Framework for Guided-Wave-Based Damage Diagnosis Using Large Simulated and Limited Experimental Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27304)
*Santosh Kapuria,Abhishek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多保真迁移学习框架，结合轻量物理仿真、卷积自编码器深度特征学习和少量实验数据，实现了板状结构中损伤的准确定位与尺寸评估，R²分别超过0.93和0.99。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导波结构健康监测中深度学习模型的实际部署面临两个主要障碍：标记实验数据稀缺，以及生成大规模高保真仿真数据集的计算成本过高。

Method: 提出多保真迁移学习框架，包括：(1) 一维时域谱元模型生成大规模合成数据集用于预训练；(2) 卷积自编码器进行深度特征学习；(3) 前馈神经网络进行损伤定位与尺寸评估；(4) 使用少量标记实验数据通过迁移学习适应真实域。

Result: 损伤定位R²>0.93，损伤尺寸R²>0.99；在未见过的数据上表现出良好的泛化能力；基于CAE的迁移学习框架在定位精度上显著优于基于CNN的对应方法。

Conclusion: 该框架被证明是一种准确、计算高效且实际可行的导波结构健康监测解决方案。

Abstract: Guided wave-based structural health monitoring (GWSHM) with onboard transducers offers significant potential for the early diagnosis of damage in engineering structures. However, the practical deployment of deep learning models is often hindered by the limited availability of labelled experimental data and the high computational cost of generating large-scale high-fidelity simulation datasets. This study presents a multifidelity transfer learning framework that integrates lightweight physics-based simulations, convolutional autoencoder (CAE)-based deep feature learning, a feed-forward neural network, and limited experimental measurements for accurate damage localisation and sizing in plate-like structures instrumented with piezoelectric transducers. A computationally efficient one-dimensional time-domain spectral element model is employed to generate a large synthetic dataset for pretraining, while transfer learning adapts the model to experimental domains using only a small amount of labelled data. The CAE-based transfer learning framework significantly outperforms its CNN-based counterpart in damage localisation accuracy. The model achieves excellent predictive performance with $R^2$ scores exceeding 0.93 for damage localisation and 0.99 for damage sizing. Its generalisation capability is demonstrated on previously unseen data, showing high prediction accuracy for damage scenarios not represented during pretraining or fine-tuning. The results establish the proposed framework as an accurate, computationally efficient, and practically viable solution for real-world GWSHM applications.

</details>


### [91] [Blackwell Approachability and Gradient Equilibrium are Equivalent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27315)
*Brian W. Lee,Nika Haghtalab,Michael I. Jordan,Ryan J. Tibshirani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了梯度均衡（GEQ）与Blackwell可逼近性在算法意义上是等价的，从而将GEQ纳入在线学习的统一框架中，并建立了与遗憾最小化、校准等框架的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GEQ是近年来提出的在线优化框架，虽与遗憾最小化等经典框架有相似之处，但先前研究表明GEQ误差与遗憾值是不可比较的目标，缺乏对GEQ在在线学习全景中精确定位的理解。

Method: 通过构造双向的归约（reduction）证明：任何Blackwell可逼近问题均可通过查询黑箱GEQ预言机求解，且渐近误差率无损失；反之亦然。同时建立了不同GEQ变体（约束/无约束决策集）之间的归约关系。

Result: GEQ与Blackwell可逼近性算法等价，结合已有的可逼近性与遗憾最小化、校准之间的等价性，推出GEQ与这些框架均等价；归约方法高效，可将乐观性、强自适应性等精细保证从遗憾最小化迁移至GEQ。

Conclusion: GEQ并非独立于经典在线学习框架，而是与遗憾最小化、校准等核心框架等价，为统一在线学习理论提供了新的视角；同时给出了GEQ的充要条件。

Abstract: Gradient equilibrium (GEQ) is a recently introduced online optimization framework that generalizes first-order stationarity from offline optimization and abstracts problems like online conformal prediction. While GEQ has curious similarities with known online learning frameworks, namely regret minimization, prior work has shown that GEQ error and regret are incomparable objectives, leaving open a precise understanding of how GEQ fits into the broader online learning landscape. In this work, we show that GEQ is equivalent to Blackwell approachability in the algorithmic sense. That is, a Blackwell approachability problem can always be solved using queries to a black-box GEQ oracle, with no asymptotic loss in the oracle's error rate, and vice versa. Taken together with known equivalences between approachability, regret minimization, and calibration, these results imply that GEQ is equivalent to these frameworks, as well. Our reductions are efficient and can be used to transfer refined guarantees, such as optimism and strong adaptivity, from regret minimization to GEQ. Along the way, we also identify necessary and sufficient conditions for GEQ, and establish reductions between different notions of GEQ with unconstrained and constrained decision sets.

</details>


### [92] [Beyond the Hard Budget: Sparsity Regularizers for More Interpretable Top-k Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27321)
*Nathanaël Jacquier,Maria Vakalopoulou,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种与Top-k稀疏自编码器兼容的稀疏正则化方法（ℓ1惩罚和ℓ1/ℓ2比率惩罚），在保持重建质量的同时提升了特征的单义性，证明硬架构稀疏与软稀疏正则化是互补的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-k SAE虽然避免了传统ℓ1惩罚的缺点，但其固定预算k无法适应输入复杂度变化，且容易过拟合到训练时的k值。现有方法没有结合显式稀疏正则化，因此需要引入既能保持架构优势又能弥补其局限的稀疏正则化技术。

Method: 在Top-k选择前的激活上施加两种正则化：①对未选中的（off-support）单元施加ℓ1惩罚；②尺度不变的ℓ1/ℓ2比率惩罚，将编码集中在更少的有效单元上。两种惩罚仅应用于batch-active单元（即批次内至少被Top-k选中一次的单元）。在两个数据集、三个视觉基础模型和多种k值下进行验证。

Result: 两种正则化器均在不牺牲重建质量的前提下持续提升了特征的单义性。ℓ1/ℓ2惩罚进一步将信息集中在更少的潜在特征上，使重建对推理时的k值选择更加鲁棒，并提升了小预算线性探针的性能。

Conclusion: 硬架构稀疏（Top-k）与软稀疏正则化并非互斥，而是互补关系，二者结合可以更有效地实现单义性分解。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a leading tool for interpreting the representations of vision foundation models, decomposing their polysemantic activations into a larger set of sparse, more monosemantic features. The Top-$k$ SAE, a now-standard variant, enforces sparsity architecturally through its activation function, retaining only the $k$ most active latents per input. Because it was designed precisely to avoid the $\ell_1$ penalty used by earlier SAEs and its known drawbacks, it has not been combined with an explicit sparsity regularizer, despite retaining limitations of its own, such as a budget $k$ that is fixed regardless of input complexity and a tendency to overfit to the training value of $k$. We introduce two sparsity regularizers compatible with the Top-$k$ architecture, both acting on the activations before the Top-$k$ selection: an $\ell_1$ penalty on the unselected (off-support) units, and a scale-invariant $\ell_1/\ell_2$-ratio penalty that concentrates the code onto fewer effective units. Both penalties are applied only to the batch-active units, those selected by the Top-$k$ operator at least once within the batch. Across two datasets, three vision foundation models, and a range of $k$, both regularizers consistently improve monosemanticity at no cost to reconstruction quality. The $\ell_1/\ell_2$ penalty further concentrates information into fewer latents, making reconstruction more robust to the inference-time choice of $k$ and improving small-budget linear probing. Our central finding is that hard architectural sparsity and soft sparsity regularization are complementary rather than mutually exclusive.

</details>


### [93] [Error-Conditioned Neural Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27354)
*Haina Jiang,Liam Wang,Peng-Chen Chen,Min Seop Kwak,Seungryong Kim,Brian Bell,Jeong Joon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出误差条件神经求解器（ENS），将PDE残差场作为网络直接输入而非优化目标，让网络学习迭代校正策略。在四个PDE系列中，ENS在大多数设置下取得最高预测精度，在湍流Kolmogorov流上提升达10倍，且避免了混合方法的高计算成本。其学习策略在分布偏移下具有泛化能力，包括零样本参数变化和跨方程迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经替代模型将PDE求解视为纯统计任务，训练后难以修正自身约束违反和 extrapolate 超出训练分布；混合方法通过梯度下降或Gauss-Newton步针对PDE残差优化，但继承了经典优化器的计算成本和不稳定性，且数值最小化残差在病态系统中可能是重建精度的不可靠代理。

Method: 提出误差条件神经求解器（ENS），不将PDE残差作为优化目标，而是将残差场作为网络的直接输入，使网络能够读取自身误差的空间结构，学习迭代校正预测的更新策略。

Result: 在四个PDE系列中，ENS在大多数设置下取得最高预测精度，在湍流Kolmogorov流上提升达10倍；避免混合方法的高计算成本；学习策略在零样本参数变化和跨方程迁移等分布偏移下具有泛化能力，在病态区域优势最为显著。

Conclusion: 数值最小化PDE残差在病态系统中可能不可靠；将残差作为输入而非目标，让网络学习校正策略，能获得更高精度、更好泛化性和更低计算成本。

Abstract: Neural surrogate models offer fast approximate mappings from PDE parameters to solutions, but they typically treat solving as a purely statistical task: once trained, they struggle to correct their own constraint violations and extrapolate beyond the training distribution. Recent hybrid methods promote physical correctness by targeting the PDE residual via gradient descent or Gauss--Newton steps, but inherit the compute cost and instability of the underlying classical optimizers. We show, theoretically and empirically, that numerically minimizing the PDE residual can be an unreliable proxy for reconstruction accuracy in ill-conditioned systems, explaining why these methods often do not make accurate predictions despite achieving low residuals. We propose error-conditioned Neural Solvers (ENS), built on a different principle: rather than an optimization target, the PDE residual field is passed as a direct input to the network at each iteration, enabling it to read the spatial structure of its own errors and learn an update policy to iteratively correct its predictions. Across four PDE families, ENS attains the highest prediction accuracy in the large majority of settings, with gains reaching $10\times$ on turbulent Kolmogorov flow, while avoiding the expensive compute cost of hybrid methods. ENS's learned correction policy generalizes under distribution shift, including zero-shot parameter changes and cross-equation transfer, where its relative advantage is largest in the ill-conditioned regimes where residual minimization is least reliable. Project website: https://neuralsolver.github.io/.

</details>


### [94] [Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27361)
*Danyal Rehman,Charlie B. Tan,Yoshua Bengio,Avishek Joey Bose,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），通过自回归建模框架替代传统的流模型，克服了归一化流在表达性和计算成本上的局限性，在分子平衡态采样任务上显著超越现有方法，并在大规模可迁移模型Robin上实现零样本能量误差降低60%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统玻尔兹曼生成器依赖归一化流，但离散时间流受严格可逆性约束导致表达性受限，连续时间流则需昂贵的似然计算，限制了其在复杂分子系统上的应用。

Method: 提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），采用自回归建模框架替代流模型，规避了拓扑约束，支持推理时的序贯干预，并利用大型语言模型中的高效架构提升可扩展性。此外，基于ArBG框架训练了1.32亿参数的可迁移模型Robin。

Result: ArBG在所有基准测试上显著优于基于流的模型，尤其在10残基的Chignolin等大型肽系统中表现突出。Robin模型在8残基系统上将零样本能量误差E-W₂降低了60%以上，超越了此前的最优水平。

Conclusion: 自回归建模框架是流模型在玻尔兹曼生成器中的有效替代方案，兼具更好的表达性、可扩展性和推理灵活性，为分子平衡态采样提供了新的研究范式。

Abstract: Efficient sampling of molecular systems at thermodynamic equilibrium is a hallmark challenge in statistical physics. This challenge has driven the development of Boltzmann Generators (BGs), which allow rapid generation of uncorrelated equilibrium samples by combining a generative model with exact likelihoods and an importance sampling correction. However, modern BGs predominantly rely on normalizing flows (NFs), which either suffer from limited expressivity due to strict invertibility constraints (discrete time) or computationally expensive likelihoods (continuous time). In this paper, we propose Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators (ArBG) -- a novel autoregressive modelling framework -- that overcomes these limitations by departing from the flow-based BG paradigm. ArBG circumvents the topological constraints of flows and enables sequential inference-time interventions, while offering enhanced scalability by leveraging architectures effective in Large Language Models. We empirically demonstrate that ArBG leads to significant improvements over flow-based models across all benchmarks, but particularly in larger peptide systems such as the 10-residue Chignolin. Furthermore, we introduce Robin, a 132 million parameter transferable model trained with the ArBG framework which improves over the previous state-of-the-art, reducing the zero-shot energy error, E-W$_2$, on 8-residue systems by over 60$\%$. The code can be found at the following link: https://github.com/danyalrehman/autobg.

</details>


### [95] [Reinforcement Learning without Ground-Truth Solutions can Improve LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27369)
*Yingyu Lin,Qiyue Gao,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Xunpeng Huang,Kun Zhou,Tongtong Liang,Zhewei Yao,Yi-An Ma,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RiVER框架，通过校准的连续奖励函数训练LLM在无标准答案的分数优化任务上，解决了尺度支配和频率支配两大挑战。在AtCoder启发式竞赛任务上训练后，RiVER在ALE-Bench、LiveCodeBench和USACO上均提升了骨干模型性能，且仅使用分数优化任务训练就能迁移到精确求解基准上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法依赖标准答案进行奖励分配，限制了其在无标准答案任务上的应用。本文旨在利用确定性执行反馈作为连续值监督信号，训练LLM完成无标准答案的分数优化任务。

Method: 提出RiVER框架：(1) 采用基于实例间比较的校准奖励塑造，缓解未校准分数导致的尺度支配问题；(2) 强调排名靠前的求解器，同时对其他有效解保留有界反馈，解决频繁采样次优解导致的频率支配问题。在12个AtCoder启发式竞赛任务上训练。

Result: 在ALE-Bench上Qwen3-8B和GLM-Z1-9B-0414分别提升8.9%和9.4%；在LiveCodeBench和USACO上分别平均提升2.4%和3.5%。使用原始执行分数的基线虽提升ALE评分，但无法迁移到精确求解基准。

Conclusion: 分数优化任务结合适当的奖励校准，可在无标准答案的情况下作为通用编码能力的有效训练环境。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for training LLMs typically rely on ground-truth answers to assign rewards, limiting their applicability to tasks where the ground-truth solution is unknown. We introduce a \textbf{R}anking-\textbf{i}nduced \textbf{VER}ifiable framework (RiVER) that trains LLMs on score-based optimization tasks without ground-truth solutions, using deterministic execution feedback as continuous-valued supervision. When applying group-relative RL to such continuous rewards, we identify two key challenges: \emph{scale dominance}, where uncalibrated score magnitudes across test instances distort policy updates, and \emph{frequency dominance}, where repeatedly sampled suboptimal solutions can outweigh rare but stronger candidates. RiVER addresses these challenges with calibrated reward shaping that uses instance-wise comparisons and emphasizes top-ranked solvers while retaining bounded feedback for other valid solutions. We train on 12 AtCoder Heuristic Contest tasks and evaluate on Algorithm Engineering Benchmark (ALE-Bench), LiveCodeBench, and USACO. RiVER advances Qwen3-8B and GLM-Z1-9B-0414 by 8.9\% and 9.4\% in ALE rating rank. More importantly, despite training exclusively on score-based tasks without any ground-truth solutions, RiVER also improves the backbones across exact-solution benchmarks such as LiveCodeBench and USACO by an absolute average improvement of 2.4\% and 3.5\%. By contrast, baselines trained with raw execution scores improve ALE rating but fail to transfer to exact-solution benchmarks. These results suggest that score-based optimization tasks, combined with proper reward calibration, can serve as effective training environments for general coding ability without ground-truth solutions.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Axon: A Synthesizing Superoptimizer for Tensor Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26344)
*Akash Kothari,Shaowei Zhu,Daniel Kroening,Chungha Sung*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出Axon，一个面向AI加速器张量程序的合成超优化器，通过程序合成自动从语义规范生成目标指令，并探索语义等价的程序变体以选择性能最佳的内核，无需手工编写重写规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编写AI加速器高性能内核需要程序员在分块、指令选择、数据布局和算子融合方面具备深厚专业知识，负担沉重，亟需自动化工具。

Method: Axon采用程序合成自动从语义规范生成目标指令；通过传播算子穿过计算图发现代数变换，使用基于无界张量的SMT保证变换语义等价；在硬件描述约束下探索分块配置并融合算子与指令以最小化内存流量。

Result: 自动生成高性能内核，通过语义等价变换和指令级优化实现性能提升，无需手工重写规则。

Conclusion: Axon通过综合超优化方法有效降低了AI加速器编程难度，自动生成高效内核，展示了程序合成在张量程序优化中的潜力。

Abstract: Writing high performance kernels for AI accelerators requires deep expertise in tiling, instruction selection, data layout, and operator fusion placing a significant burden on programmers. In this paper, we focus on tile based AI accelerator programs and present Axon, a synthesizing superoptimizer for tensor programs: it uses program synthesis to automatically generate target instructions from semantics specifications, and explores semantically equivalent program variants to select the best performing kernel empirically. Axon discovers algebraic transformations by propagating operators through computation graphs and uses SMT over unbounded tensors to guarantee that all transformations preserve semantics without requiring hand crafted rewrite rules. It then lowers tensor operations to target ISA instructions, explores tiling configurations constrained by hardware descriptions, and fuses operators and instructions to minimize memory traffic.

</details>


### [97] [A Typestate Approach to Purpose-aware Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26386)
*Joan Montas,Samuel Dodson,Anitha Gollamudi,Matteo Cimini*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出PurPL语言，利用类型状态（typestate）在编译时推理敏感数据的使用目的，确保数据使用合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用需要验证敏感数据是否按预期目的使用，但现有文献在编译时保证方面的研究成果有限。

Method: 基于typestate理论，将敏感数据类型的状态定义为可使用目的的集合，该集合可在运行时动态增减。开发了面向对象语言PurPL，具备能推理目的和数据使用合规性的typestate系统，并实现了类型检查器。

Result: 通过示例展示了PurPL语言概览，给出了形式化类型系统，并在处理敏感数据的多种编程场景上进行了类型检查实验。

Conclusion: PurPL通过typestate在编译时提供了数据使用目的的推理能力，为敏感数据合规性验证提供了新的编译时保证机制。

Abstract: Real-world applications often require verification that sensitive data is being used for their intended purpose. However, existing literature offers limited results regarding compile-time guarantees in this domain. In this paper, we explore the use of typestate to reason about the purpose of data. In typestate, types have a state, which can transition to other states in the style of automata. In our approach, the state of the type of sensitive data is defined as the set of purposes for which the data can be used. This set can grow or shrink at runtime as purposes can be added or removed during execution.
  In this paper, we have developed PurPL, an object-oriented programming language that features a typestate system that is capable of reasoning about purposes and data usage compliance according to them.
  We give an overview of PurPL through examples and present a formal type system. We have also implemented PurPL's type checker, and we report on our experiments with type checking various programming scenarios that handle sensitive data.

</details>


### [98] [Compiler-Driven Approximation Tuning for Hyperdimensional Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26547)
*Xavier Routh,Abdul Rafae Noor,Akash Kothari,Zheyu Li,Mahbod Afarin,Tajana Rosing,Vikram Adve*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出ApproxHDC框架，自动化识别和应用超维计算（HDC）工作负载中的领域特定近似，支持跨CPU、GPU及ReRAM/PCM模拟加速器的可重定向编译，通过高效搜索实现软硬件层面的近似配置优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩尔定律面临物理和经济极限，领域特定方法被用于加速机器学习工作负载。HDC作为一种新兴范式，以硬件效率为首要目标，天然容忍噪声和近似，但需要自动化框架来系统性地探索和利用其近似机会。

Method: 构建ApproxHDC框架，扩展HPVM-HDC编译器基础设施以支持跨多种硬件后端（CPU、GPU、模拟ReRAM和PCM加速器）的可重定向编译。采用高效搜索与分析方法，在指数级庞大的近似空间中识别高影响力的软硬件近似配置。

Result: ApproxHDC能够在多种硬件后端上自动识别并应用领域特定近似，实现显著性能提升的同时保持最小精度损失。

Conclusion: ApproxHDC有效解决了HDC工作负载中近似技术的自动化探索问题，通过软硬件协同优化在异构平台上实现高性能与低精度的平衡。

Abstract: As Moore's law reaches its physical and economic limits, domain-specific approaches are increasingly employed to accelerate machine learning workloads. Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC) represents one such emerging paradigm, offering an alternative to conventional deep learning techniques. Rooted in cognitive models of computation, HDC is designed bottom-up with hardware efficiency as a first-class objective. HDC workloads map naturally to heterogeneous hardware platforms, including CPUs, GPUs, and FPGAs, as well as emerging in-memory computing technologies such as Resistive RAM (ReRAM) and Phase-Change Memory (PCM). HDC algorithms are intrinsically tolerant to noise and approximation, enabling substantial performance gains with minimal accuracy loss. In this work, we introduce ApproxHDC, a framework for automated identification and application of domain-specific approximations in HDC workloads. ApproxHDC extends the HPVM-HDC compiler infrastructure to enable retargetable compilation across diverse hardware backends, including CPUs, GPUs, and simulated ReRAM and PCM-based accelerators. The space of possible approximations is exponentially large; ApproxHDC employs efficient search and analysis to navigate it and identify high-impact configurations spanning both software and hardware levels.

</details>


### [99] [Mostly Automatic Translation of Language Interpreters from C to Safe Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27122)
*Bo Wang,Brandon Paulsen,Joey Dodds,Daniel Kroening,Umang Mathur,Prateek Saxena*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出了Reboot技术，能够将C语言编写的解释器程序（6k-23k行代码）大部分自动翻译为安全的Rust代码，仅需少量人工干预（1-11次），翻译结果通过率62%-92%，并消除了内存漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将C程序翻译为安全Rust面临类型约束、所有权和借用规则等显著差异的挑战；解释器程序常处理不可信输入且存在内存相关漏洞，是这类翻译的重要目标。

Method: 两个核心思想：1）特征分解——按程序特征分解翻译过程，创建一系列完整可测试的里程碑，从最简版本开始逐步恢复特征，每个里程碑经过验证再推进；2）多智能体架构——通过自动验证和反馈协调不可靠的编码智能体，以最少人工干预保持长翻译工作流的正确性。

Result: 翻译了6个解释器（6k-23k行C代码），每个仅需1-11次人工干预；100%通过提供的测试套件，62%-92%通过单独创建的验证测试；消除了C版本中的堆缓冲区溢出和释放后使用等内存漏洞。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明，与仅使用多智能体翻译相比，特征分解将验证测试通过率提升了6%-20%，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Translating C programs to safe Rust is challenging owing to significant differences in typing constraints, ownership, and borrowing rules. Interpreter programs are particularly important targets for such translation, as they often handle untrusted inputs and suffer from memory-related vulnerabilities. We present Reboot, a mostly-automatic technique that translates real-world interpreter programs from C to safe Rust. Using Reboot, we have translated six interpreters ranging from 6k to 23k lines of C code to safe Rust, with each translation requiring only 1 to 11 brief user interventions. All translations pass 100% of the provided test suites, and achieve 62%--92% pass rates on separately created validation tests that were never exposed to the system. A security case study on mujs shows that memory vulnerabilities such as heap buffer overflows and use-after-free present in C are eliminated in the safe Rust translation. Two ideas underpin Reboot. First, feature reduction decomposes the translation by program features, creating a sequence of milestones where each is a complete, testable program; the translation starts from the simplest version and incrementally restores features, with each milestone validated before proceeding. Second, a multi-agent architecture orchestrates inherently unreliable coding agents through automated validation and feedback, keeping long-running translation workflows on track with minimal human involvement. An ablation study confirms that feature reduction improves translation correctness compared to using multi-agent translation alone, with 6%--20% improvements in pass rates on validation test suites.

</details>


### [100] [Welterweight Go: Boxing, Structural Subtyping, and Generics (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27138)
*Raymond Hu,Julien Lange,Bernardo Toninho,Philip Wadler,Robert Griesemer,Keith Randall*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出WG（轻量级Go）核心模型，涵盖Go语言的结构子类型、泛型、类型联合与类型集等关键特性，以及LWG底层语言模型，通过类型导向编译策略实现从WG到LWG的转换，采用运行时类型转换和适配器方法处理结构子类型与泛型的复杂交互，支持分离编译且无需运行时代码生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Go语言在泛型与具有非统一运行时表示的类型之间的结构子类型组合，给形式化语言带来了重大挑战，现有工作排除了底层类型、类型联合、类型集以及泛型方法等关键特性。

Method: 设计WG核心模型捕获Go的关键特性（底层类型、类型联合、类型集、泛型方法），开发LWG底层语言模型表示Go的运行时机制（原始结构体值与携带RTTI的接口值的区分），并给出从WG到LWG的类型导向编译策略。

Result: 提出了一种不同于静态单态化的编译策略，采用运行时类型转换和适配器方法来处理结构子类型、泛型与Go运行时基础设施之间的复杂交互，同时满足分离编译和无运行时代码生成的设计目标。

Conclusion: 该工作形式化了Go语言的核心特性，并验证了所提特性的可实现性，为Go语言的形式化语义和编译器实现提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Go's unique combination of structural subtyping between generics and types with non-uniform runtime representations presents significant challenges for formalising the language. We introduce WG (Welterweight Go), a core model of Go that captures key features excluded by prior work, including underlying types, type unions and type sets, and proposed new features, such as generic methods. We also develop LWG, a lower-level language that models Go's runtime mechanisms, notably the distinction between raw struct values and interface values that carry runtime type information (RTTI). We give a type-directed compilation from WG to LWG that demonstrates how the proposed features can be implemented while observing important design and implementation goals for Go: compatibility with separate compilation, and no runtime code generation. Unlike existing approaches based on static monomorphisation, our compilation strategy uses runtime type conversions and adaptor methods to handle the complex interactions between structural subtyping, generics, and Go's runtime infrastructure.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Unsupervised Memory-Enhanced Video Transformers: Obstacle Detection for Autonomous Agricultural Rover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26151)
*Théo Biardeau,Anne-Sophie Capelle-Laizé,Salwan Alwan,David Helbert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VMTAD（视频记忆变换器异常检测），一种用于农业移动机器人实时障碍检测的全无监督方法。该方法利用Transformer架构和记忆模块，通过处理前帧的编码表示来利用时序上下文，有效应对机器人移动造成的动态场景变化。在'Grillion'农业机器人上的油菜数据集评估中，VMTAD实现了0.973的检测AUC和0.997的分割AUC，轻量级变体推理时间仅14ms，满足安全制动距离要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主农业机器人在精准农业中至关重要，但现有安全传感器（如LiDAR）无法检测植物冠层下方的障碍物；基于摄像头的监督学习方法对训练数据中未出现的障碍物泛化能力差；现有的无监督异常检测方法难以应对移动机器人捕获的动态场景。

Method: VMTAD采用Transformer驱动架构，配备专用记忆模块。该记忆模块通过处理前几帧的编码表示来利用时序上下文，从而应对机器人移动造成的动态场景变化。模型仅使用正常操作图像进行训练，无需数据标签。

Result: 在'Grillion'农业机器人的油菜数据集上，VMTAD达到0.973的检测AUC和0.997的分割AUC；轻量级变体推理时间仅14ms，在准确性和实时性之间取得最佳平衡。

Conclusion: VMTAD是一种有效的全无监督实时障碍检测方法，在动态农业场景中取得了最先进性能，其推理速度满足农业机器人安全制动距离要求，为自主农业机器人的安全导航提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: While autonomous rovers have become indispensable to precision farming, achieving consistent operational safety remains a critical challenge. Conventional safety sensors, such as LiDAR, fail to detect obstacles positioned below the plant canopy, posing a significant risk. While camera-based supervised learning methods can detect common objects, they perform poorly when faced with obstacles that were not present in their training data. Actual unsupervised anomaly detection offers a solution by learning the normal visual patterns of an environment, but often fails for the dynamic scenes captured by a moving rover.\\ This paper introduces Video Memory Transformers for Anomaly Detection (VMTAD), a fully unsupervised method designed for real-time obstacle detection in dynamic agricultural scenes. VMTAD utilizes a transformer-driven architecture augmented with a dedicated memory module. This memory module leverages temporal context by processing encoded representations of preceding frames. This approach enables the system to effectively address the dynamic context caused by the robot's movement. The model is trained using only images that represent normal operation, requiring no data labels.\\ VMTAD was rigorously evaluated on the 'Grillion' agricultural rover. On a challenging rapeseed dataset, VMTAD achieved state-of-the-art performance, reaching a 0.973 detection and 0.997 segmentation Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve. A lightweight variant provides an optimal balance of high accuracy and real-time inference (14 ms), which is critical for safety, as confirmed by our analysis of the rover's total stopping distance.

</details>


### [102] [Racing a Wheeled Quadruped: Active Load Transfer Mitigation via Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26313)
*Marla Eisman,Brian Lam,Samuel Sonnino,Francesco Borrelli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合模型预测控制(MPC)和强化学习(RL)的分层控制框架，用于轮式四足机器人在自主竞速中的主动侧倾控制，通过腿部执行器作为主动悬架来管理横向载荷转移。物理赛道实验表明，该方法将横向载荷转移率(LTR)平均降低44%，最快单圈时间提升8.7%，峰值横向加速度提升21.3%至1.98 m/s²，且高速稳定性优于非侧倾基线控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决轮式四足机器人在高速自主竞速中因横向载荷转移导致的稳定性问题，通过主动侧倾控制来提高高速行驶的稳定性和操控性能。

Method: 提出分层控制框架：上层离线生成最优赛道路线；中层MPC规划器基于车辆动力学自行车模型主动最小化横向载荷转移率(LTR)；底层使用强化学习策略直接控制16个执行器，利用腿部膝关节产生抗侧倾力矩实现入弯侧倾。

Result: 物理赛道实验显示：平均LTR降低44%，最快圈速提升8.7%，峰值横向加速度提升21.3%达到1.98 m/s²，且在非侧倾基线控制器失效的高速区域仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: 主动侧倾控制框架能显著提升轮式四足机器人在自主竞速中的横向稳定性和综合性能，证明了腿部执行器作为主动悬架进行抗侧倾控制的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a hierarchical control framework using model predictive control (MPC) and reinforcement learning (RL) for active roll control to manage lateral load transfer during autonomous racing of a wheeled quadruped. The framework integrates offline time-optimal raceline generation, an online MPC planner that actively minimizes the lateral Load Transfer Ratio (LTR), and a low-level, whole-body RL policy deployed directly onto the robot's 16 actuators. The MPC is based on a vehicle dynamics bicycle model of the Unitree Go2-W platform. The robot's leg actuators act as active suspension where knee joints generate anti-roll torque to bank into turns. Physical track experiments demonstrate that active roll control reduces mean LTR by up to 44%, improves the fastest lap time by 8.7%, and boosts peak lateral acceleration capability by 21.3% to 1.98 $m/s^2$, maintaining robust high-speed stability beyond the range of a non-tilting baseline controller. Supplementary code and video can be found at https://github.com/meisman-ucb/go2w-roll-control-mpc

</details>


### [103] [Reinforcement Learning Enables Autonomous Microrobot Navigation and Intervention in Simulated Blood Capillaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26154)
*Jannik Drotleff,Samuel Tovey,Paul Hohenberger,Christoph Lohrmann,Julian Hoßbach,Konstantin Nikolaou,Christian Holm*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于物理的血毛细血管网络仿真环境，结合真实流体动力学、红细胞动力学和分支几何结构，利用深度强化学习训练微型机器人通过趋化性进行导航。研究揭示了机器人尺寸与速度的物理极限，发现智能体自主学习出多种通用导航策略，并能实现靶向阻塞与疏通毛细血管血流，恢复健康水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往强化学习研究局限于理想化几何环境，忽略了体内存在的复杂流体流场、受限分支结构和密集细胞障碍，训练出的控制策略难以适用于真实生物血管环境。

Method: 构建了物理真实的毛细血管网络仿真环境，包含真实的流体动力学流场、显式的红细胞动力学模型和解剖学来源的分支几何结构，利用深度强化学习训练智能体通过趋化信号进行自主导航。

Result: 系统绘制了机器人尺寸与游动速度的导航物理极限图谱，发现存在布朗运动和流场超过推进力的"禁止区域"；智能体自主学习出多种通用策略类型（run-and-rotate和search-and-sit）；智能体无需重新训练即可实现靶向阻塞与疏通毛细血管血流，恢复至健康基线水平。

Conclusion: 强化学习在复杂生物环境中开发自主微型机器人干预策略具有可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous microrobots navigating biological vasculature could enable targeted drug delivery and thrombolysis, yet training control policies for realistic environments remains an open challenge. Prior reinforcement learning (RL) studies of microrobotic navigation have been limited to idealized geometries that omit complex hydrodynamic flow fields, confined branching structures, and dense cellular obstacles found in vivo. Here, we develop a physically grounded simulation of a blood capillary network, incorporating realistic hydrodynamic flow fields, explicit red blood cell dynamics, and anatomically derived branching geometry, and train deep RL agents to navigate it via chemotaxis. We systematically map the physical limits of navigation across robot size and swimming speed, revealing a forbidden regime where Brownian motion and flow overcome propulsion. Successful agents independently discover multiple universal strategy types, including run-and-rotate and energy-efficient search-and-sit policies, regardless of robot parameters. Without retraining, these agents perform targeted blocking and unblocking of capillary flow, restoring throughput to healthy baseline levels. These results establish RL as a viable framework for developing autonomous microrobotic intervention strategies in complex biological environments.

</details>


### [104] [RMTL: Reinforced Micro-task Learning for Long-Horizon Manipulation with VLM Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26175)
*Anıl Can Ateş,Orhan Kahraman,Cihan Topal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出强化微任务学习（RMTL），通过将机器人操作任务分解为多个语言描述的微任务，并使用多视角VLM奖励和反向课程学习，解决了单提示VLM奖励在长时域操作任务中奖励信号平坦、难以学习的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单提示VLM奖励在长时域操作任务中奖励信号近乎平坦，智能体难以检测早期进展，导致学习困难。

Method: 将任务分解为语言描述的微任务，智能体切换执行；多视角VLM奖励基于当前微任务提示计算；反向课程学习逐步增加初始条件难度；先训练基于距离规则的PPO工人，再替换为学习的分层管理者。

Result: 在Fetch操作环境中，RMTL提供比单提示VLM奖励更具信息量的奖励信号，实现更快的学习速度。

Conclusion: 将VLM奖励分解为微任务特定的语言提示可以显著提高语言引导强化学习在机器人操作中的可扩展性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for robotic manipulation often requires manually designing a dense reward function, which is difficult to tune and often fragile, or learning a reward from human demonstrations or preferences, which can be expensive. A recent line of work uses pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) as zero-shot reward models, replacing these costs with a single text prompt. However, we argue that a single global prompt is too coarse for long-horizon manipulation tasks with randomized initial conditions. The single-prompt VLM reward is near-flat for much of the trajectory, making early progress hard for the agent to detect. We propose Reinforced Micro-Task Learning (RMTL), an approach that decomposes a manipulation task into a small set of language-described micro-tasks and trains the agent to switch between them. At each step, the agent receives a multi-view VLM reward computed using the prompt of the currently active micro-task and averaged across multiple camera views to reduce the effect of view-specific occlusions. A reverse curriculum gradually exposes the agent to harder initial conditions, while a PPO worker is first trained with a fixed distance-based rule that selects the active micro-task. We then replace this rule with a learned hierarchical manager, turning rule-based phase selection into a fully learned hierarchical policy. We instantiate RMTL on the Fetch manipulation environment using three short stage-specific prompts and without additional prompt tuning. Experiments show that RMTL provides more informative reward signals than single-prompt VLM rewards, enabling faster learning. These results suggest that decomposing VLM rewards into micro-task-specific language prompts can substantially improve the scalability of language-guided reinforcement learning for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [105] [LiMoDE: Rethinking Lifelong Robot Manipulation from a Mixture-of-Dynamic-Experts Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26183)
*Zhihao Gu,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LiMoDE，一个面向终身机器人操作的两阶段学习框架：先在多任务预训练阶段通过动态MoE结构学习先验知识，后在任务适应阶段通过终身MoE适应机制组合终身专家与冻结专家实现知识迁移，在仿真和真实任务中取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在单任务适应中通过参数高效微调缓解灾难性遗忘，但未能提取可复用技能并有效建模技能间的交互；近期工作尝试通过学习提示解决这些问题，但仍存在不足。

Method: 提出两阶段学习方案：(1) 多任务预训练阶段构建动态MoE结构，根据运动信息激活不同数量的异构专家处理不同的短期操作；(2) 任务适应阶段设计终身MoE适应机制，学习终身专家并与冻结专家动态组合，促进新任务的知识迁移。

Result: 在仿真终身学习基准和真实世界任务上进行评估，通过引入适量的额外可训练参数和推理开销，实现了优越的性能和强大的终身适应能力。
