### [140] [ForesightSafety-VLA: A Unified Diagnostic Safety Benchmark for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27079)
*Mingyang Lyu,Yinqian Sun,Yiyang Jia,Sicheng Shen,Moquan Sha,Huangrui Li,Feifei Zhao,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ForesightSafety-VLA基准，首次将安全性作为VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型的主要评估目标，定义了13类安全分类法（涵盖物理交互Safe-Core、指令安全Safe-Lang、感知安全Safe-Vis），通过场景结构、语言指令、视觉观测三个受控维度诊断失败来源，并引入过程级安全指标（累积安全成本CC、风险暴露时间RET）及四象限安全分解。基于RoboTwin平台在5个具身载体上构建66个安全增强场景，实验表明最强策略也存在不可忽视的安全成本，且结构和视觉变化比语言变化导致更严重的安全退化，揭示了具身安全与感知、 grounding和控制能力紧密耦合，无法简化为事后安全过滤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型在通用任务能力上不断进步，但其具身安全限制尚未被充分理解，缺乏以安全为首要评估目标的诊断性基准。

Method: 定义13类安全分类法（Safe-Core物理交互安全、Safe-Lang指令安全、Safe-Vis感知安全），在场景结构、语言命令、视觉观测三个受控维度下评估策略，使用累积安全成本（CC）和风险暴露时间（RET）作为过程级风险指标，并采用四象限分解（安全/不安全的成功与失败）进行细粒度分析。在RoboTwin平台上构建66个安全增强场景，覆盖5种具身载体。

Result: 即使最强的策略也存在不可忽视的安全成本和名义上的不安全成功；结构和视觉变化导致的安全退化显著强于语言变化。

Conclusion: 具身安全与感知、grounding和控制能力紧密耦合，不能仅通过事后安全过滤来保证，需要在模型设计和评估中作为核心考量。

Abstract: In embodied intelligence, safety is a prerequisite for reliable robot deployment in the physical world. Current vision-language-action (VLA) models continue to advance toward general-purpose task capability, yet their embodied safety limits remain poorly understood. To address this gap, we introduce ForesightSafety-VLA, a diagnostic benchmark that makes safety the primary evaluation target for VLA systems. We define a 13-category safety taxonomy covering physical interaction safety (Safe-Core), instruction-side safety (Safe-Lang), and perception-side safety (Safe-Vis), and evaluate policies under three controlled dimensions of variation -- scene structure, language command, and visual observation -- so that failure sources can be diagnosed rather than hidden in a single aggregate score. Beyond binary task success, ForesightSafety-VLA measures process-level risk through cumulative safety cost (CC) and risk exposure time (RET), together with a four-quadrant decomposition of safe/unsafe success and failure. We instantiate 66 safety-augmented base scenarios in RoboTwin across 5 embodiments and report results on representative VLA baselines. Across the evaluated baselines, even the strongest policy incurs non-trivial safety cost and unsafe nominal success, while structure and visual variation induce substantially stronger safety degradation than ordinary language variation. These results suggest that embodied safety is tightly coupled to perception, grounding, and control competence rather than being reducible to post-hoc safety filtering alone.

</details>


### [141] [Proposal-Conditioned Latent Diffusion for Closed-Loop Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27123)
*Shubham Vaijanath Phoolari,Aleyna Kara,Christoph Lauer,Steven Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于扩散模型的闭环交通场景生成框架，通过实例中心场景上下文、多模态提议先验、紧凑动作潜空间和提议初始化，在保持高真实感的同时提升采样效率并降低推理时间，支持测试时引导以实现安全关键行为的可控生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的交通场景生成方法虽真实感强，但计算成本高，难以部署在自动驾驶车辆规划和仿真的时间受限重规划循环中。

Method: 采用实例中心场景上下文编码和多模态提议先验进行条件生成；引入紧凑动作潜空间表示降低维度；利用提议初始化加速采样过程而无需重训练；可选测试时引导用于塑造安全关键行为。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上，该方法在真实感、安全性和可控性之间取得了有利平衡，测试时引导能够实现多目标间的系统化权衡。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了扩散模型在闭环交通仿真中的计算效率瓶颈，兼顾了生成质量与推理速度，适合部署于时间敏感的自动驾驶规划循环中。

Abstract: Closed-loop traffic simulation remains challenging because it must generate interactive multi-agent behaviors that are scene-consistent and controllable throughout rollout. Prior diffusion-based approaches achieve strong realism, but their computational cost can hinder deployment in time-constrained replanning loops for autonomous vehicle planning and simulation. We present a diffusion-based scenario generation framework conditioned on instance-centric scene context and multimodal proposal priors, with optional test-time guidance for shaping safety-critical behaviors. A compact action-latent representation and proposal-based initialization improve sampling efficiency and reduce per-step runtime without retraining. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset demonstrate a favorable balance among realism, safety, and controllability across diverse interactive scenarios, while showing that test-time guidance enables systematic trade-offs among competing objectives.

</details>


### [142] [PAMAE: Phase-Aware-MoE Action Experts Towards Reliable Flow-Matching Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27144)
*Jiayu Yang,Tao Yang,Xiang Chang,Fei Chao,Changjing Shang,Qiang Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种即插即用的相位感知混合专家动作模块（PAMAE），通过稀疏专家混合和相位感知路由替代原有流匹配动作专家，在多阶段操作任务中提升成功率达9.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配VLA策略使用单一共享动作专家，无法捕捉多阶段操作中不同执行阶段的特定控制模式，导致动作生成可靠性不足。

Method: 1) 用稀疏专家混合替换原始流匹配动作专家；2) 引入相位感知路由器，利用执行阶段线索分配动作生成；3) 轻量级相位预测头和路由对齐目标；4) 两阶段训练方案：先标准流匹配损失预热，再在辅助监督下优化相位一致路由。

Result: 在多阶段操作仿真任务上，相较强VLA基线，任务成功率提升高达9.2%。消融实验表明相位监督路由和分阶段优化对性能增益至关重要。

Conclusion: 相位一致性的专家分配是提高流匹配VLA策略可靠性和动作质量的有效机制。

Abstract: Reliable action generation for multi-stage robotic manipulation remains challenging for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. While existing flow-matching VLA policies offer strong multimodal grounding and generalization, they typically employ a single shared action expert, limiting their ability to capture phase-specific control patterns across distinct execution stages. We propose a plug-and-play Phase-Aware Mixture-of-Experts Action Module (PAMAE), as a step towards more reliable phase-consistent action generation. PAMAE replaces the original flow-matching action expert with a sparse expert mixture while preserving the pretrained VLA backbone. PAMAE introduces a phase-aware router that leverages execution-phase cues to allocate action generation across experts, supported by a lightweight phase prediction head and a routing alignment objective. To stabilize specialization, we adopt a two-stage training scheme that first warms up the expert module under the standard flow-matching loss and then optimizes phase-consistent routing under auxiliary supervision. On multi-stage manipulation simulation tasks, PAMAE improves task success by up to \textbf{9.2\%} over strong VLA baselines. Further ablations show that both phase-supervised routing and staged optimization are essential for the observed gains. Our results highlight phase-consistent expert allocation as an effective mechanism for improving the reliability and action quality of flow-matching VLA policies.

</details>


### [143] [PhysReflect-VLA: Physical Feasibility and Self-Reflective Regulation for Reliable Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27146)
*Jiayu Yang,Tao Yang,Weijun Li,Xiang Chang,Fei Chao,Changjing Shang,Qiang Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysReflect-VLA是一个即插即用的执行时可靠性框架，通过物理可行性评估和结构化自我反思增强VLA策略，在长时域机器人操作任务中平均提升5.4%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域机器人操作对物理不可行转换、接触诱导干扰以及执行中缺乏有效自我修正高度敏感，现有VLA模型以前馈方式生成动作，缺乏物理可行性检查和在线错误诊断能力。

Method: 提出PhysReflect-VLA框架，包含三个核心模块：Feasibility Operator评估动作是否导致动力学一致的状态转换；Action Explanation Operator验证转换一致性；LLM-based Reflection Module分析状态差异并生成修正指导。采用两阶段训练流程稳定可行性建模并将反思集成到控制环路中。

Result: 在多阶段、接触丰富的真实世界操作任务中，相比代表性VLA基线平均提升5.4%的成功率。消融实验表明可行性检查和基于反思的修正均有助于提升执行鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 嵌入物理一致性检查和在线自我反思对于可靠的长时域机器人操作至关重要。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation is highly sensitive to physically infeasible transitions, contact-induced disturbances, and the lack of effective self-correction during execution. Although Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide strong task grounding through multimodal learning, they typically generate actions in a feed-forward manner without explicitly checking physical feasibility or diagnosing execution errors online. We present PhysReflect-VLA, a plug-and-play execution-time reliability framework that augments VLA policies with physical feasibility evaluation and structured self-reflection in a closed-loop control pipeline. A Feasibility Operator evaluates whether candidate actions induce dynamically consistent state transitions; an Action Explanation Operator verifies transition coherence; and an LLM-based Reflection Module analyzes state discrepancies to generate corrective guidance for subsequent actions. A two-stage training procedure stabilizes feasibility modeling and integrates reflection into the control loop. Experiments on multi-stage, contact-rich real-world manipulation tasks show consistent improvements in stage-wise stability and overall task success compared with representative VLA baselines with an average gain of 5.4\%. Ablation results further indicate that feasibility checking and reflection-based correction both contribute to improved execution robustness. These results highlight the importance of embedding physical consistency checks and online self-reflection for reliable long-horizon robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [144] [Learning to Fold: prizewinning solution at LeHome Challenge 2026 (1st place online, 2nd offline)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27163)
*Ilia Larchenko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于强化学习循环改进的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略系统，用于双手机器人衣物折叠任务。该系统在ICRA 2026 LeHome Challenge中获仿真轮第一、实物轮第二。核心创新包括：VLA策略同时作为价值函数预测成功率和进度等未来量，结合AWR+RECAP的流匹配VLA、异步分布式训练管线、Thompson采样超参数优化、以及包含相机对齐工具、数据增强和DAgger式数据采集的sim-to-real方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决双手机器人衣物折叠这一复杂操作任务，通过将VLA策略与强化学习循环结合，提升策略在仿真和现实环境中的表现。

Method: 1) VLA策略同时作为价值函数，预测动作、成功率、进度和任务相关未来量，用于优势估计、实时故障检测和候选选择；2) 结合AWR（优势加权回归）与RECAP的流匹配VLA训练方法；3) 通过HuggingFace Hub实现异步分布式训练/数据收集管线；4) 使用Thompson采样进行推理时超参数优化；5) sim-to-real方案包括相机对齐工具、大量数据增强和DAgger式人工介入数据采集。

Result: 在ICRA 2026 LeHome Challenge中，仿真轮排名第1（62支队伍），实物轮排名第2。

Conclusion: 通过将RL思想与工程优化结合（AWR+RECAP、分布式训练、Thompson采样、sim-to-real配方），所提出的系统在双手机器人衣物折叠任务上取得了竞赛级领先性能，且各组件可单独或组合使用。

Abstract: I describe my solution to the LeHome Challenge 2026, an ICRA 2026 competition on bimanual garment folding. The system placed 1st of 62 teams in the online (simulation) round and 2nd in the real-world final. It improves a vision-language-action (VLA) policy with a reinforcement-learning loop. The policy is its own value function: the same network that predicts actions also predicts success, progress, and a few task-relevant future quantities, and those predictions drive advantage estimation, live failure detection, and candidate selection. The work mostly recombines existing RL ideas with engineering and optimization contributions that can be used together as one recipe or individually: AWR + RECAP combined for flow-matching VLA; an asynchronous distributed training / rollout pipeline through HuggingFace Hub; inference-time hyperparameters optimization via Thompson sampling; a sim-to-real recipe with camera-alignment tooling, heavy augmentation and DAgger-like HIL data collection.

</details>


### [145] [HumanoidUMI: Bridging Robot-Free Demonstrations and Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27239)
*Hongwu Wang,Chenhao Yu,Youhao Hu,Jiachen Zhang,Yuanyuan Li,Shaqi Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HumanoidUMI，一种轻量级、免遥操作的机器人化全身数据采集框架，用于人形机器人技能学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人数据采集依赖遥操作，受限于硬件可及性、操作者专业性和效率低下，亟需一种便携且机器人无关的全身数据采集方法。

Method: 利用轻量级VR设备和UMI启发的夹爪，采集稀疏人体关键点轨迹、腕部视角观测和夹爪动作；训练高层策略预测未来关键点，再映射为机器人原生全身参考，由全身控制器执行。

Result: 在五个真实场景中验证了该框架的有效性，证明了所采集数据可用于可迁移的人形机器人全身技能学习。

Conclusion: HumanoidUMI成功实现了无需机器人硬件的便携式全身行为数据采集，为人形机器人技能学习提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: High-quality demonstration data are essential for humanoid robot skill learning, especially for whole-body behaviors that require coordinated perception, locomotion, and manipulation. Existing data-collection methods largely rely on robot teleoperation, which is constrained by hardware accessibility, operator expertise, and limited efficiency. Inspired by the Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI), we propose HumanoidUMI, a portable and robot-free framework for humanoid whole-body data collection. HumanoidUMI uses lightweight VR devices and UMI-inspired grippers to collect sparse human keypoint trajectories, wrist-view observations, and gripper actions. These demonstrations train a high-level policy to predict future keypoints, which are retargeted to robot-native whole-body references and executed by a whole-body controller. Experiments in five real-world scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework and validate the collected demonstrations for transferable humanoid whole-body skill learning.

</details>


### [146] [Advancing Omnimodal Embodied Agents from Isolated Skills to Everyday Physical Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27251)
*Junhao Shi,Zezheng Huai,Siyin Wang,Jia Chen,Yubang Wang,Zhaoye Fei,Hechang Chen,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniAct是一个分层异步框架，通过统一语义规划器、自适应分层记忆和异步视觉抢占引擎，实现机器人在非结构化环境中的持久自主性，在40项真实世界长期任务中取得一致改进，token消耗保持亚线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统将机器人的操作、导航与API/IoT等控制视为分离问题：VLM规划器缺乏统一的网络-物理动作空间，智能体框架的上下文无界增长导致时间连贯性退化，VLA策略开环执行无法检测自身故障。需要一种统一框架实现持久自主性。

Method: 提出OmniAct框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)多模态语义规划器，在统一动作空间中进行技能路由；(2)自适应分层记忆，通过事件边界驱动的压缩实现亚线性上下文增长；(3)异步视觉抢占引擎，在物理执行期间闭合语义回路。

Result: 在两个机器人平台上协调4个IoT设备，完成40项真实世界长期任务。在所有复杂度级别上实现端到端成功率的一致提升，累计超过10万交互token下保持接近平坦的token消耗，并将中规模开源模型提升至专有模型水平。

Conclusion: 持久自主性不需要 monolithic 模型，而是需要具有明确规划、记忆和验证分离的分层异步架构。

Abstract: Building persistent embodied agents in unstructured environments demands unified orchestration of heterogeneous tools spanning both cyber (APIs, IoT) and physical (manipulation, navigation) domains, coupled with autonomous recovery from physical failures that inevitably arise over extended operation. Existing systems treat these as separate problems: VLM-based planners lack a unified cyber-physical action space, agent frameworks accumulate unbounded context that degrades temporal coherence, and VLA policies execute open-loop without detecting their own failures. We argue that persistent autonomy requires not a monolithic model but a hierarchical asynchronous architecture with explicit separation of planning, memory, and verification. To this end, we present OmniAct, a framework integrating a multimodal semantic planner for skill routing across unified action spaces, an adaptive hierarchical memory with event-boundary-driven compression for sub-linear context growth, and an asynchronous visual preemption engine that closes the semantic loop during physical execution. Across 40 real-world long-horizon tasks on two robotic platforms coordinating four IoT devices, OmniAct achieves consistent improvements in end-to-end success across all complexity levels, maintains near-flat token consumption over under 100k+ accumulated interaction tokens, and elevates mid-scale open-weight models to proprietary-level performance.

</details>


### [147] [E-TTS: A New Embodied Test-Time Scaling Framework for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27268)
*Wen Ye,Peiyan Li,Tingyu Yuan,Yuan Xu,Xiangnan Wu,Chaoyang Zhao,Jing Liu,Nianfeng Liu,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出E-TTS框架，通过历史感知的迭代精炼和视觉-语言验证器，统一了具身任务中的推理与动作缩放，在多种基准上无需额外训练即可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身任务中测试时缩放面临两大挑战：(1)推理能有效提升策略性能但其缩放机制研究不足；(2)具身任务本质上是长程序列的，仅依赖当前观测进行动作缩放缺乏历史信息利用。

Method: 提出E-TTS模块化框架：采用推理-动作成对联合采样与评分；使用历史缓冲区存储上下文供验证器评估候选；引入反馈生成形成闭环迭代精炼机制；各组件可独立组合灵活配置。

Result: 在4个基准、6个环境、3种具身类型、4种基础VLA模型上实验，无需额外专家数据或重训练，模拟环境性能提升高达33.14%，真实场景提升26.62%。

Conclusion: E-TTS有效统一了推理与动作缩放，历史感知的闭环迭代机制提升了推理效率和环境适应性，是一种即插即用的通用框架。

Abstract: Recently, a few works have made early attempts to study test-time scaling for embodied tasks. However, two major challenges remain unsolved: (1) reasoning can effectively improve the performance of the policy, but its scaling mechanism has seldom been studied; (2) historical information is essential, as embodied tasks are inherently long-horizon and sequential, making sole reliance on current observations for action scaling inadequate due to the lack of historical context utilization. To address these challenges, we introduce E-TTS, a modular and plug-and-play Embodied Test-Time Scaling framework that unifies reasoning and action scaling for robotic manipulation via history-aware iterative refinement with vision-language verifiers. To support joint reasoning-action scaling, E-TTS performs reasoning-action joint sampling and scoring in a pairwise manner. To better utilize historical information, E-TTS uses a history buffer to store historical context, which is then used by reasoning and action verifiers to evaluate the sampled candidates. Unlike conventional open-loop TTS methods, E-TTS introduces feedback generation into the sampling process to form a closed-loop iterative refinement mechanism, enhancing both inference efficiency and environmental adaptability. Each component functions as an independent and composable module, allowing flexible and adaptive configuration depending on task requirements. To evaluate the advantages of our framework, we conduct experiments across 4 different benchmarks, 6 environments, 3 embodiments, and 4 base vision-language-action models. The experimental results demonstrate that, without requiring additional expert data collection or retraining, E-TTS consistently improves performance, achieving up to a 33.14% increase in simulation and 26.62% in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [148] [BOWConnect: Parallel Bayesian Optimization over Windows with Learned Local Cost Maps for Sample-Efficient Kinodynamic Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27292)
*Sourav Raxit,Abdullah Al Redwan Newaz,Jose Fuentes,Leonardo Bobadilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BOWConnect，一种双向并行运动学动力学运动规划器，通过基于贝叶斯优化的学习型引导函数、双向并行树搜索和空间哈希机制，解决了现有采样方法在高维空间、动态约束和狭窄通道中的三大局限性，在10个基准测试中实现100%成功率，并在真实地面车辆和四旋翼飞行器上验证了实时无碰撞规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于采样的运动规划方法存在三个根本性局限：高维状态空间中的样本效率低下、动态约束下的成本启发式不可靠、以及狭窄通道环境中性能差，亟需一种能够克服这些缺陷的规划方法。

Method: 将贝叶斯优化窗口（BOW）作为基于学习的引导函数集成到并行树搜索框架中，每个工作线程学习局部成本地图和约束以引导采样；采用双向架构同时在起点和终点区域生长前向和后向树；通过空间哈希机制加速连接查询，并利用边界值问题求解器生成运动学一致的桥梁轨迹。

Result: 在10个基准测试环境中实现100%成功率，在复杂场景（包括狭窄通道和非凸空间）中达到最快或接近最快的规划时间；在真实地面车辆和四旋翼飞行器上实现实时无碰撞规划。

Conclusion: BOWConnect通过将学习机制融入并行双向树搜索框架，有效克服了传统采样方法的三大局限性，在仿真和真实环境中均展现出卓越的规划性能和可靠性。

Abstract: This paper presents BOWConnect, a bidirectional parallel kinodynamic motion planner that addresses three fundamental limitations of existing sampling-based methods: sample inefficiency in high-dimensional state spaces, unreliable cost heuristics under dynamic constraints, and poor performance in narrow passage environments. Unlike classical planners that rely on random control sampling and geometric distance heuristics, BOWConnect integrates Bayesian Optimization over Windows (BOW) as a learning-based steering function within a parallel tree-based exploration framework, enabling each worker to learn local cost maps and constraints to guide sampling toward dynamically feasible and collision-free controls. A bidirectional architecture simultaneously grows forward and backward trees from the start and goal regions in parallel threads, with a spatial hashing mechanism enabling fast connection queries and a boundary value problem solver generating kinodynamically consistent bridge trajectories. Extensive evaluations across ten benchmark environments demonstrate that BOWConnect achieves 100\% success while delivering the fastest or near-fastest planning time in complex scenarios, including narrow passages and non-convex spaces where state-of-the-art planners fail or degrade substantially. Real-world deployment on a ground vehicle and a quadrotor confirms real-time planning with no collisions. Videos of real-world and simulated experiments, high-resolution versions of the figures, and the open-source code are available at https://bow-connect.github.io/.

</details>


### [149] [LA4VLA: Learning to Act without Seeing via Language-Action Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27295)
*Tao Lin,Yuxin Du,Yiran Mao,Zewei Ye,Yilei Zhong,Bing Cheng,Yiming Wang,Jiting Liu,Yang Tian,Junchi Yan,Feiran Wu,Zenan Meng,Hu Wei,Yuqian Fu,Gen Li,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型中视觉监督主导语言监督导致策略过度依赖视觉线索的问题，提出LA4VLA框架，通过无视觉的语言-动作预训练获取语言条件化动作先验，结合LA4-33K数据集和多种预训练范式，显著提升VLA策略的鲁棒性和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在预训练中，密集的视觉-动作监督会压倒稀疏的语言-动作信号，导致策略依赖视觉捷径而非学习语言如何条件化动作执行，对视觉变化敏感。

Method: 提出LA4VLA框架：(1) 将专家演示轨迹分解为原子动作片段，配以低层动作描述，构建LA4-33K数据集；(2) 开发LA4VLA-1B轻量模型；(3) 探索三种语言-动作监督融入VLA学习的范式：仅LA预训练、顺序LA-to-VLA预训练、混合LA-VLA预训练。

Result: 在仿真和真实任务中，LA预训练的策略始终优于匹配的VLA预训练基线；混合LA-VLA预训练相较无预训练基线，在仿真任务中平均成功率提升17.8个百分点，真实任务中提升45.0个百分点。

Conclusion: LA4VLA是一种有效且互补的预训练策略，能够构建更强、更鲁棒的VLA策略。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are commonly pretrained on robot demonstrations by jointly mapping visual observations and language instructions to actions. However, dense visual-action supervision can dominate the comparatively sparse language-action signal. As a result, policies may rely on visual shortcuts rather than learn how language conditions action execution, making them sensitive to visual variations. To address this limitation, we propose LA4VLA, a language-action pretraining framework that enables policies to acquire language-conditioned action priors without visual observations. These priors capture reusable manipulation skills shared across tasks and scenes, reducing reliance on scene-specific visual cues. Specifically, LA4VLA decomposes expert demonstration trajectories into atomic action segments and pairs each segment with a corresponding low-level action description. This yields LA4-33K, a dataset of 33K Language-Action (LA) episodes derived entirely from existing demonstrations without additional robot data collection. We further develop LA4VLA-1B, a lightweight 1B-parameter VLA model, and investigate three paradigms for incorporating language-action supervision into VLA learning: LA-only pretraining, sequential LA-to-VLA pretraining, and mixed LA-VLA pretraining. Across simulation and real-world tasks, LA-pretrained policies consistently outperform matched VLA-pretrained counterparts, while combining LA and VLA supervision leads to further gains. In particular, mixed LA-VLA pretraining improves the average success rate of LA4VLA-1B over the no-pretraining baseline by up to 17.8 and 45.0 percentage points in simulation and real-world tasks, respectively. These results establish LA4VLA as an effective and complementary pretraining strategy for building stronger and more robust VLA policies.

</details>


### [150] [VibeAct: Vibration to Actions for Contact-Rich Reactive Robot Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27344)
*Yuemin Mao,Uksang Yoo,Jean Oh,Jonathan Francis,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VibeAct提出了一种框架，通过在真实世界和仿真之间共享接触和滑移的物理表征，将压电麦克风的振动触觉信号与基于仿真的强化学习策略训练桥接起来，在灵巧手操作任务中提升了策略成功率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作依赖快速、局部且常被视觉遮挡的接触事件。压电麦克风虽能提供高带宽振动信号，但这些信号难以在仿真中端到端模拟，导致难以从仿真到现实迁移策略。

Method: 在真实灵巧手中嵌入压电麦克风，通过遥操作采集振动数据；在标定的数字孪生中回放数据自动标注每根手指的接触和滑移标签；训练触觉估计器从真实麦克风波形预测接触和滑移；同时在仿真中基于相同的接触/滑移表征训练策略，绕过原始音频仿真。

Result: 在五类接触密集型任务（重抓取、手中重定向、插入）中，VibeAct始终优于仅依赖本体感觉和点云输入的基线方法。在需要持续反应控制的任务中增益最大，连续滑移幅度通道是最具信息量的观测。策略成功迁移到物理灵巧手-手臂平台。

Conclusion: 通过解耦触觉感知与策略学习，VibeAct实现了快速触觉反馈在仿真训练和现实部署中的有效利用，无需模拟原始音频信号，为灵巧操作中的触觉-仿真迁移提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation depends on contact events that are fast, local, and often visually occluded. Piezoelectric microphones offer a compact and high-bandwidth way to sense these interactions, but the resulting vibro-acoustic signals are difficult to simulate faithfully enough for end-to-end sim-to-real policy learning on dexterous robot hands. We propose VibeAct, a framework that bridges real vibrotactile sensing and simulation-based reinforcement learning through a shared physical representation of contact and slip. In the real world, we embed piezoelectric microphones into a dexterous robot hand and collect vibro-acoustic data through teleoperation, then replay the recordings in a calibrated digital clone to automatically label per-finger contact and slip. A tactile estimator learns to predict contact and slip from real microphone waveforms, while manipulation policies are trained in simulation on the same representation computed directly from simulated contacts. This decoupling lets policies exploit rapid tactile feedback without simulating raw audio. Across five contact-rich tasks spanning regrasping, in-hand reorientation, and insertion, VibeAct consistently outperforms a proprioception-and-point-cloud baseline in simulation, with the largest gains on tasks requiring sustained reactive control, where the continuous slip-magnitude channel proves the most informative observation. The learned policies transfer to a physical dexterous hand-arm platform, improving success rates on deployed tasks. Project videos and additional details are at https://vibeact.github.io/.

</details>


### [151] [Bridging Performance and Generalization in Reinforcement Learning for Agile Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27348)
*Jonathan Green,Jiaxu Xing,Nico Messikommer,Angel Romero,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向RL驱动的无人机竞速的零样本泛化框架，通过结合基于学习进度的任务感知切换与物理启发的程序化赛道生成器，在保持竞速速度的同时实现了7.4倍的泛化性能提升，并在无需显式状态估计的视觉端到端控制中成功泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL无人机竞速策略在特定环境中表现优秀，但零样本泛化能力极差——在未见过的赛道配置中往往直接坠毁。同时，现有提升泛化能力的方法会显著降低飞行速度，性能与泛化之间存在严重权衡。

Method: 提出一个两阶段框架：(1) 基于学习进度的任务感知切换机制，动态调整训练过程中赛道任务的分布；(2) 物理启发的程序化赛道生成器，产生多样且物理可行的赛道。两者共同训练出快速且鲁棒的通用策略，无需测试时自适应。

Result: 在真实世界多种未见赛道上实现了强零样本性能，泛化能力相比最先进方法提升7.4倍，同时保持了有竞争力的竞速速度。在视觉端到端控制（无显式状态估计）这一所有先前方法均失败的设定下，该方法仍能成功泛化。

Conclusion: 通过任务感知训练和物理启发式赛道生成，可以在不牺牲速度的前提下显著提升无人机竞速RL策略的零样本泛化能力，该方法在实际部署中具有强鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous drone racing is a fundamentally challenging regime for autonomous aerial robots, requiring time-optimal control while operating under persistent actuation saturation. While reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved human-level performance in this domain, current methods fail to generalize; policies trained on specific environments often crash immediately in unseen configurations. This failure reflects the intrinsic difficulty of zero-shot generalization in agile flight, arising from high-dimensional task variation and the tight coupling between safety and performance at high speeds. Existing approaches that improve generalization impose a substantial cost on flight speed: control policies must significantly degrade performance to achieve even modest levels of generalization. In this work, we propose a framework for zero-shot generalization in agile flight for RL-based drone racing. By combining task-aware switching based on learning progress with a physically informed procedural track generator, the framework produces a fast and robust generalist policy without test-time adaptation. Our method achieves strong zero-shot performance across a wide range of unseen racetracks in the real world, demonstrating a 7.4x improvement in generalization over the state-of-the-art approaches, while maintaining competitive racing speeds. We validate our method's results in both simulation and real-world settings, including a challenging vision-based, end-to-end control setting that operates without explicit state estimation, where all prior approaches fail to generalize.

</details>


### [152] [Continual Robot Policy Learning via Variational Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27353)
*Jiaxu Xing,Zhiyuan Zhu,Yunfan Ren,Ismail Geles,Yifan Zhai,Rudolf Reiter,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种持续学习框架，通过结合分析物理先验与神经网络残差学习隐藏动力学模型，并利用在线推断的隐条件进行可微仿真策略学习，使无人机在变化风场等重复扰动下实现约5倍于在线残差拟合的快速恢复（~1秒），大幅降低追踪误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的机器人面临持续变化的环境动力学（如风速、负载、电池等变化），而现有学习控制器通常在训练后即固定，无法利用部署经验持续改进策略性能。

Method: 1) 结合分析物理先验与神经残差构建动力学模型；2) 使用循环编码器从近期交互推断隐条件，并以此条件化残差模型和策略；3) 通过可微仿真在多样化的学习动力学上进行策略学习；4) 部署时用在线推断的隐条件替换采样条件，通过识别而非残差重新拟合来恢复重复动力学。

Result: 在真实四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务中，面对变化风场，策略约在1秒内恢复，比在线残差重新拟合快约5倍；大扰动悬停和跟踪误差分别降低65.7%和53.3%。

Conclusion: 所提框架利用部署经验的持续学习能有效提升机器人在未观测扰动下的策略性能，通过隐条件识别实现快速恢复，优于现有在线自适应方法。

Abstract: Robots deployed in the real world rarely operate under a single fixed dynamics model: wind changes, payloads vary, batteries drain, contacts shift, and hardware wears. Yet most learning-based controllers are trained once and deployed as if learning were complete. This prevents the robot from using deployment experience to further improve task performance. In this work, we propose a continual learning framework that uses real-world experience to improve robot policies under hidden and recurring dynamics. Our method learns a condition-aware dynamics model from real state-action trajectories by combining an analytical physics prior with a neural residual for unmodeled effects. A recurrent encoder infers the current hidden condition from recent interaction, and this estimate conditions both the residual model and the policy. Policy learning is performed via differentiable simulation using diverse learned dynamics sampled from the latent model. At deployment, these sampled conditions are replaced by conditions inferred online from recent real interaction, allowing the policy to recover recurring dynamics by recognition rather than residual re-fitting. Through extensive simulation studies and real-world experiments, we demonstrate that the framework improves policy performance under diverse unobserved disturbances. On real quadrotor trajectory tracking under changing wind, the policy recovers from recurring disturbances in roughly 1s, about 5x faster than online residual re-fitting. It also reduces large-disturbance hover and tracking errors by 65.7% and 53.3% over the state-of-the-art online adaptation approaches

</details>


### [153] [RouterVLA: Turning Smoke Tests into Supervision for Heterogeneous VLA Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27355)
*Xingyu Ren,Chugang Yi,Ge Ma,Youran Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RouterVLA在34,752条LIBERO-Plus rollout记录上，通过结果不交叠的交叉拟合，利用预部署评估滚转记录复用进行策略选择监督。透明探针-成功规则将留出成功率从0.4686提升至0.6149（+14.64pp），而仅标量配置下的学习型评分器与规则统计无差异，表明部署投入本身承载路由价值。复用已评估轨迹会使增益膨胀1.87倍，证明可信的路由需要结果分离；模型扩展提升个体策略，而部署感知路由提升系统整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索预部署评估滚转记录能否被复用来监督策略选择，以及在机器人团队中候选VLA策略的吸烟测试结果如何被压缩为全局优胜者。

Method: 提出RouterVLA方法，采用结果不交叠的交叉拟合策略：记录探针构建每个冻结专家的配置文件，独立试验评分所选专家而不进入其配置文件。在34,752条LIBERO-Plus rollout记录上进行实验。

Result: 透明探针-成功规则将留出成功率提升14.64个百分点（从0.4686到0.6149）；仅标量配置下学习型评分器与规则统计无差异；复用已评估轨迹使增益膨胀1.87倍。

Conclusion: 部署投入（commissioning）承载路由价值，额外标量评分器容量不创造路由价值；可信的账本路由需要结果分离；模型扩展提升个体策略，部署感知路由提升系统整体性能。

Abstract: We study whether pre-deployment evaluation rollouts can be reused to supervise policy selection. Robot teams routinely smoke test candidate vision-language-action (VLA) policies, then compress those trials into a global winner. RouterVLA evaluates this idea with outcome-disjoint cross-fitting: recorded probes build a profile for each frozen expert, and a separate trial scores the selected expert without entering its profile. Across 34,752 LIBERO-Plus rollout records, a transparent probe-success rule raises held-out success from 0.4686 to 0.6149, a +14.64pp gain. Under the scalar-only profiles studied here, learned scorers are statistically indistinguishable from this rule, showing that commissioning carries the routing value while extra scalar scorer capacity does not create it. Reusing the scored trial inflates the measured gain by $1.87\times$, so credible ledger routing needs outcome separation; model scaling improves individual policies, while commissioning-aware routing improves the system built from them.

</details>


### [154] [World Action Models Enable Continual Imitation Learning with Recurrent Generative Replays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27374)
*Manish Kumar Govind,Dominick Reilly,Smit Patel,Hieu Le,Srijan Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REGEN是一个基于世界动作模型（WAM）的持续模仿学习框架，通过递归生成伪重放轨迹，使机器人策略在不存储原始人类示范的情况下复习先前学过的任务。实验表明，REGEN相比顺序微调将灾难性遗忘减少高达50%，性能接近需要真实重放数据的特权经验回放方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决持续模仿学习中灾难性遗忘问题，避免存储大量原始人类示范数据的隐私和存储开销，同时保持对先前任务的学习能力。

Method: 提出REGEN框架，利用世界动作模型（WAM）的生成能力，递归地基于先前的任务指令和当前任务的观察，合成伪重放轨迹供机器人策略排练。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界操作任务中，REGEN相比顺序微调减少高达50%的灾难性遗忘，性能逼近使用真实重放数据的特权方法。同时识别出长程视觉退化与动作-观察不一致性为生成重放的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 世界动作模型（WAM）可以作为不依赖存储示范的持续机器人学习的有前途基础。

Abstract: Going beyond predicting robot actions, World Action Models (WAMs) can also generate future visual observations. We build on this generative capability to propose Recurrent Generative Replay (REGEN), a continual imitation learning framework that synthesizes pseudo-replay trajectories, enabling a robot policy to rehearse previously learned tasks without storing their original human demonstrations. During continual adaptation, REGEN recursively queries the WAM to synthesize pseudo-replay trajectories conditioned only on prior task instructions and current-task observations. Experiments in both simulation and real-world manipulation settings show that REGEN reduces catastrophic forgetting by up to $50\%$ relative to sequential fine-tuning, while approaching the performance of privileged experience replay methods that require access to real replay data. Finally, we analyze the factors limiting generated replay, identifying long-horizon visual degradation and action-observation inconsistency as the primary bottlenecks. Our results establish WAMs as a promising foundation for continual robot learning without stored demonstrations.

</details>


### [155] [Scalable Behavior Cloning with Open Data, Training, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27375)
*Arthur Allshire,Himanshu Gaurav Singh,Ritvik Singh,Adam Rashid,Hongsuk Choi,David McAllister,Justin Yu,Yiyuan Chen,Huang Huang,Pieter Abbeel,Xi Chen,Rocky Duan,Phillip Isola,Jitendra Malik,Fred Shentu,Guanya Shi,Philipp Wu,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ABC是一个全开源的行为克隆操作框架，包含最大开源遥操作数据集ABC-130K（3500小时、13万+片段、195项任务），以及开源硬件、训练基础设施和仿真管线，并提供400小时仿真数据及协同训练方案，验证了DiT和VLA模型在真实世界中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆研究中缺乏大规模、标准化、可复现的开源数据集和完整工具链，研究者难以公平比较和推进该领域发展。

Method: 构建了ABC-130K大规模遥操作数据集，开源硬件设置、训练基础设施、仿真管线及400小时仿真数据，提出仿真-真实协同训练方案，并系统比较了Diffusion Transformers和Vision-Language-Action模型的架构选择。

Result: 成功执行了如折纸盒和从钱包中取出信用卡等灵巧操作任务，仿真与真实世界评估高度相关，可作为可靠的模型消融替代方案。

Conclusion: 通过提供可复现的工具包，使研究者处于平等地位，为社区共同学习行为克隆奠定基础。

Abstract: We introduce ABC, a fully open-source stack for manipulation with behavior cloning. At its core is ABC-130K: the largest open-source teleoperation dataset to date, featuring 3,500 hours of data spanning over 130K episodes across 195 diverse tasks. Furthermore, we open-source our accessible hardware setup, training infrastructure, and simulation pipeline. We also release 400 hours of sim-teleop data and provide a co-training recipe that produces correlated simulation and real-world evaluation, offering a reliable proxy for ablating model-design and training decisions before costly real-world evaluation. We explore various training recipes and compare common architectural choices for Diffusion Transformers (DiT) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, grounding our findings in real-world evaluations. The resulting policies successfully execute dexterous tasks such as box folding and extracting credit cards from wallets. By providing a reproducible toolkit, we aim to place researchers on an equal footing, establishing the necessary foundation to learn the ABCs of Behavior Cloning together as a community.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [MPE-Adam: Multi-Population Evolutionary Optimization with Adam Refinement for QAOA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26670)
*Chi Quan Luu,Thai T. Vu,John Le*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: MPE-Adam 是一种将多群体进化搜索（全局探索）与 Adam 梯度优化（局部精炼）相结合的分阶段混合优化框架，用于提升量子近似优化算法（QAOA）中的参数优化性能，在 MaxCut 问题上相比进化算法和 SPSA 取得了更优的近似比和更低的方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法（如 QAOA）中的经典参数优化面临高维、非凸且存在测量噪声的参数空间，而现有单阶段优化器未能区分全局探索和局部精炼的角色，限制了互补策略的利用。

Method: 提出 MPE-Adam 混合优化框架，采用多群体进化搜索（Multi-Population Evolutionary Search）进行全局空间探索，再结合 Adam 梯度下降进行局部收敛精炼，结构化为模块化组件以适配量子软件流水线。

Result: 在最多 22 节点的随机 3-正则图 MaxCut 实例上，MPE-Adam 相比仅进化搜索和 SPSA 基线取得了更高的近似比和更低的方差，改进具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 结构化多阶段优化能够同时提升变分量子算法的解质量和量子软件层面的灵活性，MPE-Adam 作为模块化组件可有效集成到量子软件流水线中。

Abstract: Parameter optimization is a central bottleneck in variational quantum algorithms such as the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA). The classical optimizer must navigate a high-dimensional, non-convex parameter space under measurement noise. From a quantum software perspective, this process forms a multi-stage workflow: global exploration of the parameter space followed by local refinement within the hybrid quantum-classical loop. Most existing approaches, however, employ single-stage optimizers that do not separate these roles, which limits the use of complementary strategies. We propose MPE-Adam, a hybrid optimization framework that integrates multi-population evolutionary search for global exploration with Adam-based gradient refinement for local convergence. The method is structured as a modular component suitable for quantum software pipelines. We evaluate MPE-Adam on MaxCut instances generated from random 3-regular graphs with up to 22 nodes. The results show that MPE-Adam achieves higher approximation ratios and lower variance than evolutionary-only and SPSA-based baselines, with statistically significant improvements. These findings indicate that structured multi-stage optimization improves both solution quality and software-level flexibility in quantum applications.

</details>


### [157] [Generative Models on Analog Hardware with Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27294)
*Yu-Neng Wang,Sara Achour*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出模拟交互系统（AIS）框架，弥合了硬件实现的物理微分方程与现代生成模型之间的差距。通过时间分段参数、隐藏物理状态和WGAN训练，在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上实现了3-4倍于先前硬件的FID改进，每张图像能耗23uJ，比数字方案低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟硬件（如耦合振荡器、模拟伊辛机）能以极低能耗求解微分方程，但现代生成模型依赖灵活可编程的软件定义动力学，而硬件受限于固定的物理微分方程，表达能力有限，存在根本性不匹配。

Method: 提出AIS统一框架，引入两种硬件兼容机制：时变分段参数和隐藏物理状态；开发Wasserstein GAN训练流程，无需模型遵循特定轨迹；采用稀疏连接和低比特量化参数实现实际部署。

Result: 基于振荡器的AIS在MNIST上FID=27.6，Fashion-MNIST上FID=80.8，比先前最优硬件生成模型提升3-4倍；4比特稀疏架构下每张图像能耗23uJ，相比数字基线提高两个数量级。

Conclusion: AIS框架有效缩小了模拟硬件与生成模型之间的表达差距，表明通过合理的架构设计和训练方法，物理约束的硬件系统可以在极低能耗下实现有竞争力的生成建模性能。

Abstract: Analog hardware platforms such as coupled oscillators and Analog Ising Machines naturally solve differential equations at a fraction of the energy cost of digital computation, making them attractive for low-power generative modeling, yet a fundamental mismatch exists: modern generative models assume flexible, software-defined dynamics, whereas analog hardware imposes fixed, physics-determined differential equations with limited approximation capacity. This paper introduces Analog Interaction Systems (AIS), a unified framework for hardware-implementable dynamical systems, and empirically characterizes their expressivity gap relative to neural network baselines. Two hardware-compatible mechanisms are proposed to narrow this gap - time-varying piecewise parameters and hidden physical states - and a Wasserstein GAN training procedure is developed to enable training of these models without requiring them to follow a specific trajectory. We characterize how area and power scale with connection density and precision, showing that sparse connectivity and low-bit-width quantized parameters are necessary for practical implementation, and estimate an energy cost of 23uJ per generated image for the chosen architecture, representing a 2-orders-of-magnitude improvement over digital baselines. On MNIST and Fashion-MNIST, our oscillator-based AIS achieves FID scores of 27.6 and 80.8, outperforming the best prior hardware-implementable analog generative models by 3-4x with a 4-bit sparse architecture.

</details>


### [158] [An Instruction Set Architecture for IMPLY-based Memristive Processing-in-Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27319)
*Liam Splittgerber,Fabian Seiler,Nima TaheriNejad*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于忆阻存内计算（IMC）的独立通用微控制器架构，专为超低功耗边缘设备设计，采用IMPLY状态逻辑范式实现RV32I指令集，并通过仿真验证了其在能耗和电路指标上优于传统CMOS微控制器，并在智能环境传感器节点案例中证明了功能可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CMOS微控制器受限于冯·诺依曼瓶颈和静态功耗泄漏，无法满足超低功耗边缘计算的极严格能耗约束，而现有忆阻存内计算研究主要聚焦于用作数据密集型计算的协处理器加速器，缺乏针对独立通用IMC微控制器架构的探索。

Method: 提出一种基于忆阻交叉阵列的独立通用IMC微控制器架构，采用IMPLY状态逻辑范式实现完整RV32I指令集，并设计了支持交叉阵列中计算的新型寻址方案。

Result: 通过仿真评估了所提架构的能耗及其他电路级指标，并与传统微控制器进行了对比，同时在智能环境传感器节点应用案例中验证了设计的功能可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的忆阻IMC通用微控制器架构在超低功耗边缘计算场景中具有显著优势，为突破传统CMOS架构的能效瓶颈提供了可行的替代方案。

Abstract: The push towards expanded ultra-low-power edge computing necessitates hardware capable of operating under extremely strict energy constraints. Traditional Complementary Metal-Oxide-Semiconductor (CMOS) microcontrollers are fundamentally limited in this domain by the von Neumann bottleneck and by the static power leakage inherent to volatile memory. Memristive In-Memory Computing (IMC) offers a promising solution to these inefficiencies by unifying data storage and computation into a single non-volatile component. However, the State of the Art (SoA) predominantly focuses on accelerators designed to be a co-processor for data-intensive computation. This leaves the prospect of standalone, general-purpose IMC microcontroller architectures underexplored. This thesis proposes such an architecture tailored for ultra-low-power edge devices, alongside an instruction set closely derived from the RV32I standard. Using the IMPLY stateful logic paradigm, a complete implementation of the proposed instruction set is provided, and the novel addressing schema required to support computation in the memristive crossbar array is described as well. Then, the energy use and other circuit-level metrics of the proposed architecture are evaluated through simulation and compared against those of traditional microcontrollers. Finally, the functional viability of the design is demonstrated through an application case study, describing how the proposed design could be used in an intelligent environmental sensor node.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Having Dog Ears "for Real": Effects of Active and Passive Haptics on Embodying Non-Human Body Parts in VR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26364)
*Omar A. Khan,Bao Han Trinh,Lee Lisle,Tiffany D. Do*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过在线调查和VR用户实验，研究了主动触觉（振动手套）与被动触觉（物理头带）及其组合对非人体部位（狗耳）具身感的影响。发现被动触觉效果最优，组合反而降低效果，且非人体部位具身感与全身具身感正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR中非人体部位的化身虽在亚文化中流行，但形态差异会降低具身感。触觉反馈可补偿此效应，但不同触觉模态及其组合对具身感的影响尚缺乏比较研究。

Method: 先通过在线问卷（n=63）确定动物耳朵为生态效度高的非人体部位；随后开展2×2被试内实验（n=28），操纵主动触觉（振动手套）和被动触觉（物理头带），测量参与者触摸虚拟狗耳时的具身感。

Result: （1）被动触觉产生最强的整体具身感；（2）组合模态反而削弱了被动触觉的益处；（3）非人体部位具身感与全身具身感呈正相关。

Conclusion: 被动触觉在增强非人体部位具身感方面优于主动触觉及其组合，组合触觉可能存在干扰效应。研究结果对具身化文献及VR应用设计具有启示意义。

Abstract: Embodying non-human body parts in VR is a prevalent practice among certain subcultures and is a personally important creative outlet to many individuals. However, the discrepant morphology between real and virtual bodies can decrease Sense of Embodiment (SoE). Haptic feedback can compensate by increasing SoE felt towards non-human body parts, but there is a literature gap in comparing the effects of different haptic modalities, and their combinations, on SoE. Through an online survey sent out to social VR communities (n = 63), we determined that animal ears are a commonly embodied and ecologically valid non-human body part to study. We then ran a 2x2 within-subjects user study (n = 28) with two independent variables: active haptics, delivered through vibrotactile gloves, and passive haptics, delivered through a physical headband, for when participants reach up to touch virtual dog ears appended to their avatar in VR. Our findings show that (1) passive haptics produced the strongest overall embodiment outcomes, (2) combining modalities reduced the benefits of passive haptics, and (3) SoE towards non-human body parts positively correlates with SoE towards the entire avatar. We discuss implications of our findings in various domains, and on embodiment literature.

</details>


### [160] [Assistive Visual Cues for Visual Neglect Patients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26407)
*Per Bjerre,Andreas Køllund Pedersen,Hendrik Knoche*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究比较了内源性线索和外源性线索对视觉忽视患者注意引导的效果，发现内源性线索效果更优，两者结合可进一步提升，但患者间差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线索提示研究多针对正常视力人群，而视觉忽视患者存在视野注意缺陷，需要更强线索辅助治疗，因此有必要专门探究两类线索对该群体的效果。

Method: 通过实验比较内源性线索（基于符号/位置预测）和外源性线索（基于突兀刺激）对视觉忽视患者目标获取时间和错误率的影响，并以单病例详细分析最优线索组合的性能。

Result: 视觉忽视患者在忽视区域内对内源性线索反应更好；部分患者结合两种线索可进一步提升表现。示例患者中最佳内源性线索平均获取时间3.5秒，最佳外源性6.5秒，结合后降至2.8秒。

Conclusion: 内源性线索对视觉忽视患者的注意引导优于外源性线索，联合使用可能更有效，但需个体化设计以适应患者间的显著差异。

Abstract: Previous research on exogenous and endogenous cues has shown how they direct attention and improve interaction speed and error rate in applications. However, most studies focus on people with normal sight. People suffering from visual neglect have difficulties attending to parts of the visual field. One treatment method calls for the use of strong visual cues to remind patients of their neglected area and help guide their attention to it. Therefore, we examine the effects of endogenous and exogenous cues on visual neglect patients. Our results showed that visual neglect patients perform better with endogenous cues, when targets are within their neglected area. In some cases, combining exogenous and endogenous cues improve performance further. However, the performance varies greatly between patients. Using one neglect patient as an example, we saw that the best endogenous cue had an average acquisition time of 3.5 seconds compared to 6.5 for the best exogenous. Combining exogenous and endogenous cues further improved acquisition time to 2.8 seconds.

</details>


### [161] [TinyCNNDeep: Lightweight Attention-Based CNN for EEG Classification of Eye States and Sleep Deprivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26506)
*Thien Nhan Vo,Yen Nhi Tran Thi,Ngan Nguyen Xuan Phuong,Xuan-The Tran*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出TinyCNNDeep，一种结合残差学习和Squeeze-and-Excitation注意力机制的轻量级卷积神经网络，将多通道EEG信号转换为2D灰度图像，用于联合识别睡眠状态（正常/剥夺）和眼动状态（睁眼/闭眼）。在35名受试者的数据集上，四类分类准确率达83.69%，远超基线模型（随机森林47.66%），而EEGNet等经典架构近乎随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠剥夺会损害警觉性和认知功能，但当前研究缺乏从EEG中联合识别睡眠条件（正常vs剥夺）和眼动状态（睁眼vs闭眼）的方法，这一问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出TinyCNNDeep轻量级CNN，结合残差学习和Squeeze-and-Excitation注意力模块。将5个生理相关通道（Fp1, Fp2, O1, Oz, O2）的短时多通道EEG片段，通过逐通道Z-score归一化、最小-最大缩放和中心填充转换为224×224灰度图像，利用2D卷积联合建模通道间和时序结构。

Result: 在35名受试者的正常睡眠和睡眠剥夺数据集上，TinyCNNDeep的受试者平均准确率为83.69%，比最强基线（随机森林+时频特征，47.66%）高出36.03个百分点，而EEGNet、ShallowConvNet、DeepConvNet等经典架构表现接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 基于图像的EEG表示结合残差特征提取和通道注意力机制，能够在最小电极配置下为多类睡眠相关EEG分类提供准确且计算高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Sleep deprivation impairs vigilance and cognitive function, yet jointly identifying the sleep condition (normal vs deprived) and the eye state (open vs closed) from electroencephalography (EEG) remains underexplored. We address this four-class problem with TinyCNNDeep, a lightweight convolutional neural network that combines residual learning with a Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE) attention module. We convert short multi-channel EEG segments from five physiologically relevant channels (Fp1, Fp2, O1, Oz, O2) into 224x224 grayscale images through per-channel Z-score normalization, min-max scaling, and center padding, enabling 2D convolutions to jointly model inter-channel and temporal structure. On a 35-subject dataset recorded under normal-sleep and sleep-deprivation sessions, TinyCNNDeep attains a subject-wise mean accuracy of 83.69%, outperforming the strongest baseline (Random Forest with combined time-frequency features, 47.66%) by 36.03 percentage points, while three established EEG architectures (EEGNet, ShallowConvNet, DeepConvNet) operate near chance. Per-subject analysis quantifies inter-subject variability, and confusion-matrix inspection shows that residual misclassifications concentrate between eyes-closed states across sleep conditions. These results indicate that an image-based EEG representation paired with residual feature extraction and channel attention provides an accurate and computationally efficient framework for multiclass sleep-related EEG classification under a minimal electrode configuration.

</details>


### [162] [DanceDuo: Bridging Human Movement and AI Choreography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26507)
*Gia-Cat Bui-Le,Tuong-Vy Truong-Thuy,Hai-Dang Nguyen,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: DanceDuo是一个基于扩散模型的AI舞蹈生成平台，支持用户选择音乐、人物模型并导入个人舞蹈视频进行对比，通过人体姿态估计实现AI生成舞蹈与用户表演的对比分析，用户研究证实其界面直观且舞蹈对比功能备受好评。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用深度学习和生成模型的进展，开发一个能够根据多种音乐风格生成AI编舞序列的平台，以鼓励舞蹈练习并提升用户体验。

Method: 采用扩散模型生成与音乐同步的AI编舞序列，集成人体姿态估计模型实现用户表演与AI生成序列的对比，提供音乐选择、人物模型选择和视频导入等交互功能。

Result: 用户研究表明界面直观，舞蹈对比功能获得特别好评。

Conclusion: DanceDuo显著推动了AI在舞蹈编排中的集成应用，为娱乐和专业领域提供了新的途径。

Abstract: In recent years, advancements in deep learning and generative models have revolutionized music-driven dance generation. This paper introduces a novel platform, namely DanceDuo, leveraging diffusion models to generate AI-choreographed dance sequences synchronized with a variety of music genres, to encourage dancing practice. The system allows users to interact with AI by selecting music tracks, humanoid models, and importing personal dance videos for comparison, fostering a rich and engaging user experience. DanceDuo not only offers dance generation but also integrates human pose estimation models to provide users with insightful comparisons of their own performances with AI-generated sequences. We conducted a comprehensive user study, revealing that users found the interface intuitive, with particular praise for the dance comparison feature. Our DanceDuo contributes significantly to the integration of AI in dance choreography, offering novel avenues for both recreational and professional applications.

</details>


### [163] [Budget-Aware Keyboardless Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26508)
*Quang-Thang Nguyen,Gia-Phuc Song-Dong,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅用普通摄像头和打印键盘纸的虚拟键盘系统，结合现代计算机视觉技术和传统图像处理算法，通过指甲颜色分析实现按键检测，无需复杂校准或特殊光照，实验展示了实际应用的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统输入设备（键盘、鼠标、显示器）限制了用户的移动性，现有虚拟键盘方案往往需要复杂设置或昂贵设备，因此需要一种低成本、易部署的虚拟键盘解决方案。

Method: 使用普通摄像头拍摄打印键盘布局的纸张，结合现代分割/检测模型与传统图像处理算法识别键盘区域，通过分析用户指甲颜色变化来检测触摸动作。

Result: 实验表明该方案在键盘和按键检测方面具有良好的实际应用效果，参与用户研究的受试者也对该系统表现出兴趣。

Conclusion: 提出的基于普通摄像头和打印键盘的虚拟键盘系统具有实用价值，为低成本、易部署的人机交互方式提供了新思路。

Abstract: Interacting with computers typically relies on traditional input devices such as keyboards, mice, and monitors, which can be cumbersome for users seeking greater mobility. Virtual keyboards have been explored to address these limitations, but they often involve complex setups or expensive equipment. This paper proposes a novel virtual keyboard system that leverages only a standard camera and a paper with a printed keyboard layout. Unlike previous methods requiring complex calibration or special lighting conditions, our approach can work on standard environment using modern computer vision technologies. Combining modern segmentation and detection models with traditional image processing algorithms, we efficiently identify the keyboard region. Touch detection is performed using an algorithm analyzing the color of the user's fingernail. Experiments demonstrated a promising results our proposed solution of keyboard and keystroke detection for practical applications. Participants attended our user study also found the proposed system interesting.

</details>


### [164] [Co-Designing Community-Centered AI Education for Adults: A Midwestern Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26565)
*Yao Lyu,Leonymae Aumentado,Holden Winton,Jared Lee Katzman,Sparkle Berry,Zachary Rowe,Kimberly Sanders,Tawanna R. Dillahunt*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究报告了一个面向非高等教育成年人的AI教育案例，参与者为54名来自美国中西部城市东区非裔美国人社区的成年人。通过社区合作设计的AI教育课程，发现参与者对AI的担忧从一般性转向具体、基于本地的问题，并提出了基于社区能力的AI素养框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI教育日益重要，但高等教育以外的成年人群体受到的关注不足，特别是在边缘化社区中。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，与社区合作伙伴共同设计AI教育课程，面向54名成年人（48名线下、6名线上）开展教育 session，并收集分析参与者的反馈和讨论。

Result: 参与者对AI的担忧持续存在，但从一般性担忧转变为针对AI设计和部署的、基于本地情境的具体问题。

Conclusion: 需要建立基于本地社区情境的AI素养框架，以增强社区能力，而非采用通用性框架。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) education is increasingly important, yet adults outside higher education receive less attention. We report a case study of an AI education session with 54 adults (48 in-person and 6 virtual) in a predominantly African American community on the east side of a major Midwestern city. We ask: "What does AI education for adults outside formal educational systems look like in practice?" and "What does this AI education session reveal about AI literacy at the community level?" Through a co-designed session developed with community partners, we found that concerns about AI persisted but shifted to specific, locally grounded questions about AI design and deployment. We also discuss AI literacy from a community capacity perspective and argue for AI literacy frameworks grounded in local community contexts that strengthen community capacity.

</details>


### [165] [An exploratory behavioral and electroencephalographic study of artificial intelligence-assisted learning modes in high school students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26579)
*Kashika Khurana,Ally Liew*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过EEG和行为数据，对比了三种AI交互模式（导师、协作者、解决者）对青少年认知投入的影响，发现行为指标存在显著差异，EEG模式虽有差异但未达统计显著性，并初步探索了AI的短期后遗效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多将AI接触视为二元条件（有AI vs. 无AI），缺乏对交互模态差异及接触后效应的区分，因此本文旨在探究不同AI交互模式是否影响额叶EEG频谱活动及问题解决行为。

Method: 48名14-18岁青少年（男女各半）在三种AI交互模式（导师、协作者、解决者）下完成两次平衡顺序的测验，采用脑电图记录额叶频谱活动，并用量化行为指标（主动性、加工过程、压力）进行序数尺度评估，使用Friedman检验、重复测量方差分析、配对t检验及效应量计算进行统计分析。

Result: 三种AI交互模式在所有三种行为测量上均表现出显著差异。EEG模式在AI交互条件下呈现描述性差异，但未达到统计显著性。初步探索了AI的短期后遗效应可能性。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了初步的行为证据和电生理观测结果，揭示了不同AI交互模式可能引发的神经活动和行为差异，并为未来人-AI交互研究建立了可复现的分析框架。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) is rapidly integrating into education, concerns have emerged regarding its potential implications on cognitive engagement and problem-solving behavior. However, existing research largely treats AI exposure as a binary condition (AI vs. no-AI), with limited differentiation between interaction modalities and post-exposure effects. This study investigates whether distinct AI interaction modes (Tutor, Collaborator, Solver) influence frontal EEG spectral activity. Electroencephalography (EEG) data and quantified behavioral metrics were recorded from 48 study participants (24 males, 24 females; ages 14-18) across two counterbalanced quizzes in a within-subject design. Statistical analyses included Friedman tests, repeated-measures ANOVA, paired t-tests, and effect size calculations. Behavioral changes were mathematically analyzed in an observation matrix of three characteristics -Initiation, Processing, and Stress-measured on an ordinal scale. Each mode showed significant differences in all three behavioral measures. Descriptive EEG patterns in AI interaction mode were observed, and the possibility of short-term carryover effects of AI was explored. Although the EEG data did not reach statistical significance, the patterns observed across the three AI interaction modes warrant further investigation. This study provides preliminary behavioral evidence and investigative electrophysiological observations, exploring possible AI-interaction-mode-based differences in neural activity and behavior, while establishing a replicable framework for future human-AI interaction studies.

</details>


### [166] [HiLSVA: Design and Evaluation of a Human-in-the-Loop Agentic System for Scientific Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26614)
*Kuangshi Ai,Patrick Phuoc Do,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: HiLSVA是一个支持人机协同（混合主动）的科学可视化智能体系统，通过计划优先的多智能体架构、显式人工监督、逐步溯源追踪和测试时自适应学习，在保证安全可复现的前提下实现自然语言与直接操控的灵活交互，实验表明混合主动模式能提升任务完成度、用户控制感和工作流透明度，但存在执行效率与人类监督之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在科学可视化中过度强调自主性而忽视人类分析控制，导致透明度和人工监督不足，亟需将人类置于回路中的协作式设计。

Method: 提出HiLSVA系统，采用计划优先的多智能体架构，集成显式人类监督、逐步溯源追踪和测试时自适应学习，支持自然语言和直接操控两种交互方式，并通过沙箱执行确保安全可复现。

Result: 通过代表性案例研究和12名不同专业水平用户的对照实验，结果表明混合主动交互提升了任务完成度、用户控制感和工作流透明度，同时揭示了执行效率与人类监督之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 强调了以人为中心的设计在智能体科学可视化中的重要性，为未来协作式可视化系统的开发提供了指导方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents enable natural language interaction for scientific visualization (SciVis). Still, prior systems have essentially prioritized autonomy over human analytical control, thereby limiting transparency and human oversight. We present HiLSVA, a human-in-the-loop agentic system that supports mixed-initiative SciVis workflows. HiLSVA integrates a plan-first multi-agent architecture with explicit human oversight, stepwise provenance tracking, and learn-at-test-time adaptation from user feedback. The system supports fluid handoff between humans and agents through both natural language and direct manipulation of visualizations, while sandboxed execution ensures safe, reproducible workflows. In doing so, HiLSVA reframes agentic SciVis as a collaborative process that augments, rather than replaces, human analytical reasoning. We evaluate HiLSVA through representative case studies and a controlled user study with twelve participants of varying expertise across multiple autonomy settings. Results show that mixed-initiative interaction improves task completion, user control, and workflow transparency across different levels of user expertise, while revealing a tradeoff between execution efficiency and human oversight. These findings highlight the importance of human-centered design in agentic SciVis and guide the development of future collaborative visualization systems. We encourage readers to explore our demo video, case studies, and source code at https://hilsva.github.io/.

</details>


### [167] [Reviving Reflection-in-Action: Instilling Designerly Thinking in AI-Supported Ideation through Multimodal Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26626)
*Samangi Wadinambiarachchi,Jenny Waycott,Greg Wadley*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了多模态提示（尤其是草图模态）在AI创意支持工具中的应用，发现草图模态虽能提升设计流畅性，但用户仍偏好文本提示，并讨论了如何在AI交互中保留反思性手绘以维持学生核心设计技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI创意支持工具主要依赖文本提示生成面向解决方案的输出，但多模态提示（尤其是引入"生产性摩擦"以鼓励迭代和反思）在设计师-AI交互中的潜在价值尚未被充分探索。

Method: 开发了原型AI工具SketchifAI，采用混合方法、被试内设计，研究不同输入模态（文本、草图、草图+标签）对设计专业学生意图表达能力、创意支持感知和发散思维表现的影响。

Result: 草图模态倾向于增强流畅性，但在多样性、原创性或质量方面与文本模态相比无显著差异；然而，参与者表现出对文本提示的强烈偏好。

Conclusion: 讨论了如何设计AI工具以重新引入"通过草图反思"的机制，确保设计师-AI交互支持而非侵蚀学生的基本设计技能。

Abstract: Current AI-powered creativity support tools (AI-CSTs) primarily use text prompting to generate solution-oriented outputs. However, the potential value of multimodal prompting in designer-AI interaction, specifically the introduction of productive friction to encourage iteration and reflection, has not been fully explored. To address this, we developed SketchifAI, a prototype AI-CST, and evaluated it with design students. In a mixed-methods, within-participants study, we examined how different input modalities (text, sketch, and sketch-plus-tags) affected design students' perceived ability to express their intent, their perception of creativity support, and their divergent thinking performance. Our preliminary findings suggest that the sketch modality tended to enhance fluency, with inconclusive evidence for differences in variety, originality, or quality compared to text modality. Yet, paradoxically, participants showed a strong preference for text prompting. We discuss how AI tools might be designed to reintroduce reflection-through-sketching, ensuring that designer-AI interaction supports, rather than erodes, essential design skills in students.

</details>


### [168] [Invisible Impact of Empathy on Behavioral Change: Isolating the Effect of Empathy in Long-term Physical Activity Coaching Chatbot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26641)
*Li Siyan,Kai-Hui Liang,Shopnil Shahriar,Yilin Ye,Shiyoh Goetsu,Wei-Wei Du,Masahiro Yoshida,Tsunayuki Ohwa,Xuhai Xu,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对13名参与者进行为期六周的实验，研究构建了三款仅在共情水平上有所差异的WhatsApp身体活动教练聊天机器人。参与者难以区分共情条件，非共情版本被认为更吸引人、更有用，但高共情版本与更大的步数增加和更快的意图改善相关，表明共情的作用是微妙的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对话系统通常默认共情是必要的，但并未评估其真实影响，尤其是在行为改变场景中，动机和依从性往往取决于微妙的用户-聊天机器人动态。本文旨在检验这一假设。

Method: 构建三个仅在共情水平上有所不同的WhatsApp身体活动教练聊天机器人，进行六周内受试者研究（N=13），通过参与者区分能力、参与度评分、步数变化和意图改善等指标进行评估，并采用精细化可能性模型（ELM）的外围路径解释结果。

Result: 参与者难以区分共情条件；非共情版本在参与度和有用性方面评分更高；高共情版本与更大的平均步数增加和更快的意图改善相关。

Conclusion: 共情的作用是微妙的：虽然外行用户难以明确识别，但它仍能塑造支持持续改变的动机和信任。提出了设计下一代身体活动教练聊天机器人的启示，平衡有效性与类人连接。

Abstract: Current dialogue systems, powered by large language models, often treat empathy as essential without assessing its true impact, especially in behavior change, where motivation and adherence often depend on subtle user-chatbot dynamics. We examine this assumption by building three WhatsApp physical-activity (PA) coaching chatbots that differ only in empathy level and evaluating them in a six-week within-subject study (N = 13). Participants struggled to distinguish between the empathy conditions, and the non-empathetic version was often rated as more engaging and useful. However, higher-empathy variants were still associated with a larger overall average increase in step counts and faster improvement in intention to follow advice. These results suggest empathy's role is nuanced: it may be hard for lay users to identify explicitly, but it can still shape motivation and trust that support sustained change. We interpret this pattern through the Elaboration Likelihood Model's peripheral route. We highlight design implications for building next-generation PA coaching chatbots that balance effectiveness with human-like connection.

</details>


### [169] [From Content to Strategy: Understanding the Motivations, Processes, and Impacts of AI-Guided Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26672)
*Chang Wan,Angel Hsing-Chi Hwang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对26名用户的深度访谈，探讨了AI引导沟通（AI-guided communication）这一新兴模式。研究发现用户倾向于在亲密关系中使用AI分析挑战性场景，因为它能促进自我反思、缓解情绪、防止冲突升级、提供多元视角和安全空间。用户认为AI提升了共情和沟通能力，但也存在自我怀疑和担心失去独特性的顾虑。对长期关系影响的看法存在分歧，取决于AI解决短期人际挑战的感知有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的应用已从单纯的消息内容生成扩展到引导个人沟通策略（即AI引导式沟通），但这一新兴使用模式及其后果的理论和实证理解仍然有限。

Method: 对26名使用AI制定沟通策略的用户进行深度访谈。

Result: 参与者强烈偏好使用AI分析亲密关系中的挑战性场景，原因包括促进自我反思、缓解情绪、防止冲突升级、提供多元视角和安全空间；AI引导式沟通增强了共情和沟通技能，但也引发自我怀疑和失去独特性的担忧；对长期关系影响的看法不一，取决于AI解决短期人际挑战的感知有用性。

Conclusion: AI引导式沟通在亲密关系场景中具有积极潜力，但存在用户自我认同方面的负面效应，其长期关系影响具有情境依赖性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence-mediated communication (AI-MC) is conceptualized as applying AI to augment or generate message content (Hancock et al., 2020). However, advances in generative AI have expanded its use beyond generating content to guiding individuals' communication strategies, that is, AI-guided communication, yet theoretical and empirical understandings of this emerging use pattern and its consequences remains limited. To address this gap, this study conducted 26 in-depth interviews with individuals who have used AI to develop their communication strategies. Findings suggest participants strongly preferred using AI to analyze challenging scenarios in close relationships, because it fostered self-reflection, eased emotions, prevented conflict escalation, offered multiple perspectives, and provided a safe, nonjudgmental space for self-disclosure. Participants also stated that AI-guided communication enhanced their empathy and communication skills, though some voiced self-doubt and worried about losing their uniqueness. Views on long-term relational impact were mixed, depending on perceived usefulness of AI for resolving short-term interpersonal challenges.

</details>


### [170] [Modeling Adaptive Visual Search in Semantically Hierarchical Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26725)
*Saku Sourulahti,Jussi P. P. Jokinen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个计算认知模型，研究信息分组如何影响视觉搜索，利用计算理性将用户行为视为对认知和任务约束的适应。模型强调人类利用层次化任务表征，通过语义和视觉结构提升搜索效率，并通过两项人类实验验证：语义分组与空间分组一致时可改善搜索性能。模型能复现任务时长和眼动模式，并可用于用户界面线框的快速原型设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究信息分组（特别是语义分组）对视觉搜索效率的影响机制，理解人类如何利用层次化记忆结构在认知和视觉系统约束下优化搜索行为，并将该机制应用于用户界面设计的快速评估。

Method: 基于计算理性框架构建层次化认知模型，将用户行为建模为对认知约束和任务约束的适应性优化；通过两项人类行为实验（视觉搜索与语义分类）收集任务时长和眼动数据，对模型进行验证。

Result: 语义分组与空间分组对齐时可显著提升搜索性能；模型成功复现了人类实验中的任务完成时间和眼动模式。

Conclusion: 该模型扩展了现有视觉搜索模型，能够解释人类如何利用层次化语义-视觉结构提升搜索效率，并具备在真实用户界面线框设计中进行快速原型评估的潜力。

Abstract: This paper introduces a computational cognitive model to investigate how information grouping impacts visual search, a key consideration in user interface design. The model uses computational rationality to view user behavior as an adaptation to cognitive and task constraints. Our work highlights that humans use hierarchical task representations, exploiting semantic and visual structures to improve search efficiency within the constraints of the visual system. We validate this model with data from two human studies focused on visual search and semantic categorization, demonstrating that semantic grouping improves search performance when it aligns with spatial grouping. Our model replicates task durations and eye movement patterns. By improving understanding of how hierarchical memory structures are utilized in human cognition, the model extends previous visual search models. We showcase our model in the rapid prototyping and evaluation of semantic visual groupings within user interface wireframes, suggesting a pathway toward applications in more complex, real-world interface design.

</details>


### [171] ['A bit of chaos and madness': The AI Assessment Scale and the work of assessment reform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26729)
*Mike Perkins,Darius Postma,Jasper Roe,Susan Sisay,Craig Holdcroft*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对越南一所私立国际大学和英国一所公立大学的30名学术人员进行焦点小组访谈，探讨生成式AI评估量表（AIAS）的实施经验。研究发现AIAS能提供共享语言、明确边界并促进评估反思，但实施效果受治理、工具获取、教师信心、工作量、学科背景等因素影响，可能沦为合规工具而非促进教学创新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI对大学评估设计提出挑战，现有AI评估框架（如AIAS）缺乏教职员工实施经验的实证证据。

Method: 定性研究，在两个国家（越南、英国）高校开展5场焦点小组访谈（30名学术人员），采用混合主题分析，以批判性AI素养作为敏化概念。

Result: 识别出六个主题：识别与整合AI、促进条件、能力建设、采纳路径、实践伦理、重构教学法。AIAS能促进真实评估设计，但若与学习成果、学科背景和教师能力脱节，可能沦为合规层。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了GenAI评估框架从政策采纳到教学实施的制度条件的实证证据，强调实施效果取决于治理、工具、教师信心、工作量、诚信问题、学科情境及学习成果对齐等多重因素。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) has intensified pressure on universities to redesign assessment while maintaining integrity, equity, and validity. Structured frameworks such as the Artificial Intelligence Assessment Scale (AIAS) offer one response, but evidence of how staff experience their implementation remains limited. This qualitative study examines AIAS implementation at a private international university in Vietnam and a public university in the United Kingdom. Data from five focus groups with 30 academic staff were analysed using hybrid thematic analysis, with Critical AI Literacy used as a sensitising concept. Six themes were developed: recognising and integrating AI, facilitating conditions, building capacity, pathways to adoption, ethics in practice, and reframing pedagogy.
  Staff valued the AIAS as a shared language for legitimising GenAI use, clarifying boundaries, and prompting reflection on assessment design. However, implementation was shaped by governance, tool access, staff confidence, workload, integrity concerns, disciplinary context, and alignment with learning outcomes. The findings show that the AIAS could prompt authentic assessment design and student engagement, but may become a compliance layer when disconnected from learning outcomes, disciplinary context, and staff capacity. This study contributes empirical evidence on the institutional conditions through which GenAI assessment frameworks move from policy adoption to pedagogical enactment.

</details>


### [172] [MedSWFlow: An Open-Source LLM Workflow for Drafting Medical Social Work Case Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26884)
*Yulin Mao,Shiyu Li,Shuping Song,Yuling Zhang,Yajun Song*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MedSWFlow是一个开源、模型无关的大语言模型工作流，用于起草医疗社会工作报告案例计划，将专业任务分解为六个阶段并通过分阶段提示生成结构化案例档案和可审查的评估表格与服务计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗社会工作报告案例计划的编制是一项复杂且高度专业化的任务，现有方法缺乏标准化和可复现性，需要一种系统化的AI辅助框架来提升效率与一致性。

Method: 将案例计划编制分解为评估、问题分析、目标设定、干预计划、风险预判和预期效果评估六个阶段；通过标准化案例输入、构建结构化案例档案、利用分阶段提示生成可审查的评估表格和服务计划；采用模型无关设计，支持多种LLM提供商。

Result: 开发并开源了MedSWFlow框架，实现了基于LLM的可复现案例计划生成，输出内容作为供从业者审查的草稿而非最终决策。

Conclusion: MedSWFlow为医疗社会工作报告案例计划编制提供了标准化、可复现的AI辅助方案，但输出结果需经专业人员审查后才能用于实际决策。

Abstract: We present MedSWFlow, an open-source, model-agnostic LLM workflow for drafting medical social work case plans. The framework translates professional case-planning tasks into six stages: assessment, problem analysis, goal setting, intervention planning, risk anticipation, and planned effect evaluation. Drawing on established social work and behavioral frameworks, MedSWFlow standardizes case inputs, builds structured case profiles, and generates reviewable assessment forms and service plans through staged prompting. The system is released as an open-source research framework for reproducible case-plan generation across LLM providers. Outputs are intended as practitioner-reviewed drafts rather than final service decisions. Source code: https://github.com/santhiyacw-droid/MedSWFlow/tree/main.

</details>


### [173] [Optimizing Human-Machine Interface for Real-Time AI Support in the Operating Room: the CVS Copilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26886)
*Lorenzo Arboit,Nicolas Chanel,Aditya Murali,Pietro Mascagni,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过17名外科医生的混合方法用户中心设计研究，优化了腹腔镜胆囊切除术中AI辅助安全手术的人机界面（CVS Copilot），发现大多数医生支持AI决策支持但拒绝自主决策，偏好视觉化、医生控制、最小侵入性的显示方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统用于腹腔镜胆囊切除术中自动关键安全视野（CVS）评估已接近临床转化，但临床安全性和有效性不仅取决于算法性能，还取决于人机界面（HMI）的质量。本研究旨在探索如何最优地呈现和控制AI生成的预测信息。

Method: 采用混合方法、以用户为中心的设计研究，招募17名外科医生（包括住院医师、主治医师和教授），通过访谈探索交互模式、辅助时机、可视化策略和控制机制，使用反思性主题分析和人因启发式方法进行分析。

Result: 大多数外科医生（16/17）支持AI用于术中决策支持但拒绝自主决策；主治医师（13/14）偏好关键时刻的最小AI反馈，住院医师（3/3）倾向于带置信度指示器和按需解剖覆盖的可选指导；医生一致优先选择视觉化、医生控制、最小侵入性显示（最小覆盖层16/17，按需解剖分割13/17）。

Conclusion: 研究结果设计出CVS Copilot——一种医生控制、角色自适应的人机界面，提供基于AI的CVS评估，具有最小默认可视化和可选覆盖层功能。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems for automated Critical View of Safety (CVS) assessment in laparoscopic cholecystectomy are nearing clinical translation. Beyond algorithmic performance, clinical safety and effectiveness depend on the quality of the human-machine interface (HMI). This work examines how AI-generated predictions should be presented and controlled intraoperatively. Seventeen surgeons, including residents, attending surgeons, and professors, took part in a mixed-methods, user-centered design study to optimize an intraoperative HMI for AI-assisted safe laparoscopic cholecystectomy. Interviews explored interaction modalities, timing of assistance, visualization strategies, and control mechanisms across surgical roles, and were analyzed using reflexive thematic analysis and human-factors heuristics. Most surgeons (16/17) supported the use of AI for intraoperative decision support while rejecting autonomous decision-making. Attendings preferred minimal AI feedback at decisive moments (13/14), whereas residents favored optional guidance (3/3) with confidence indicators and on-demand anatomical overlays. Across interviews, surgeons consistently prioritized visual, surgeon-controlled, minimally intrusive displays, with the strongest support for a minimal overlay (16/17) and on-demand anatomical segmentation (13/17). Recurrent concerns included persistent overlays, haptic feedback, and numeric confidence displays, although these were not uniformly raised across the cohort. These findings informed the design of CVS Copilot, a surgeon-controlled, role-adaptive HMI that provides AI-based CVS assessment with minimal default visualization and optional overlays.

</details>


### [174] [Game Changers: Designing and Measuring Dynamic Feedback To Help Users Self-Regulate in a VR Pointing Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26925)
*Bastian Ilsø Hougaard,Scott Bateman,Iris Brunner,Lars Evald,Hendrik Knoche*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过虚拟现实指向任务，系统研究了三种性能指标（短完成时间、直线运动、高峰值速度）在不同时间点（持续、动作结束、任务结束）驱动的动态反馈对玩家表现和感知的影响。研究发现动态反馈平均有助于玩家更直更快地指向，但个体差异显著。研究为游戏设计师根据期望的性能目标选择反馈方案提供了依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏通过反馈动态传达信息对玩家表现、学习和提升至关重要，但性能指标如何影响玩家在指向或操控等核心游戏任务中的表现和感知尚不清楚。

Method: 采用虚拟现实指向任务，系统探索三种性能指标（短完成时间、直线运动、高峰值速度）驱动的反馈在不同时间点（持续、动作结束、任务结束）对玩家的影响，并进行定量比较。

Result: 动态反馈平均帮助玩家指向更直更快，但对部分玩家效果微小或相反。研究对三种反馈形式进行了定量比较。

Conclusion: 研究改进了游戏设计师设计动态反馈的基础，帮助其知晓何时采用与期望游戏性能目标一致的反馈方案。

Abstract: The way games dynamically convey information through feedback is critical to players' ability to perform, learn, and improve. However, it is poorly understood how performance metrics impact player performance and perception in core game tasks like pointing or steering. With a virtual reality pointing task we systematically explored how three performance metrics driving the feedback affected players when rewarding short completion times, straight movements, or high peak speed. across different points in time - continuously, at end-of-action, or at end-of-task. On average the dynamic feedback helped people point more straight and faster, while for others it had small or opposite effect. The study quantitatively compared dynamic feedback across three forms with the metrics driving the form as the intended locus of quantitative comparison. Our work improves game designers basis for crafting dynamic feedback by helping them know when to employ feedback schemes that align with desirable game performance objectives.

</details>


### [175] [Continuous Behavioral Synthesis for Adaptive Health Dashboards: An LLM-Mediated Architecture Integrating Explicit Preference, Spatial Reorganization, and Attention Allocation Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26937)
*Tiziano Santilli,Mina Alipour,Mahyar T. Moghaddam*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用大语言模型作为行为合成引擎的技术架构，通过整合三种稀疏异质的用户信号（显式微反馈、空间优先级、注意力投入），采用分层提示工程方法实时生成自适应仪表盘界面，并在个人健康监测领域验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自适应用户界面工程依赖基于规则的系统和需要大量历史数据的机器学习方法，难以从稀疏、异质的用户信号中实现即时个性化适应。

Method: 提出一种将LLM作为行为合成引擎的架构，集成三种行为通道（界面元素的显式微反馈、通过拖拽交互推断的空间优先级、悬停驻留时间衡量的注意力投入），采用分层提示构建方法分离时间上下文、行为信号提取、显式偏好实施和用户画像合成，结合手动指定的行为解释与LLM中介的合成。

Result: 在个人健康监测领域实例化系统，管理14种健康指标和7种可视化模态，通过多场景适应行为分析、配置驱动初始化、多信号行为适应及代表性截图展示了系统有效性和界面输出。

Conclusion: 该架构能够在无需大量历史数据的情况下，从稀疏多模态用户信号中实现即时界面个性化适应，解决了将低层交互模式转化为高层设计决策的技术挑战。

Abstract: The engineering of adaptive user interfaces has traditionally relied on either rule-based systems encoding designer intuitions about user needs or machine learning approaches requiring substantial historical data before achieving effective personalization. We present a technical architecture that leverages Large Language Models as behavioral synthesis engines to enable immediate adaptation from sparse, heterogeneous user signals. Our system integrates three distinct behavioral channels, i) explicit micro-feedback on individual interface elements, ii) spatial priority inferred from manual widget reorganization through drag-and-drop interaction, iii) and attentional investment measured through dwell time during hover events, within a structured prompt engineering framework that continuously regenerates dashboard layouts while maintaining explanatory coherence. The architecture addresses the technical challenge of translating low-level interaction patterns into high-level design decisions through a layered prompt construction methodology that separates temporal context determination, behavioral signal extraction, explicit preference enforcement, and user profile synthesis. The approach combines manually specified behavioral interpretations and temporal heuristics with LLM-mediated synthesis, enabling the reconciliation of multiple simultaneous signals that would be difficult to encode through explicit rules alone. We demonstrate the system through an instantiation in the personal health monitoring domain, including an analytical evaluation of adaptation behavior across multiple scenarios and a working implementation managing fourteen distinct health metrics across seven widget visualization modalities. The evaluation compares profile-driven initialization, multi-signal behavioral adaptation, and presents the resulting interfaces through representative post-adaptation screenshots.

</details>


### [176] [What Holds Back Brain-Computer Interfaces? Uncovering Challenges and Opportunities in BCI-controlled Games for Cerebral Palsy Rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26951)
*Bastian Ilsø Hougaard,Kirstine Schultz Dalgaard,Kirstine Johanne Stougaard Klebæk,Hendrik Knoche,Mads Jochumsen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文初步探讨了脑机接口（BCI）在脑瘫（CP）康复中的设计约束，通过临床治疗师和十名CP患者的体验反馈，发现BCI游戏中的间歇性辅助虽能缓解单调性，但会引发对自主控制感的怀疑；治疗师认为BCI康复可作为传统训练的补充，帮助从游戏化练习过渡到自主训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCI为CP康复提供了有前景的途径，但基于BCI的CP康复的设计约束尚不明确，特别是CP患者如何通过BCI体验控制感以及如何体验计算机介导的游戏辅助。

Method: 基于临床治疗师的见解和十名CP患者使用BCI游戏原型的体验报告，进行初步的临床和用户视角分析。

Result: BCI游戏中的间歇性辅助（sporadic help）缓解了单调性，但也引发了患者对自主控制感（agency）的怀疑；治疗师认为BCI康复可作为传统训练的补充，帮助从游戏化练习过渡到自主训练。

Conclusion: 概述了BCI训练用于CP康复的关键挑战和机遇，为未来设计和研究提供指导。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) offer promising avenues for cerebral palsy (CP) rehabilitation at home and in the clinic, using games that promote engagement and sustained training effort. Nonetheless, the design constraints of BCI-based CP rehabilitation remain unclear, especially how individuals with CP experience a sense of control through BCI, and how they experience computer-mediated game assistance. To address this gap, we present preliminary clinical and user perspectives on BCI-based CP rehabilitation, drawing on in-clinic insights from a CP therapist and experiential accounts from ten individuals with CP engaging with BCI game prototypes. Sporadic help in BCI games eased monotony, but also fostered doubts regarding agency. The therapist saw BCI rehabilitation as complementary to traditional training, facilitating the transition from playful exercises to autonomous, self-managed training. We outline key challenges and opportunities to inform and empower further design and research of BCI training for CP.

</details>


### [177] [Floor Raiser or Ceiling Limiter? Differential Storytelling Outcomes with a Child-Centric GenAI System Across Individual Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27067)
*Min Fan,Wanqing Ma,Xinyue Cui,Xiaolu Dai,Shengyu Huang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过40名7-12岁儿童的实验，发现GenAI辅助讲故事能缩小故事质量差距83.5%（提升低端、约束高端），但仅改善创意和丰富性，不改善连贯性和叙事结构；年龄影响关键词选择偏好，图像再生与结构质量正相关但受基线影响；提出机制依赖性脚手架设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索GenAI是否均等地惠及所有儿童，以及其作用机制和边界条件。

Method: 混合方法被试内实验（N=40），对比GenAI辅助与传统故事板条件，分析故事质量、关键词选择、图像再生行为。

Result: GenAI条件缩小质量差距83.5%（天花板效应与地板效应并存），仅提升创意和丰富性；年龄影响关键词语义距离偏好；图像再生与结构质量正相关但受基线控制减弱。

Conclusion: 提出机制依赖性脚手架（mechanism-contingent scaffolding）作为适应性GenAI讲故事系统的设计原则。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) holds promise for democratizing creative literacy, yet whether it benefits all children equally remains unclear. Using a child-centric GenAI storytelling system for children aged 7-12, we conducted a mixed-methods within-subjects experiment (N = 40, Grades 2-6) comparing GenAI-assisted and traditional storyboard conditions. Three findings emerged. First, the GenAI-assisted condition was associated with a floor-raising convergence pattern, with the quality gap narrowing by 83.5%, driven by lower-end support and upper-end constraint mechanisms. This convergence was dimension-selective, improving creativity and richness while leaving coherence and narrative structure tied to baseline performance.
