Second, younger children more often selected semantically distant keywords while older children preferred semantically closer ones, although engagement orientation varied across individuals regardless of age. Third, image regeneration was positively associated with structural quality dimensions, though this association was attenuated after baseline control. We propose mechanism-contingent scaffolding as a design principle for adaptive GenAI storytelling systems serving diverse children.

</details>


### [178] [Urban Context and Travel Experience Events: An Exploratory Comparison of Two German Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27077)
*Marie Güntert,Esther Bosch*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过移动应用程序追踪德国汉堡（城市）和图特林根（乡村）共91名参与者的日常出行，运用多层回归分析发现：图特林根地区公共交通运力充足对出行体验正向影响最大，而准点性差和个人不适感负向影响最大；汉堡地区准点性差和负面信息事件影响最大。这些发现可为改善地方公共交通提供决策依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究影响公共交通出行体验的事件因素，以及这些事件如何影响出行行为和体验，旨在为改善地方公共交通提供决策支持。

Method: 采用移动应用程序追踪21名（图特林根）和70名（汉堡）参与者的日常出行，收集实时出行体验评价和情境数据，运用多层回归分析评估准点性、运力、信息提供等事件对途中体验的影响。

Result: 图特林根：运力充足正效应最强，准点性差和个人不适感负效应最强；汉堡：准点性差和负面信息事件影响最大。

Conclusion: 城乡地区影响公共交通体验的关键事件不同，这些差异为制定针对性改善措施提供了依据。

Abstract: The presented study investigates events influencing public transportation experience in both urban (Hamburg) and rural (Tuttlingen) areas in Germany, with the aim of identifying events that affect travel experience and as a result travel behavior. Using a mobile application, 21 participants in Tuttlingen and 70 participants in Hamburg tracked everyday trips, providing real-time evaluations of travel experiences along with situational data. Multi-level regression analyses were applied to assess the impact of events such as punctuality, capacity offer, information about public transportation and others on the ontrip experience. Results indicate that a sufficient public transportation capacity offer has the strongest positive effect in Tuttlingen, whereas a lack of punctuality and low personal well-being have the strongest negative effects. In Hamburg, a lack of punctuality and a negative information event have the largest impacts. These identified effects provide a foundation for decision-making and measures to improve local public transportation.

</details>


### [179] [Behind the Mask: A Taxonomic Analysis of Activities in Online Social Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27111)
*Debora F De Souza,Gabriela Beltrao,Berta Chulvi,Sergio Dantonio,Mehmet Gokay Ozerim,Javier Torregrosa,Adrian Giron,Angel Panizo,Pablo Miralles Gonzalez,Helena Liz,Javier Huertas Tato,Sonia Sousa,Alejandro Martin,Monika Maciuliene,David Camacho*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过对恶意行为者特征和活动的分析，结合专家协作与文献综述，设计了一个分类体系，用于区分在线社交网络上传播虚假信息的恶意行为者，并通过反移民话语案例研究验证了其应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线社交网络中虚假信息的传播日益严重，需要在人机交互等多个学科领域识别恶意行为者，但目前缺乏系统化的分类体系来区分这些行为者的特征、活动和策略。

Method: 通过与领域专家协作及学术文献综述，设计了一个分类体系，并在反移民话语社交媒体渠道的案例研究中应用该分类体系进行分析。

Result: 成功设计并应用了一个分类体系，能够区分在线社交网络中恶意行为者的特征、活动和策略，并通过反移民话语案例研究验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 该分类体系有助于研究人员和从业者在设计网络系统时负责任地识别和应对虚假信息传播中的恶意行为者。

Abstract: The broadcast of disinformation in online social networks (OSN) is a growing concern examined across several disciplines, including human-computer interaction (HCI). The pervasive issue has been prompting novel approaches to identify the malicious actors behind the dissemination of deceptive and fabricated content. Analyzing the characteristics and activities of these actors, we designed a taxonomy informed by collaboration with subject matter experts (SMEs) and a review of the academic literature. Our study explores how to distinguish the characteristics, activities, and strategies of malicious actors on OSN and examines how they contribute to the spread of disinformation. We describe the design process and the application of the taxonomy in a case study analyzing anti-migration discourse in social media channels, and reflect on its potential to aid researchers and practitioners in the responsible design of network systems.

</details>


### [180] ["Everyone Says Them": Deception Typologies, Probabilistic Trust, and Grassroots Safety Knowledge Among Gay Dating App Users in China](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27284)
*Yibo Meng,Lyumanshan Ye,Yingfangzhong Sun,Bingyi Liu,Huidi Lu,Xiaolan Ding*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过22位中国男同性恋用户的半结构化访谈，揭示了约会应用中欺骗行为的类型学（包括关系、情感、财务和商业欺骗），用户发展出的分层概率性验证策略，以及作为协作实践的风险识别机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管同性恋约会应用已成为性少数男性寻求关系和社群的关键平台，但其中存在的欺骗性互动在HCI和CSCW研究中尚待深入探索。

Method: 对22位来自Blued、Aloha、Fanka和Soul等平台的中国男同性恋用户进行半结构化访谈。

Result: 识别出超越个人资料虚假陈述的欺骗类型学（关系、情感、财务、商业欺骗）；发现用户通过长期平台使用形成分层、概率性的验证策略（信任评估是多信号、暂定性的过程）；揭示风险识别是社群内经验流通、策略抽象化和规则编码化的协作实践。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解性少数群体在数字平台上的信任建构和风险应对提供了新视角，拓展了欺骗行为研究从个人层面到社群协作层面的理解。

Abstract: Gay dating applications have become critical platforms for sexual minority men to seek relationships and community, yet they also expose users to deceptive interactions that remain underexplored in HCI and CSCW research. This study examines how gay male users in China experience, identify, and respond to deception on dating applications. Through semi-structured interviews with 22 participants across platforms including Blued, Aloha, Fanka, and Soul, we make three contributions. First, we identify a typology of deceptive practices extending beyond profile misrepresentation to encompass relational, emotional, financial, and commercial forms of deception. Second, we document the layered, probabilistic verification strategies users develop through long-term platform use, showing that trust assessment operates as a multi-signal, provisional process rather than a binary judgment. Third, we demonstrate that risk recognition is a collaborative practice shaped by the circulation of experience, the abstraction of recurrent tactics, and the codification of shared rules within the community.

</details>


### [181] [Reading the Same Data Differently: Interpretive Labor Across System Boundaries in Electronic Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27301)
*Yibo Meng,Bingyi Liu,Zhiqi Gao,Shuai Ma,Hongyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文基于对中国社区矫正系统中26名受监督者和12名管理者的半结构化访谈，揭示了电子监控作为一种双端感知系统存在"解释性错位"：受监督者只能从结果推断系统逻辑，而管理者则依靠情境知识、专业经验和制度程序来重建行为，双方因数据、情境和推理过程的不对称获取而产生结构性分歧，进而影响行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将电子监控视为刑事司法工具或合规机制，但对其感知数据如何在日常实践中产生意义（即双端如何理解同一数据流）的认知不足。

Method: 对中国社区矫正系统中26名受监督者和12名管理者进行半结构化访谈，分析双方对同一电子监控数据流的理解差异。

Result: 发现"解释性错位"现象：受监督者在数据解释可见性有限的情况下，通过结果推断系统逻辑；管理者则利用情境知识、专业经验和制度程序从模糊痕迹中重建行为。这种错位通过试探、策略性适应、过度合规、脱离和抗争等行为表现出来。

Conclusion: 贡献了一个CSCW视角下连续感知作为分布式解释工作的理论框架，并识别出使数据到决策的过程在系统双端更可读、可争议和可问责的设计机会。

Abstract: Electronic monitoring (EM) systems are increasingly used in community corrections to enforce spatial, temporal, and behavioral rules through continuous sensing. While prior work has examined EM as a criminal justice tool or as a mechanism for compliance, less is known about how sensed data become meaningful in everyday practice. This poster examines EM as a dual-sided sensing system in which supervised individuals and authorities reason about the same data stream from different positions. Based on semi-structured interviews with 26 supervised individuals and 12 authorities in China's community corrections system, we show that supervised individuals infer system logic from outcomes with limited visibility into how data are interpreted, while authorities reconstruct behavior from ambiguous traces using contextual knowledge, professional experience, and institutional procedures. We call this structural divergence interpretive misalignment. It emerges from asymmetric access to data, context, and reasoning processes, and it shapes behavior through probing, strategic adaptation, over-compliance, disengagement, and contestation. We contribute a CSCW account of continuous sensing as distributed interpretive work and identify design opportunities for making data-to-decision processes more legible, contestable, and accountable across system sides.

</details>


### [182] [AI Healthcare Chatbots as Information Infrastructure: A Large-Scale Study of User-Reported Breakdowns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27302)
*Muhammad Hassan,Ramazan Yener,Ece Gumusel,Masooda Bashir*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究分析了来自59款AI医疗健康聊天机器人的超过15,000条用户评论，通过主题建模和解释性分析识别出三大类系统性故障：访问障碍与服务不可靠、用户体验与交互质量问题、账单与客户支持问题。研究发现隐私和安全问题与最负面的用户体验相关，并将AI医疗健康聊天机器人视为信息基础设施，揭示了访问、可用性和信任方面的失败如何影响用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI医疗健康聊天机器人已被广泛用于健康信息查询和自我管理，但其实际表现和对用户的影响尚缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 对59款AI医疗健康聊天机器人应用的超过15,000条用户评论进行主题建模和解释性分析。

Result: 识别出三类反复出现的故障：访问障碍与服务不可靠、用户体验与交互质量、账单与客户支持问题；隐私和安全问题与最负面体验高度关联。

Conclusion: 将AI医疗健康聊天机器人视为信息基础设施，指出其在访问、可用性和信任方面的失败对用户产生影响，并为设计师、政策制定者和信息专业人员提供了改进数字健康系统的可操作建议。

Abstract: AI healthcare chatbots are increasingly used to support health information seeking and self-management, yet their performance and impact on users remains to be studied. This study examines over 15,000 user reviews from 59 AI healthcare chatbot apps to explore how these systems function in everyday informational and emotional contexts. Topic modeling and interpretive analysis identify three recurring breakdowns: access barriers and service unreliability, user experience and interaction quality, and billing and customer support issues. Privacy and security concerns are associated with the most negative experiences. By framing AI healthcare chatbots as information infrastructures, our findings highlight how failures in access, usability, and trust affect users, offering actionable insights for designers, policymakers, and information professionals aiming to improve digital health systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Orchestrating Black-Box Schema Converters: An Empirical Study of Automated, Quality-Ranked Conversion Across Heterogeneous Schema Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26180)
*Felix Neubauer,Giridhar Chinnikkaramadom Govindan,Jürgen Pleiss,Benjamin Uekermann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模式转换编排方法，将异构的模式语言转换器视为有向图中的黑盒节点，通过路径发现、执行、排序和全步骤溯源来自动化转换过程。实现为开源工具Schema Conversion Orchestrator，在60个真实世界转换任务中成功处理43个，并定位了当前转换器生态的具体不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统常需在多种模式语言（如JSON Schema、XSD、SHACL）中维护同一数据模型，而现有转换器难以寻找、质量参差不齐且常有信息丢失，导致模型演化时的维护负担沉重。

Method: 将模式语言建模为图中的节点，转换器（作为黑盒）建模为有向边，转换任务即为寻找并执行路径。系统支持路径发现、执行、基于质量排序，并提供每一步的完整溯源信息，失败时自动尝试替代路径。实现为开源编排器并集成到MetaConfigurator中。

Result: 在60个真实世界转换任务中，编排方法成功为43个任务提供了可用结果；剩余17个失败任务精确揭示了当前转换器生态的具体缺失环节。

Conclusion: 编排方法能有效利用现有异构转换器实现自动化、可复现、质量可排序的模式转换，并对工具构建者和转换质量度量提出了启示。

Abstract: Modern software systems routinely need the same data model in several schema languages: a model may exist as JSON Schema for a web API, as XSD for data exchange, and as SHACL for a knowledge graph. Keeping these representations consistent as the model evolves is a recurring construction and maintenance burden, because converters between schema languages are hard to find, scattered across ecosystems, of uneven quality, and frequently lossy. We study, empirically, to what extent such imperfect, heterogeneous converters can be orchestrated into automated, reproducible, and quality-ranked conversions, and where the current converter landscape reaches its limits. Our approach models schema languages as nodes and converters, treated as black boxes, as directed edges, so that conversions become paths that are discovered, executed, ranked, and reported with full per-step provenance, with failures handled by trying alternatives. We realize it as the open-source Schema Conversion Orchestrator, integrate it into MetaConfigurator, and evaluate it on 60 conversion tasks built from real-world schemas across five schema languages, using agent-assisted, human-reviewed quality annotations. Orchestration surfaces a usable result for 43 of 60 tasks; the remaining failures localize concrete gaps in the converter landscape. We discuss implications for tool builders and for measuring conversion quality.

</details>


### [184] [Augmentation with Dilution: A Large-Scale Empirical Study of Human Contributor Ecosystems After AI Coding Agent Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26289)
*Weixing Zhang,Bowen Jiang,Anne Koziolek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过11,097个GitHub仓库的因果分析发现，AI编码代理并未显著改变人类贡献者绝对数量，但降低了人类贡献者密度（-1.9%），新人相对参与份额下降3.7个百分点，而审阅深度增加5.3%，呈现"增强但稀释"的模式：AI不替代人类，但系统性重塑了开源生态的参与结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将人类贡献者视为静态背景，缺乏以人类贡献者生态为因变量、考察AI编码代理采纳后人类参与者数量、组成和行为变化的大规模实证研究。

Method: 采用交错双重差分设计（Staggered DiD），使用Sun和Abraham估计量，对2023年1月至2026年5月间11,097个GitHub仓库进行因果推断。

Result: 1) AI代理采纳后人类贡献者绝对数量无显著变化（ATT=0.014, p=0.224）；2) 人类贡献者密度显著下降（ATT=-0.019, p=0.002）；3) 新人相对参与份额显著下降3.7个百分点（ATT=-0.037, p<0.001），效应即时出现且持续稳定；4) 审阅深度显著增加5.3%（ATT=+0.0168, p<0.001）；5) 效应随项目规模、编程语言和项目成熟度系统变化。

Conclusion: AI代理并非在取代人类贡献者，而是在系统性地重塑开源生态系统的参与结构，呈现"增强与稀释并存"的模式——AI将负担从代码生产阶段转移到审阅阶段。

Abstract: AI coding agents are penetrating open-source software development at an unprecedented pace, yet existing research predominantly treats human contributors as a static backdrop rather than as the subject of inquiry. This paper presents the first large-scale empirical study that takes the human contributor ecosystem as its dependent variable, examining how the number, composition, and behavior of human participants change following AI coding agent adoption in open-source projects. Using a staggered difference-in-differences design on a dataset of 11,097 GitHub repositories spanning January 2023 to May 2026, we provide causal evidence via the Sun and Abraham estimator. Our results show that AI agent adoption does not significantly change the absolute number of human contributors (ATT = 0.014, p = 0.224), but significantly reduces human contributor density (ATT = -0.019, p = 0.002), indicating that the relative share of human participation declines as AI-generated pull requests accumulate. The relative participation share of newcomers declines significantly by 3.7 percentage points (ATT = -0.037, p < 0.001), with the effect emerging immediately after adoption and remaining stable throughout the observation window. Review depth increases significantly by 5.3% (ATT = +0.0168, p < 0.001), indicating that AI agents shift burden from the code production stage to the review stage. Moderator analysis reveals that these effects vary systematically with project size, programming language, and project maturity. Together, these findings present a pattern of augmentation with dilution: AI agents are not displacing human contributors, but are systematically reshaping the participation structure of open-source ecosystems.

</details>


### [185] [Towards Safety-Aware Mutation Testing for Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26456)
*Donghwan Shin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向自动驾驶系统（ADS）的安全感知变异测试（SAMT）范式，通过系统性地在ADS模块间消息中注入时间受限故障来模拟真实交互失败，并基于STPA等安全工程框架衍生变异规则，以评估测试充分性、指导场景生成和系统修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ADS测试缺乏系统性停止标准，传统覆盖度量无法捕获导致大多数事故的组件交互故障，而直接移植代码级或深度学习模型级变异测试不足以应对ADS的模块间交互问题。

Method: 提出SAMT范式：(1) 在ADS模块间消息中注入时间受限的故障，模拟交互失败；(2) 从STPA等安全工程框架自上而下衍生变异生成规则，确保变异体代表真实危害。

Result: SAMT为评估测试充分性提供了严格机制，支持自动化场景生成，并指导ADS修复。同时指出了关键开放性挑战。

Conclusion: SAMT通过将系统思维嵌入变异测试管道，实现了从组件级故障注入到交互级安全感知测试的范式转变，有望更有效地保障ADS安全性。

Abstract: Simulation-based testing is essential for ensuring the safety of Autonomous Driving Systems (ADS), yet the community lacks a systematic criterion for determining when we can safely stop additional test scenario generation. Existing coverage metrics typically focus on individual component reliability or treat the ADS as a black box, failing to capture certain component interactions that cause most ADS accidents. While traditional mutation testing provides a falsifiable measure of test adequacy, directly porting code- and deep learning model-level mutations to the corresponding modules of ADS is insufficient.
  In this vision paper, we propose a paradigm shift toward Safety-Aware Mutation Testing (SAMT). Unlike traditional mutation testing, which creates mutants (i.e., faulty versions of the software under test) by injecting artificial faults into individual components, SAMT systematically injects temporally bounded faults into the messages exchanged between ADS modules to simulate realistic interaction failures. To ensure these mutants represent genuine hazards, we propose deriving mutant generation rules directly from top-down safety engineering frameworks, such as System-Theoretic Process Analysis (STPA). By embedding systems thinking into the mutation testing pipeline, SAMT provides a rigorous mechanism for evaluating test adequacy, enabling automated scenario generation, and guiding ADS repair. We also outline critical open challenges.

</details>


### [186] [An Empirical Study of LLM-Generated Specifications for VeriFast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26490)
*Wen Fan,Minh Tran,Sanya Dod,Xin Hu,Marilyn Rego,Danning Xie,Jenna DiVincenzo,Lin Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了大型语言模型（LLM）为基于分离逻辑（SL）的验证器VeriFast生成规范的能力，测试了303个C函数、8种提示方法、10个LLM和3种输入类型。结果显示LLM在保持功能行为方面表现良好（超过91%），但验证成功率仅为31.4%，其中94%的错误源于LLM在SL验证器领域知识上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态验证工具（尤其是基于分离逻辑的验证器）在工业规模软件验证中效果显著，但需要大量人工编写复杂的辅助规范。现有研究将LLM应用于生成代码、测试和证明，但主要针对非SL验证器。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统评估LLM为SL验证器生成规范的可行性与局限性。

Method: 本文以303个C函数为测试基准，采用两阶段实验设计：（1）探索8种提示方法、10种LLM和3种输入类型（源代码、形式化契约等）；（2）通过定量分析（功能行为保持率、验证成功率）和定性分析（错误分类）评估LLM生成的代码和规范质量。

Result: LLM在源代码和规范中保持功能行为的比例均超过91%，但验证成功率仅为31.4%。Gemini 2.5 Pro和提供形式化契约能获得更高的验证成功率。94%的错误源于LLM在VeriFast等SL验证器领域知识上的错误。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在保持代码功能行为方面表现良好，但在生成可验证的SL规范方面仍有显著不足，主要受限于SL验证器的领域知识。这些发现为优化LLM生成SL验证器规范提供了指导方向。

Abstract: Static verification tools can assure industrial scale software, but require significant human labor to write specifications. This is particularly true of static verifiers based on separation logic (SL verifiers), which excel at verifying heapmanipulating programs, but require many complex auxiliary specifications to reason about heap structure. Recent work applies large language models (LLMs) to generate code, tests, and proofs, including specifications for verifiers, but mostly targeting non-SL verifiers. To address this gap, this paper thoroughly evaluates how well LLMs perform when prompted to generate specifications for verifying 303 C functions with the SL verifier VeriFast. We explored eight prompting approaches, ten LLMs, and three input types in two stages. Quantitative and qualitative analyses are used to assess the LLM-generated code and specifications for functional behavior, verifiability and errors. The results show that LLMs preserve functional behavior in source code and specifications (both over 91%), but achieve modest verification success (31.4%). Using Gemini 2.5 Pro and providing formal contracts lead to higher success rates in our setting. Moreover, most errors (94%) come from LLMs' mistakes in the domainspecific knowledge of SL verifiers such as VeriFast. These findings provide guidance for optimizing LLM-generated specifications for SL verifiers.

</details>


### [187] [Evaluation-Strategy Gap in Fault Diagnosis of Deep Learning Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26492)
*Sigma Jahan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 深度学习程序训练失败的诊断技术在不同程序间的性能差距达0.190（平衡准确率），主要源于程序级特征结构差异；曲率特征对未见过程序的故障检测有效，而优化器和激活特征仅对训练中见过的程序有帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有故障诊断技术通常仅使用程序内交叉验证评估，但实际部署中会遇到未见过的程序，评估方式与真实场景不匹配，需要量化性能差距并分析其原因。

Method: 构建包含38个真实DL程序、5,542个故障注入训练轨迹的DynFault语料库，比较程序内评估与留出完整程序评估的性能差异，并分析曲率特征和优化器特征在不同程序上的表现。

Result: 程序内评估与留出程序评估之间存在0.190的平衡准确率差距；曲率特征对未见过程序的稳定性检测仍有效，优化器和激活特征仅对训练中见过的程序有帮助。

Conclusion: 现有DL故障诊断技术存在显著的跨程序泛化差距，特征来源（程序级结构）是主要原因；曲率特征更具泛化能力，而优化器特征存在过拟合风险。

Abstract: Deep Learning (DL) programs can fail during training for many reasons, and diagnosing the cause is a costly and time-consuming maintenance task. Techniques for diagnosing such failures are commonly assessed using within-program cross-validation, which may be inadequate for deployment settings involving previously unseen programs. It is therefore necessary to assess how performance differs across these settings and to identify the causes of any performance gap in established fault diagnosis techniques for DL. We investigate this gap using DynFault, a corpus of 5,542 fault-injected training traces from 38 real-world DL programs. We found a gap of 0.190 in balanced accuracy for existing fault diagnosis techniques between within-program evaluation and holding out whole programs. We also found the gap comes from program-level structure in the features, which led us to examine two runtime feature sets, curvature features and optimizer features, and their behavior on unseen programs. We found that curvature features are useful for instability detection on unseen programs, while optimizer and activation features help only on programs seen during training.

</details>


### [188] [Same Scrutiny, More Time: Eye Tracking Insights into Reviewing LLM-Labelled Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26505)
*Ranim Khojah,Francisco Gomes de Oliveira Neto,Mazen Mohamad,Julian Frattini,Philipp Leitner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过Wizard-of-Oz实验发现，明确标注为LLM生成的代码虽然未改变代码审查的彻底性，但会显著影响开发者的注意力分配（更多注视时间）和审查策略（侧重逻辑正确性、利用提示指导审查），揭示了开发者意图与实际行为之间的差距，为LLM工具设计和AI政策制定提供了启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码存在错误和幻觉风险，需要仔细审查，但开发者实际如何审查标注为LLM生成的代码尚不清楚，存在信任与实际行为之间的未知差距。

Method: 采用Wizard-of-Oz实验，通过眼动追踪和退出访谈收集行为数据和参与者反馈，结合贝叶斯数据分析和定性分析进行混合方法研究。

Result: 标注LLM生成未改变审查彻底性，但开发者对LLM标注代码的注视时间更长；从业者调整了审查策略，专注于逻辑正确性等特定标准，并利用提示作为审查指导。

Conclusion: 开发者意图与实际审查行为之间存在差距，建议软件公司重新审视AI政策（特别是在LLM辅助开发方面），LLM工具设计应考虑代码标注策略并将提示作为软件制品纳入审查流程。

Abstract: Modern software development increasingly involves the use of large language models (LLMs) to generate code. Despite their rapid advancement, LLMs remain prone to errors and hallucinations, emphasizing the importance of careful code inspection. However, in practice, developers' trust in LLM-generated code and their willingness to review it thoroughly may differ from these recommendations. How developers actually behave when reviewing LLM-generated code remains largely unexplored. In this study, we conduct a Wizard-of-Oz experiment to examine how software engineers behave when code is explicitly labeled as LLM-generated during a code review task. We collect both behavioral data and participant feedback through eye-tracking and exit interviews. Combining Bayesian data analysis with qualitative analysis, we found that while the thoroughness of code review did not change for participants, they spent more time fixating on LLM-labelled code, indicating that the label itself influences attention. Practitioners also adapted their review strategy for LLM-labelled code by assessing the code based on specific criteria (e.g., logical correctness), or using the prompt to guide their review. These findings inform LLM-based tool design on labelling while incorporating the prompt as a software artifact. Our study reveals a gap between reviewers' intentions and actual reviewing behaviour, highlighting the need for software companies to revisit their AI policies (particularly regarding LLM-assisted development) to better support developers in reviewing LLM-generated code.

</details>


### [189] [ConcoLixir: Reactive LLM Discovery Oracles for Python Concolic Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26545)
*Dong Chen,Chih-Duo Hong,Fang Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ConcoLixir是一个用于Python混合执行测试的反应式LLM扩展，通过LLM作为发现预言机（而非替代求解器）来生成初始种子、在求解器失败后提出具体输入、以及在覆盖率停滞时定位未覆盖代码。实验表明，在合成、真实世界和库目标上，平均行覆盖率分别提升8.6、15.1和17.0个百分点，完整评估仅需1.63美元API费用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python混合执行测试面临三个固有局限：库调用导致符号变量退化为具体值；正则表达式、校验和、解析器等语义操作难以求解；探索在已覆盖路径上停滞。现有方法无法有效应对这些问题。

Method: 提出ConcoLixir，在传统混合执行框架上集成LLM作为发现预言机。LLM不替代求解器或正确性预言机，而是：（1）生成初始种子；（2）求解器失败时提出具体输入；（3）覆盖率停滞时定位未覆盖代码。每个候选输入均经过混合执行，仅依赖观察到的覆盖率和路径约束指导后续探索。

Result: 在合成、真实世界和库目标上，ConcoLixir相比无LLM预言机的基线混合测试器，平均行覆盖率分别提升8.6、15.1和17.0个百分点。改进在语义障碍和库边界附近最为显著。完整评估的API费用仅1.63美元。

Conclusion: 有界LLM发现能力可以补充符号推理而无需替代之，证明了LLM在混合执行测试中作为高效、低成本辅助工具的有效性。

Abstract: Concolic testing combines concrete execution with symbolic constraint solving, but Python programs expose recurring limits. Library calls can cause symbolic variables to downgrade to concrete values. Regular expressions, checksums, parsers, and other semantic operations can be hard to solve, and exploration can plateau on already covered paths. We present ConcoLixir, a reactive LLM extension for Python concolic execution. The LLM acts as a discovery oracle, not a replacement for the solver or a correctness oracle. It generates initial seeds, proposes concrete inputs after solver failures, and targets uncovered code when coverage stalls. Each candidate is executed concolically, and only observed coverage and collected path constraints guide later exploration. Across synthetic, real-world, and library targets, ConcoLixir improves mean line coverage over the baseline concolic tester without an LLM oracle by 8.6, 15.1, and 17.0 percentage points. The gains are strongest near semantic barriers and library boundaries, and the full evaluation costs \$1.63 in API charges. These results show that bounded LLM discovery can complement symbolic reasoning without replacing it.

</details>


### [190] [Beyond Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27258)
*Daniel Jackson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文质疑面向对象编程的核心原则——将系统功能按问题域个体进行划分，认为这一原则导致了功能碎片化和混淆问题，并提出放弃面向对象，改用将问题域个体与功能模块解耦的替代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出面向对象编程的核心理念（按问题域个体划分功能）已主导软件开发五十余年，但这一划分既不自然也不直接，后续软件工程方法试图缓解的功能碎片化和混淆问题实际上是该原则的必然结果，因此需要从根本上重新审视这一原则。

Method: 通过分析面向对象原则的内在缺陷，论证其必然导致功能碎片化和混淆，并提出一种替代方案：将问题域中的个体与划分功能的模块进行解耦，即放弃面向对象范式。

Result: 本文为理论性论证，未提供实验性结果，但指出面向对象原则是功能碎片化和混淆问题的根源，替代方案（解耦个体与模块）能更直接地解决这些问题。

Conclusion: 解决面向对象带来的问题的更简单途径是回归本源，放弃面向对象，采用将问题域个体与功能模块解耦的替代方法。

Abstract: A core principle of object orientation -- that the functionality of a system can be partitioned amongst objects that correspond to individuals in the problem domain -- has influenced how software has been specified, designed and implemented for more than fifty years. Later developments in software engineering sought to build on this principle. But in fact this partitioning is neither natural nor straightforward, and the problems that these later developments sought to mitigate -- the fragmentation and conflation of functionality -- were often, in fact, the inevitable consequences of this founding principle. An easier path to addressing these problems therefore starts by going back, abandoning object orientation, and replacing it with an alternative approach that decouples the individuals of the problem domain from the modules that partition functionality.

</details>


### [191] [Quantum Mutant Equivalence via Transpilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26604)
*José Campos,Andriy Miranskyy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于编译器的量子变异测试等价变异体检测方法（TBE），通过对原始和变异电路进行相同配置的转译并比较OpenQASM代码来识别等价变异体。在348,299个存活变异体中，TBE成功识别了29,536个等价变异体（32.1%），达到了100%的精确率和82%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件中变异测试面临等价变异体问题，现有研究生成了大量量子电路变异体，但无法区分存活变异体是由于测试不足还是语义等价所致，亟需一种针对量子程序的等价变异体检测方法。

Method: 提出Transpiler-Based Equivalence (TBE)方法，通过对原始和变异电路在相同配置下进行转译，然后比较两者生成的OpenQASM代码是否一致，若一致则判定为等价变异体。

Result: 在348,299个存活变异体中，92,011个为等价变异体；TBE成功识别了其中29,536个（32.1%），达到100%精确率和82%准确率。

Conclusion: TBE是一种轻量级且高效的量子程序等价变异体识别方法，能够有效降低变异测试中的噪声，提升测试评估的准确性。

Abstract: Mutation testing evaluates test suite quality by introducing artificial faults (mutants) and checking whether tests detect (kill) them. A central challenge is the equivalent mutant problem: some mutants are syntactically different from the original program but semantically identical to it and therefore cannot be killed by any test. If left unidentified, such mutants waste testing effort and distort mutation scores. In quantum software, mutation testing is increasingly used, but the equivalent mutant problem remains unsolved. A recent study generated more than 700,000 quantum circuit mutants and found that roughly half survived the available tests, making it unclear whether these survivors reflect weak tests or semantic equivalence. We propose Transpiler-Based Equivalence (TBE), a lightweight approach that identifies equivalent quantum mutants by transpiling original and mutated circuits under the same configuration and comparing their resulting OpenQASM code. We evaluate TBE on 348,299 surviving mutants, 92,011 of which are equivalent; TBE identifies 29,536 of them (32.1%) as equivalent while achieving 100% precision and 82% accuracy.

</details>


### [192] [Knowledge-Based Pull Requests: A Trusted Workflow for Agent-Mediated Knowledge Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26721)
*Xinyu Zhang,Weiwei Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出知识型拉取请求（KPR）工作流，将外部贡献者的代码、测试和交互记录视为知识源而非默认合并候选，通过项目自有可信代理在项目环境内重新生成代码，从而将“知识是否应进入项目”与“特定实现是否应合并”两个决策解耦，以降低跨信任边界的软件协作成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拉取请求将“知识采纳”与“实现合并”两个决策捆绑在一起，导致跨信任边界（如开源、企业、供应商、承包商等场景）协作时，理解意图、协商范围和管理长期责任的成本高昂；而AI编码代理使得代码本身变得廉价，上述非编码成本反而成为瓶颈。

Method: 提出KPR工作流：外部协作代理将本地代码、测试和清理后的交互轨迹作为知识源，经人工确认后提炼为知识包（含设计备忘录、风险清单、测试计划、实现简报等）；项目自有内部可信代理在接收项目的环境、工程规范、测试和安全策略下重新生成候选代码。贡献了候选工件模式、成本核算视图、协作网关架构、基于七个已合并公开PR的受控模拟实验。

Result: 受控模拟实验表明，KPR知识包可从真实PR材料中实例化，并在描述消融、差异消融和合成投毒补丁条件下经受压力测试。

Conclusion: KPR是一个可实证测试的工作流，其价值取决于可审计的提取、转换和项目端代码再生过程能否降低理解与重做高上下文外部变更的成本。

Abstract: AI coding agents are changing the bottleneck in software collaboration: code is increasingly cheap, while understanding intent, negotiating scope, and governing long-term project responsibility remain costly. This paper proposes \emph{Knowledge-Based Pull Requests} (KPR), a trusted workflow for agent-mediated software collaboration across trust boundaries, including open source, enterprise, vendor, contractor, and customer-driven settings. In KPR, an external collaborator's local code, tests, and cleaned agent interaction trace are treated as knowledge sources rather than as the default merge candidate. Agents distill these sources into a human-confirmed knowledge package and render it into reviewer-facing forms such as design memos, risk checklists, test plans, or implementation briefs. A project-owned inner trusted coding agent then regenerates candidate code inside the receiving project's environment under repository context, engineering conventions, tests, and security policy. KPR therefore separates two decisions that traditional pull requests often collapse: whether the knowledge should enter the project, and whether a particular implementation should be merged. We contribute the KPR workflow, a candidate artifact schema, a cost-accounting view, a collaboration gateway architecture, a minimal controlled simulation pilot over seven merged public pull requests, and an evaluation agenda. The pilot shows that KPR packages can be instantiated from real PR material and stress-tested under description ablation, diff ablation, and synthetic poisoned-patch conditions. We position KPR as an empirically testable workflow: its value depends on whether auditable extraction, transformation, and project-side regeneration reduce the cost of understanding and reworking high-context external changes.

</details>


### [193] [A Deterministic Control Plane for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26924)
*Padmaraj Madatha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对10,008个GitHub仓库的实证研究发现，LLM编码智能体的配置文件（规则文件、智能体定义、IDE markdown）存在大量未管理的克隆传播（10.1%为SHA-256完全重复，75.5%跨组织边界）、极少被修订（58%仅一次提交）且几乎不声明权限边界（<1%）。为此提出Rel(AI)Build——一个位于编码框架之上的确定性控制平面，通过内容寻址、HMAC锁文件、哈希链审计日志、分层权限、攻击词块列表、相位状态机及跨IDE编译等手段管理智能体定义供应链，并通过注入测试验证了各机制的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体拥有广泛的文件和shell访问权限，但其配置文件（规则文件、智能体定义、IDE特定markdown）这一引导层目前缺乏管理，存在安全风险和治理盲区。

Method: 1) 对10,008个公开GitHub仓库(n=6,145个智能体配置文件)进行大规模实证研究，分析配置传播、修订频率和权限声明模式。2) 提出Rel(AI)Build确定性控制平面，包括SHA-256内容寻址、HMAC锁文件、哈希链审计日志、分层权限、攻击词块列表、相位状态机、跨7种IDE目标编译、Jaccard相似度检测prompt漂移等机制。3) 通过注入违反进行一致性测试验证。

Result: 10.1%的跟踪路径为独立仓库间的SHA-256精确重复，其中75.5%的克隆对跨组织边界；58%的配置文件仅有一次提交，年龄归一化后提交率仅为0.4 vs 0.6次/月（对比CI/CD工作流）；<1%的智能体配置文件声明了权限边界（对比CI/CD工作流的33%）。Rel(AI)Build的每个机制均通过注入测试验证了其声称的不变性。

Conclusion: 智能体配置层存在严重的管理缺陷（未声明的克隆传播、低修订率、权限缺失），需要确定性的、工具无关的治理方案，而非依赖进一步的LLM编排。Rel(AI)Build提供了可行的解决路径，但开发者实际使用效果尚待后续研究。

Abstract: LLM coding harnesses grant agents broad file and shell access, yet the configuration layer that steers them -- rules files, agent definitions, IDE-specific markdown -- is largely unmanaged. A prevalence study of 10,008 public GitHub repositories (n=6,145 agent config files) finds that agent configurations propagate as undeclared shared components: 10.1% of tracked paths are SHA-256 exact duplicates across independent repositories (fork-adjusted, threshold-independent), with 75.5% of clone pairs crossing organisational boundaries. Two further patterns are indicative: configurations are rarely revised (58% single-commit; 0.4 vs 0.6 commits/month age-normalised against CI/CD workflows), and rarely declare permission boundaries (<1% of agent configs vs 33% of Actions workflows, n=31 true positives).
  We propose a deterministic control plane above the harness that maps one-to-one to these gaps. Rel(AI)Build treats agent definitions as a managed supply chain (SHA-256 content addressing, HMAC-stamped lockfiles, hash-chained audit logs); enforces tiered permissions and attack-derived blocklists before LLM invocation; gates feature work through a phase state machine with requirement-to-file-to-test traceability; compiles a single canonical definition to seven IDE targets; and detects prompt drift via Jaccard similarity. Conformance tests on injected violations confirm each mechanism enforces its stated invariant; developer outcomes remain future work. Governance of this layer must be deterministic and tool-agnostic -- not delegated to further LLM orchestration.

</details>


### [194] [Are LLMs Ready for Anti-Pattern Detection in Microservice Architectures?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26927)
*Marco De Luca,Domenico Amalfitano,Porfirio Tramontana,Anna Rita Fasolino*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）通过基于提示的分析流程检测微服务架构中的架构反模式（APs）的能力，并与静态分析工具MARS进行了比较。结果表明LLMs在局部、异构或语义丰富的反模式检测上具有竞争力，但在需要显式结构或跨服务依赖证据的反模式上仍不如静态分析，可作为互补工具而非替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统容易反复出现阻碍可维护性、可演化性和运行质量的架构反模式。现有的反模式检测方法主要依赖静态分析和手工规则，这些方法虽然有效，但通常依赖特定工具、受限于显式编码的检测逻辑，且难以适应异构代码库。作者希望探索LLMs是否能通过提示分析流程来支持微服务架构中的反模式检测。

Method: 设计基于提示的分析流程，利用静态仓库工件进行检测。在包含16种架构反模式的微服务仓库基准上评估了三种通用LLMs，并与最先进的静态分析工具MARS进行了对比，采用统一的精确率和召回率评估协议。

Result: LLMs在多个反模式检测上取得了有竞争力的性能，特别是在证据是局部、异构或语义丰富的情况下。但在需要显式结构或跨服务依赖证据的反模式上表现有限，此时静态分析更为可靠。

Conclusion: LLMs目前尚不能替代传统分析工具，但已可作为微服务系统架构评估的有前景的互补辅助手段。

Abstract: Microservice systems are prone to recurrent architectural anti-patterns (APs) that hinder maintainability, evolvability, and operational quality. Most existing AP detection approaches rely on static analysis and handcrafted rules, which can be effective but are often tool-dependent, limited to explicitly encoded detection logic, and difficult to adapt to heterogeneous repositories. In this paper, we investigate whether large language models (LLMs) are ready to support architectural anti-pattern detection in microservice architectures through a prompt-based analysis pipeline over static repository artifacts. We evaluate three general-purpose LLMs on a curated benchmark of microservice repositories annotated with 16 architectural anti-patterns, and compare their performance against the state-of-the-art static-analysis tool MARS using a uniform evaluation protocol based on precision and recall. Our results show that LLMs can provide useful support for anti-pattern detection, achieving competitive performance on several anti-patterns, especially when the relevant evidence is local, heterogeneous, or semantically rich. At the same time, they exhibit clear limitations on anti-patterns that require explicit structural or cross-service dependency evidence, where static analysis remains more reliable. These findings suggest that LLMs are not yet a replacement for traditional analyzers, but already represent a promising complementary aid for architectural assessment in microservice systems.

</details>


### [195] [CLIR: Liveness-Driven and Structure-Aware Fuzzing for the Cranelift Compiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26977)
*Shangtong Cao,Tianlei Song,Qiuping Yi,Tianyu Chen,Guoai Xu,Ningyu He,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CLIR是一个针对Cranelift编译器的差分测试框架，通过语法保留的分层生成策略、活跃度引导的指令优化机制和诊断驱动的跨架构适配方案，相比现有基线方法检测到8倍至24倍以上的独特bug，并在72小时内发现24个跨架构bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cranelift作为新兴的编译器后端（被Rust采纳为实验性后端），其测试面临三大挑战：SSA形式的严格约束下构造有效IR、生成足够计算密度的序列以压力测试后端组件、以及跨异构架构实现广泛后端覆盖与高效根因分析的平衡。现有编译器测试方法无法充分应对这些挑战。

Method: 提出CLIR差分测试框架，包含三个核心机制：(1) 语法保留的分层生成策略，保证SSA合法性；(2) 活跃度引导的指令优化机制，最大化计算密度；(3) 诊断驱动的跨架构适配方案，实现异构后端的高效根因分析。

Result: CLIR相比cranelift-fuzzgen、wasm-smith、WASMaker分别检测到8倍、24倍和8倍更多的独特bug（RustSmith未发现任何bug）。在72小时内发现24个跨所有目标架构的bug，其中21个已确认，9个已修复。

Conclusion: CLIR框架通过针对性解决Cranelift测试中的三大挑战，显著提升了编译器后端测试的有效性和效率，证明了其方法在异构架构编译器测试中的优越性。

Abstract: Modern compilers are complex software systems that must correctly translate high-level programming languages into machine code across multiple architectures. Cranelift, a fast and modern compiler backend originally developed for WebAssembly and recently adopted as an experimental backend for Rust, has gained increasing importance due to its superior compilation speed compared to LLVM and comprehensive multi-architecture support, including x86-64, AArch64, s390x, and RISCV64. However, despite decades of development in compiler testing, testing Cranelift still presents unique challenges, including (1) constructing valid IR under the strict enforcement of SSA form, (2) generating sequences with sufficient computational density to stress backend components, and (3) balancing broad backend coverage with efficient root cause analysis across heterogeneous architectures.
  To address these challenges, we propose CLIR, a differential testing framework that integrates a syntax-preserving hierarchical generation strategy to guarantee SSA validity, a liveness-guided instruction refinement mechanism to maximize computational density, and a diagnosis-guided cross-architecture adaptation scheme to facilitate efficient root cause analysis across heterogeneous backends. Our comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that CLIR significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art baselines, detecting 8x, 24x, and 8x more unique bugs than cranelift-fuzzgen, wasm-smith, and WASMaker, respectively, while RustSmith uncovered no bugs. Consequently, within 72 hours of testing, CLIR discovered 24 bugs spanning all target architectures, with 21 confirmed and 9 fixed.

</details>


### [196] [To Run or Not to Run: Analyzing the Cost-Effectiveness of Code Execution in LLM-Based Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26978)
*Zhihao Lin,Junhua Zhu,Mingyi Zhou,Xin Wang,Zhensu Sun,Renyu Yang,David Lo,Li Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过两阶段实证研究（分析7,745条SWE-bench智能体轨迹 + 3000次端到端修复实验），发现LLM程序修复智能体中“生成-运行-修订”的执行范式虽被广泛使用（平均每任务8.8次测试运行），但其实际收益高度集中而非均匀分布。限制执行对修复成功率影响极小（商业智能体仅下降1.25个百分点且不显著），却能大幅节省token和时间成本。结论：执行应被视为有明确成本-效益权衡的资源，而非默认能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于执行反馈的LLM程序修复智能体（“生成-运行-修订”范式）已成为主流，但执行行为的高成本（时间和算力）及其实际收益尚未被系统性地量化研究，导致该范式可能被过度使用而缺乏成本效益意识。

Method: 两阶段实证研究：阶段一，分析SWE-bench排行榜上7,745条智能体轨迹，规模化刻画执行行为特征；阶段二，在200个SWE-bench实例上评估三种智能体（Claude Code、Codex、OpenCode）共3000次端到端修复尝试，对比四种执行范式（从完全禁止到无限制）的性能与成本差异。

Result: (1) 执行普遍使用，平均每任务8.8次测试运行，不同智能体/模型差异大（2-19次），后期执行成功率显著高于早期；(2) 限制执行对成功率影响极小：商业智能体禁止执行后成功率仅降1.25个百分点且统计不显著，但显著节省token和时间成本；(3) 执行收益高度集中，而非均匀分布。

Conclusion: 当前智能体不加区分地使用执行，在收益甚微的实例上也付出了执行成本。执行应被视为一种有明确成本-效益权衡的资源，而非默认启用的能力，建议按需调用而非无差别使用。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for program repair are increasingly built on a "generate-run-revise" paradigm, iteratively executing tests to evaluate and refine patches. This execution-based approach has become standard practice in state-of-the-art systems. However, executions can be time-consuming and expensive, yet their impact on these agents remains underexplored. In this paper, we conduct a two-stage empirical study over execution behavior in LLM-based program repair. To characterize execution behavior at scale, we first analyze 7,745 agent traces from SWE-bench leaderboard submissions. Second, we evaluate 3,000 end-to-end repair attempts across 200 SWE-bench instances and three agents (Claude Code, Codex, and the open-source OpenCode) under four execution paradigms, which allows for a fine-grained comparison of performance and cost. Our analysis reveals three key observations: (1) Code execution is used across all agents and models analyzed, with an average of 8.8 test runs per task. Execution behavior varies substantially across agents and models, with frequency ranging from 2 to 19 per task, and late-stage executions consistently achieve higher success rates than early-stage ones. (2) Execution restrictions have little effect on repair success: on commercial agents with SOTA models the resolve-rate gap between Prohibited and Unrestricted is only 1.25 percentage points and not statistically significant, while Prohibited saves substantial token and wall-clock cost. (3) Execution benefit is concentrated rather than uniform. These patterns suggest that current agents apply execution indiscriminately, paying its cost on instances where it provides little benefit. Execution, therefore, should be treated as a resource with an explicit cost-benefit tradeoff, not a default capability.

</details>


### [197] [How Much Static Structure Do Code Agents Need? A Study of Deterministic Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26979)
*Zhihao Lin,Mingyi Zhou,Yizhuo Yang,Li Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究轻量级静态分析能否为LLM代码智能体提供确定性锚点：通过将调用图、继承层次等结构事实以纯文本注释形式注入，约束概率探索，使导航更可预测。实验发现该方法使函数定位提升2.2pp、交互轮次减少1.6轮、运行方差减半，但存在规模敏感性和边际收益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的代码智能体依赖关键词搜索进行仓库导航，忽略了调用图、继承层次和配置依赖等结构关系，导致导航随机性强、难以复现。研究者希望探究轻量级静态分析能否提供确定性锚点，使导航更可控和可预测。

Method: 以OpenAI Codex为强基线，系统性地注入不同粒度的结构注释（调用图、继承拓扑等），测量其对定位准确率、轨迹行为和运行间稳定性的影响。对比不同粒度（轻量/密集）和方向性（正向/反向/双向）的注释效果。

Result: (1)轻量调用/继承拓扑使函数级定位提升2.2个百分点(Func@5)，交互轮次减少1.6轮；(2)最优粒度和方向性取决于仓库特征，密集语义呈现边际收益递减，枢纽型项目受益于仅反向链接；(3)标签将链接跟随率从0.15-0.18提升至0.21-0.24，运行方差减半，单次运行可靠性提升3.4个百分点(Pass@1)，代价是约10%额外的输入token。

Conclusion: 确定性锚定效应表明：静态结构并非让智能体变得更"聪明"，而是让导航变得更有纪律和可复现。建议中型项目默认采用轻量拓扑，大型仓库裁剪前向边，隐式依赖场景使用密集标签。

Abstract: LLM-based code agents navigate repositories through keyword search but miss the structural relationships, such as call graphs, inheritance hierarchies, and configuration dependencies, that define how software actually works. This makes agent navigation stochastic and difficult to reproduce across runs. We investigate whether lightweight static analysis can provide deterministic anchors for these agents: stable structural facts injected as plain-text comments that constrain probabilistic exploration and make navigation more predictable. Starting from a strong baseline, Codex from OpenAI, we systematically inject varying granularities of structural annotations and measure their effects on localization, trajectory behavior, and run-to-run stability. Our study identifies what we call the deterministic anchoring effect: static structure helps less by making agents "smarter" and more by making their navigation disciplined and reproducible. Three observations support this finding: (1) Anchoring works: lightweight call/inheritance topology improves function-level localization (+2.2pp Func@5) and shortens trajectories (-1.6 interaction rounds); (2) Anchoring is scale-sensitive: the optimal granularity and directionality depend on repository characteristics, where denser semantics show diminishing returns and hub-heavy projects benefit from inverse-only links that expose "who-calls-me" without forward edges; (3) Anchoring stabilizes: tags raise link-following rate from 0.15-0.18 to 0.21-0.24, roughly halve run-to-run variance, and improve single-run reliability (Pass@1 +3.4 pp) on medium-scale repositories, at the cost of roughly 10% more input tokens. These observations suggest practical guidelines: default to lightweight topology on medium projects, prune forward edges in large repositories, and reserve dense tags for implicit-dependency cases.

</details>


### [198] [Cleaning Logs for Downstream Tasks (Registered Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27000)
*Zahra G. Yazdi,Van-Hoang Le,Nyyti Saarimäki,Donghwan Shin,Domenico Bianculli,Lionel Briand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LogPurifier，一种基于日志模板间依赖关系的任务无关日志清洗方法，旨在识别并移除与系统执行行为无关的游离消息，提升下游任务（模型推理和异常检测）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日志中常包含大量冗余和无关信息（游离消息），这些信息会负面影响下游分析任务（如模型推理和异常检测）的性能，因此需要有效的日志清洗方法。

Method: 提出LogPurifier，一种基于日志消息模板间依赖关系的任务无关日志清洗方法，通过识别和移除游离消息来净化日志数据。

Result: 计划通过受控实验设计进行实证评估，衡量LogPurifier对模型推理和异常检测两项下游任务的有效性和效率的影响。

Conclusion: LogPurifier旨在通过任务无关的方式清洗日志，有望提升下游分析任务的性能，但具体效果有待实验验证。

Abstract: Background: Software systems generate logs during execution to record critical events and runtime information for troubleshooting and monitoring. However, in practice, logs often contain significant amounts of redundant and irrelevant information, which can negatively impact the performance of downstream analysis tasks, such as model inference and anomaly detection. Objective: The objective of this study is to clean log data by identifying and removing free-standing messages -- messages that are not relevant to the execution behaviors of interest and are interleaved with messages capturing the system's functional behavior. Method: To address this objective, we propose LogPurifier, a task-agnostic log-cleaning approach based on dependency relationships between log message templates. The paper presents a plan for an empirical evaluation using a controlled experimental design to assess the impact of LogPurifier on the effectiveness and efficiency of two downstream tasks: model inference and anomaly detection.

</details>


### [199] [The Spec Growth Engine: Spec-Anchored, Code-Coupled, Drift-Enforced Architecture for AI-Assisted Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27045)
*Hartwig Grabowski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Spec Growth Engine框架，通过机器可读的规范图、Spine上下文汇编器、垂直切片增长协议和漂移门机制，解决AI编码代理中的上下文爆炸和规范-代码静默漂移两大结构性故障模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理虽能大幅加速实现速度，但引入两个现有规范驱动方法未完全解决的结构性故障模式：(1) 上下文爆炸——代理需同时推理整个仓库，随上下文窗口填满而降低输出质量；(2) 静默规范-代码漂移——代码演化但规范未更新，差异变得不可见直到修复成本高昂。

Method: 提出Spec Growth Engine框架，包含四个核心组件：(1) 机器可读的规范图，节点携带明确的契约/设计分离；(2) Spine上下文汇编器，将代理上下文限定到所有权路径；(3) 垂直切片增长协议，强制执行最难优先排序；(4) 漂移门，将规范-代码差异作为阻塞合并条件。该设计综合了Parnas信息隐藏、C4、ADR、Walking Skeleton、Reflexion Models、Fitness Functions等成熟软件工程原则。

Result: 该框架以轻量级、代码耦合、机器可执行的整体方式运作，避免了RUP或MDA等重量级框架的开销。

Conclusion: 通过将经过验证的软件工程原则综合为轻量级、机器可执行的框架，同时解决AI编码代理的两个核心故障模式，无需重量级方法的负担。

Abstract: AI coding agents dramatically accelerate implementation speed but introduce two structural failure modes that existing spec-driven approaches do not fully solve: (1) context explosion -- the agent must reason over an entire repository at once, degrading output quality as the context window fills; and (2) silent spec-code drift -- code evolves, the specification does not, and the divergence becomes invisible until it is costly to repair.
  We present the Spec Growth Engine, a lightweight framework that addresses both failure modes through a machine-readable spec graph whose nodes carry explicit contract/design separation, a Spine context assembler that scopes agent context to an ownership path, a vertical-slice growth protocol that enforces hardest-first ordering, and a drift gate that makes spec-code divergence a blocking merge condition.
  The design synthesises well-established software engineering principles (Parnas information hiding, C4, ADRs, Walking Skeleton, Reflexion Models, Fitness Functions) into a lean, code-coupled, machine-enforced whole -- without the overhead of heavy-weight frameworks such as RUP or MDA.

</details>


### [200] [ATGBuilder: Feature-Assisted Graph Learning for Activity Transition Graph Construction with Seed Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27080)
*Chenhui Cui,Zixiang Xian,Danyu Li,Tao Li,Rubing Huang,Dave Towey,Shikai Guo,Jiakun Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ATGBuilder是一种基于特征辅助图学习的方法，利用大语言模型（LLM）总结UI活动元数据，并显式建模widget触发信息作为边属性，通过辅助的widget属性重构目标进行模型训练，从而在种子监督下高质量构建Activity Transition Graphs（ATGs），显著优于现有方法，并能改进自动化GUI探索工具的导航效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ATG构建方法存在静态分析可能遗漏可行转换或提取不可行转换、动态探索生成不完整转换的问题，而基于学习的方法对activity-layout和widget-trigger信息的利用仍然有限。

Method: ATGBuilder使用LLM将UI活动元数据总结为紧凑的文本功能摘要，显式建模widget-trigger信息作为边属性，并在训练中引入辅助的widget属性重构目标。

Result: 在多个基准测试上显著优于最先进方法，并通过改进自动化GUI探索工具的导航效果进一步验证了有效性。

Conclusion: ATGBuilder通过融合LLM语义摘要和widget触发信息的显式建模，有效提升了种子监督下ATG构建的质量，并可应用于增强GUI自动化探索。

Abstract: Android applications are organized around activities that provide visual Graphical User Interface (GUI) containers that host the UI and handle user interaction events. Activity Transition Graphs (ATGs) have been widely used to model apps' GUI navigation. However, the construction of high-quality ATGs is challenging: ATGs based on static analysis may miss acceptable transitions and may extract infeasible ones; while dynamically explored ATGs can yield incomplete transitions. Recent learning-based approaches can treat ATG construction as a seed-supervised link-prediction task. However, the use of activity-layout and widget-trigger information for ATG construction remains limited. We propose ATGBuilder, a feature-assisted graph-learning approach for seed-supervised ATG construction. ATGBuilder uses a Large Language Model (LLM) to summarize UI activity metadata from layouts into compact textual functionality summaries. ATGBuilder explicitly models widget-trigger information into the edge attribute: It then uses an auxiliary widget-attribute reconstruction objective on this information during model training. ATGBuilder's performance was evaluated across a series of ablations on the frontmatter corpus, and an experiment on benchmark using manually-checked ground-truth ATGs. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that ATGBuilder significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. We further demonstrate its effectiveness by improving automated GUI exploration tools through better navigation guidance.

</details>


### [201] [On the Reproducibility of Quantum Software Defect Datasets: A Case Study of Bugs4Q](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27124)
*Haruto Ohto,Yuta Ishimoto,Shinsuke Matsumoto,Shinji Kusumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对量子软件缺陷数据集Bugs4Q的可复现性进行了复现实验。结果表明，随着Qiskit版本从v0.20.1升级到v2.3.1，Bugs4Q的可复现性从62.2%骤降至16.2%，其中93.6%的失败源于依赖问题。与先前关于经典缺陷数据集的研究不同，Bugs4Q中的多数复现失败无法仅通过调整依赖版本解决，而需要修改源代码（如迁移导入路径和API调用）。研究者据此构建了Bugs4Q-Robust补丁版本，将可复现性提升至78.4%，强调了量子软件生态快速演进下数据集持续维护的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 朱等人已证明经典缺陷数据集（如Defects4J）会随时间推移出现复现失败问题，但尚不清楚这些发现是否适用于量子软件缺陷数据集。本研究旨在通过复现实验来填补这一认知空白。

Method: 采用复现研究方法，对包含37个Bugs4Q工件、涉及21个核心库版本的77,700次量子程序执行进行分析，并对复现失败进行根因人工检查。

Result: Bugs4Q可复现性从Qiskit v0.20.1的62.2%降至v2.3.1的16.2%；93.6%复现失败为依赖相关问题；与经典数据集不同，多数失败需要源代码级修改而非仅调整依赖版本；补丁版本Bugs4Q-Robust将可复现性提升至78.4%。

Conclusion: 量子软件缺陷数据集面临比经典软件更严峻的可复现性挑战——由于量子生态快速演进，依赖问题和API变更导致数据集需持续维护；仅调整依赖版本不足以恢复可复现性，需进行源代码级迁移。

Abstract: The reproducibility of software defect datasets is essential for obtaining reliable and comparable research results. Zhu et al. have shown that defect datasets such as Defects4J suffer from reproduction failures (i.e., reported bugs become non-reproducible) as time passes since their creation. However, it remains unclear whether these findings generalize to quantum software defect datasets. We therefore conduct a replication study of the prior work using Bugs4Q, a widely used dataset of real-world bugs in quantum programs. Our analysis includes 77,700 quantum program executions of 37 Bugs4Q artifacts across 21 core-library versions. The experimental results showed that the reproducibility of Bugs4Q dropped from 62.2% on Qiskit v0.20.1 to 16.2% on v2.3.1, the latest version as of April 1, 2026. A manual inspection of the root causes further indicated that 93.6% of the failures were dependency-related. While these findings are consistent with those of the prior work, we also observed differences. In particular, most reproduction failures in Bugs4Q cannot be resolved merely by adjusting dependency versions; instead, they require source-code modifications such as migrating import paths and API invocations. Based on this observation, we curated Bugs4Q-Robust, a patched version of Bugs4Q to restore reproducibility. Bugs4Q-Robust increases reproducibility from 16.2% to 78.4% on Qiskit v2.3.1. Our findings highlight the importance of continuous dataset maintenance in the rapidly evolving quantum software ecosystem.

</details>


### [202] [Smaller Models, Unexpected Costs: Trade-offs in LLM Quantization for Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27205)
*Fernando Vallecillos-Ruiz,Giordano d'Aloisio,Max Hort,Luca Traini,Antinisca Di Marco,Leon Moonen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对LLM量化在自动程序修复（APR）任务上进行了实证评估，分析了13种量化配置在6个代表性LLM上的效果。结果表明：量化模型与原始模型修复的问题集重叠度低但修复数量相当；量化虽能将内存占用降低85%，但会导致推理时间和能耗增加；48%的配置在帕累托分析中被严格支配；量化效果高度依赖模型架构和任务复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管量化技术被广泛用于减少LLM的内存占用，但其对复杂软件工程任务（如APR）的影响往往仅通过基准分数概括，掩盖了模型行为变化和非功能性开销。因此，需要系统评估量化在APR任务上的实际影响，包括有效性、内存、能耗等多维度权衡。

Method: 选取6个代表性LLM，配置13种量化方案（涵盖不同位宽、量化方法和目标组件如权重和KV缓存），在HumanEval-Java和Defects4J两个APR基准上进行评估，进行帕累托前沿分析。

Result: 量化模型与原始模型修复的问题集重叠度低但数量相当；内存占用降低高达85%，但推理时间和能耗增加；48%的配置在帕累托分析中被严格支配；量化效果对模型架构和任务复杂度敏感。

Conclusion: 不存在普适最优的量化方法，量化在有效性、内存和能耗之间的权衡高度依赖于底层模型架构和任务复杂性，需根据具体场景选择量化配置。

Abstract: Language Models (LLMs) are powerful toolsand have been increasingly adopted for complex software engineering tasks. As the number of parameters increases, results can often be improved, but this also imposes substantialmemory requirements. While quantization effectively reduces thememory footprint, its overall impact is often summarized onlyby benchmark scores, which mask changes in model behaviorand non-functional overheads. In this work, we conduct anempirical evaluation of LLM quantization using AutomatedProgram Repair (APR), a complex task in software engineering.We analyze 13 quantization configurations spanning differentbit-widths, methods, and target components (weights and KVcache) across six representative LLMs, evaluated on two APRbenchmarks (HumanEval-Java and Defects4J).
  Our findings reveal that base and quantized models can provide different sets of repaired problems with little overlap, whileretaining a comparable number of repaired problems. Althoughquantization successfully reduces memory footprints by up to85%, it increases both inference time and energy consumption,which we attribute to suboptimal hardware utilization. OurPareto trade-off analysis shows that 48% of the configurationsevaluated are strictly dominated by alternatives. Rather thanidentifying a superior quantization method, our findings highlightthat the trade-offs between effectiveness, memory footprint,and energy efficiency are sensitive to the underlying modelarchitecture and the complexity of the task.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Random Walk on Bézier Curves for Global Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26714)
*Jinpeng Wang,Xingguo Xu,Yujing Sun,Jiguang Yu,Kaichen Ouyang,Yuansheng Gao*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出贝塞尔游走进化（BWE），一种基于几何驱动的优化框架，通过自适应贝塞尔曲线建模和距离感知随机游走机制，在元启发式优化中实现探索与利用的动态平衡。在CEC2017和CEC2022的41个基准函数（10-100维）及5个工程约束问题上的实验表明，BWE相比7种经典和6种最新优化器（包括L-SHADE和CMA-ES）具有优异的整体性能和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元启发式优化中探索与利用的平衡问题一直是个核心挑战，现有方法缺乏可解释性和自适应性。

Method: 提出BWE框架，将进化搜索重构为决策空间中的自适应轨迹构建，核心机制包括：1) 贝塞尔曲线建模；2) 距离感知随机游走机制；3) 自适应调整曲线阶数以平滑过渡从全局探索到局部利用。

Result: 在CEC2017和CEC2022的41个基准函数（10-100维）上，BWE相比7种经典和6种最新优化器（包括L-SHADE和CMA-ES）取得优异的整体性能和可扩展性；在5个工程约束设计问题上的额外评估验证了其实用性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: BWE作为一种几何驱动的可解释性优化框架，通过自适应贝塞尔曲线机制有效平衡探索与利用，在基准测试和工程应用中均表现出色，为替代传统仿生元启发式算法提供了新思路。

Abstract: Balancing exploration and exploitation remains a central challenge in metaheuristic optimization. To address this issue, this paper proposes Bézier Walk Evolution (BWE), a geometry-driven optimization framework that reformulates evolutionary search as adaptive trajectory construction in the decision space. BWE integrates Bézier curve modeling with a distance-aware random walk mechanism to generate topology-guided search trajectories. By adaptively varying the curve order during evolution, the proposed method enables a smooth transition from diversified global exploration to refined local exploitation. Higher-order Bézier curves leverage multiple population-derived control points to enhance search diversity, while lower-order curves generate near-linear trajectories to improve convergence efficiency. This adaptive geometric search mechanism provides an interpretable alternative to conventional nature-inspired designs. Extensive experiments on 41 benchmark functions from the CEC2017 and CEC2022 suites, spanning dimensions from 10 to 100, show that BWE achieves strong overall performance and favorable scalability compared with 7 classical and 6 state-of-the-art optimizers, including L-SHADE and CMA-ES. Additional evaluations on five constrained engineering design problems further demonstrate the practical applicability and robustness of BWE.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [MIRAGE: Protecting against Malicious Image Editing via False Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26199)
*Anshul Nasery,Ramnath Kumar,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Sewoong Oh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MIRAGE方法，通过在图像中添加对抗性扰动，触发商业图像编辑系统的预生成安全审核机制，从而阻止未经授权的AI图像编辑，成功率超过88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像免疫方法需要访问模型权重和编辑提示，无法有效防御GPT-Image、Gemini Flash Image等商业闭源图像编辑系统。

Method: MIRAGE：利用对抗性扰动，在开源嵌入和审核模型的表示空间中将图像对齐到违反政策的分类概念，触发商业编辑系统预生成安全审核的自动拒绝机制，无需特定提示。

Result: 针对多个闭源图像编辑API，成功率超过88%。

Conclusion: MIRAGE是一种简单、提示无关且有效的图像免疫方法，为保护个人图像免受未经授权的AI编辑提供了实用方案。

Abstract: The proliferation of AI-powered image editing systems raises serious concerns because it allows personal images to be arbitrarily manipulated at scale, with minimal effort, and a lower barrier to entry. Prior work on image immunization adds imperceptible perturbations to an image to protect against unauthorized manipulations. However, these methods usually require access to the model weights and the image manipulating prompt. This significantly limits their use, especially against powerful commercial image-editors such as GPT-Image, Gemini Flash Image (Nano Banana), and Grok Imagine. To address this, we take a system-level view of the problem and identify a previously unexplored attack surface common to all major commercial image editing systems: pre-generation safety moderation.Rather than disrupting the generative model itself, we propose to immunize images by causing these moderation classifiers to flag images as policy-violating, triggering an automatic refusal regardless of the editing prompt. We operationalize this by adding adversarial perturbations to align our image to policy-violating concepts in the representation space of an ensemble of open-source embedding and moderation models. We call our method MIRAGE, which stands for Moderation Induced Resistance Against Generative Editing. We evaluate MIRAGE against multiple closed-source image editing APIs and demonstrate success rates of more than 88%. Our approach is simple, prompt-agnostic, and effective, offering a practical path towards protecting personal images from unauthorized AI-powered editing.

</details>


### [205] [Data Facts: A Metadata Schema for Structured Data Exchange in the NANDini Multi-Agent Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26211)
*Jin Gao,Maria Gorskikh,Pradyumna Chari,Brittany Box,Mukul Kemla,Pratik Behera,Abhishek Mehta,Ramesh Raskar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Data Facts轻量级JSON元数据模式，通过单一`data_facts_url`指针连接代理发现与数据访问，并实现三层安全管道（JWT认证、能力网关授权、A2A凭证委托）。实验表明：数据感知代理准确率100%（vs 无数据35.2%），TTL将陈旧数据错误从37.6%降至8.8%，校验和实现100% corruption检测，安全管道零泄露阻挡全部46次伪造攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协议（NANDA、A2A、MCP）仅解决代理身份与通信，缺乏结构化数据交换机制；现有企业框架（IDS-RAM、Gaia-X、Ocean Protocol）依赖人工治理，无法适配自主实时代理交互。

Method: 设计Data Facts JSON元数据模式，在Agent Facts注册记录中添加`data_facts_url`指针，编码数据集身份、访问层级（公开/半私有/私有）、端点、TTL和SHA-256校验和；对私有/半私有数据实施JWT认证+能力网关授权+A2A凭证委托三层安全管道。

Result: 840次决策评估中数据感知代理准确率100% vs 无数据35.2%（p<0.001）；TTL将陈旧数据错误从37.6%降至8.8%；校验和100%检测所有注入率下的数据损坏；安全管道零泄露阻挡46次伪造攻击。

Conclusion: Data Facts填补了自主代理生态系统中从代理发现到数据访问的关键鸿沟，为NANDini愿景提供了可行基础设施。

Abstract: NANDini (Networked Agents Natural Distillation of Interconnected Nodal Intelligence) envisions an automated ecosystem where intelligent agents independently create, process, and exchange data to drive decisions at scale. Realizing this vision requires infrastructure beyond agent discovery and communication: agents must be able to advertise, evaluate, and verify the datasets they hold.
  Current protocols, including NANDA for federated registry and A2A and MCP for inter-agent messaging, address identity and communication but provide no mechanism for structured data exchange. Existing enterprise data-sharing frameworks, such as IDS-RAM, Gaia-X, and Ocean Protocol, assume human-in-the-loop governance that is incompatible with autonomous, real-time agent interactions.
  We introduce Data Facts, a core NANDini concept: a lightweight JSON metadata schema that bridges agent discovery and data access through a single pointer, `data_facts_url`, added to an existing Agent Facts registry record. The linked document encodes dataset identity, access tier, whether public, semi-private, or private, endpoint, a time-to-live for freshness validation, and a SHA-256 integrity checksum.
  For private and semi-private data, we implement a three-layer security pipeline: JWT authentication, capability-scoped gateway authorization, and an A2A credential delegation protocol. Across 840 decision-making evaluations, data-informed agents achieve 100% accuracy versus 35.2% without data access (p < 0.001); TTL enforcement reduces stale-data errors from 37.6% to 8.8%; checksum verification achieves 100% corruption detection at all injection rates; and the security pipeline blocks all 46 forgery attempts with zero data leakage.

</details>


### [206] [CyberChainBench: Can AI Agents Secure Smart Contracts Against Real-World On-Chain Vulnerabilities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26216)
*Jintao Huang,Fengqing Jiang,Radha Poovendran,Zhiqiang Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CyberChainBench是一个评估LLM代理在智能合约安全方面能力的基准，涵盖漏洞检测、漏洞利用生成和补丁合成三个互补任务。基于DeFiHackLabs的541个真实漏洞事件，通过在历史分叉上进行端到端链上评估，最佳配置在检测上得37.5%，利用上得43.7%，但补丁生成仅得23.4%，顶级代理(Codex+GPT-5.5)在200个案例中实现了5740万美元的利用利润，每案例成本2.39美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能合约安全评估大多局限于静态分析或单一任务，缺乏综合评估LLM代理在完整安全生命周期（检测、利用、修复）中能力的基准，且缺少基于真实历史漏洞事件和链上状态验证的端到端评估框架。

Method: 从DeFiHackLabs收集541个真实漏洞事件（覆盖9条EVM链），构建三个互补任务：漏洞检测、漏洞利用生成和补丁合成。利用Harbor创建隔离评估环境，代理通过与历史区块链状态交互、读取代码、追踪交易并在主网分叉上验证利用来完成任务。每个案例锚定特定区块，包含漏洞类型、定位和攻击者利润的结构化真实标签。漏洞利用按经济影响评分，补丁通过重放历史攻击和合法交易进行验证。定义了五种漏洞类型分类体系。

Result: 最佳配置在检测任务上得分37.5%，利用任务上得分43.7%，补丁合成仅得分23.4%，呈现明显的难度梯度。顶级代理(Codex+GPT-5.5)在200个利用案例中实现了5740万美元的总利用利润，每案例成本2.39美元。

Conclusion: 当前LLM代理在智能合约安全方面能力有限，尤其补丁合成是最具挑战的任务，表明需要更先进的自动化安全修复技术。CyberChainBench为评估和推动智能合约安全自动化提供了全面的基准。

Abstract: We present CyberChainBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLM-based agents on smart contract security across three complementary tasks: vulnerability detection, exploit generation, and patch synthesis. Built from 541 real-world exploit incidents from DeFiHackLabs spanning 9 EVM chains, the benchmark provides end-to-end on-chain evaluation where agents interact with historical blockchain state through isolated evaluation environments orchestrated by Harbor, using tools to read code, trace transactions, and validate exploits on mainnet forks. Each case is anchored to a specific block and includes structured ground truth covering vulnerability type, localization, and attacker profit. Exploits are graded by economic impact on historical forks; patches are validated by replaying historical attacks and legitimate transactions as fail-to-pass test oracles on a proxy-upgradeable subset. We define a five-type vulnerability taxonomy and evaluate multiple agent--model configurations. Results reveal a clear difficulty gradient: the best configuration scores 37.5% on detection, 43.7% on exploitation, but only 23.4% on patching, with the top agent (Codex with GPT-5.5) realizing \$57.4M in total exploit profit across the 200-case exploit set at a cost of $2.39 per case.

</details>


### [207] [Expecting (Targeted Ads)? Network Analysis of User Health Data Leakage in Fertility Tracking Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26276)
*Yeeun Jo,Shahanaasree Sivakumar,Mahnoor Jameel,Camille Cobb,Adam Bates,Brad Reaves*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过对20款Android生育追踪应用的网络测量研究，发现其中5款应用存在用户健康数据显性泄露（通过TLS剥离流量）和隐性泄露（通过上下文定向广告URL），同时部分应用虽采用广告模式但未发现数据泄露，揭示了用户隐私担忧的技术基础以及消费者选择的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生育追踪应用的用户隐私问题已有很多人因学研究，但技术层面的数据处理实践（尤其是与第三方广告服务的数据共享）缺乏关注。

Method: 对Google Play商店中20款Android生育追踪应用进行基于网络的测量研究，系统化应用功能后在记录TLS剥离流量的环境中执行标准化用户交互，分析网络流量中的第三方广告服务数据共享行为。

Result: 5款应用存在显性健康数据泄露和隐性泄露（通过上下文定向广告URL）；部分应用采用广告模式但无数据泄露；另有应用与广告服务交互极少。

Conclusion: 研究为用户的广泛隐私担忧提供了技术依据，同时强调了消费者选择在应用隐私影响中的重要性。

Abstract: While human factors in the privacy of fertility tracking apps -- health trackers that record user's menstrual or pregnancy data -- has been the subject of extensive study, little attention has been paid to the technical aspects of apps' data handling practices. We conduct a network-based measurement study of a corpus of 20 Android fertility tracking apps from the Google Play Store, focusing on how user data is shared with third party advertising services. After systematizing app features, we conduct a series of standardized user interactions across all apps in an environment that records TLS-stripped network traffic. In a subset of apps (n=5) we identify explicit leakage of user health data as well implicit leakage through highly targeted contextual advertising URL's. Equally importantly, we observe additional apps that use an ad-based monetization model without apparent leakage of user data, as well as several apps the interact only minimally with ad services. These findings provide technical grounding for widespread user concerns, but also underscore the importance of consumer choice in the privacy implications of app-based fertility tracking.

</details>


### [208] [TEMPO-Diffusion: Temporally Exposed Malicious Poisoning of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26285)
*William Aiken,Paula Branco,Guy-Vincent Jourdan,Iosif-Viorel Onut*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TEMPO-Diffusion，一种针对扩散模型的时间条件性后门攻击框架，支持针对特定类的定向攻击、多子图像后门和基于时间触发器的图像修复。同时引入CALISA数据集，实验表明该方法能有效毒化类特定合成数据生成并在下游分类器中实现高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于噪声的后门攻击依赖输入时触发注入、非定向激活和分布外目标生成，降低了攻击的隐蔽性和实际相关性。本文旨在解决这些问题，提出更具隐蔽性和实用性的定向后门攻击方法。

Method: 提出TEMPO-Diffusion框架，将恶意分布偏移定位到时间维度的分布内曝光。支持三类攻击：(i)针对特定类的定向攻击，(ii)多子图像后门（在多个不同输出图像的多个位置重建特定特征），(iii)基于时间条件触发器的图像修复。同时引入CALISA数据集用于评估。

Result: 在CIFAR10、GTSRB和CALISA三个数据集上的实验表明，TEMPO-Diffusion能够可靠地毒化类特定合成数据生成，并在使用该数据训练的下游分类器中诱导高攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 本文验证了基于时间条件性后门攻击的可行性和有效性，为扩散模型的安全研究提供了新的视角和评估基准。

Abstract: Noise-based backdoor attacks on diffusion models typically rely on input-time trigger injection, untargeted activation, and out-of-distribution target generation. Such assumptions reduce both the stealthiness and the practical relevance of these attacks. In this work, we present TEMPO-Diffusion, a targeted backdoor framework that localizes the malicious distribution shift to a temporal, in-distribution exposure. TEMPO-Diffusion supports: (i) targeted attacks on and to specific classes, (ii) multiple sub-image backdoors that reconstruct specific features within multiple, different output images and at multiple locations, and (iii) in-painting with time-conditioned triggers. To study relevant, practical security concerns in leveraging backdoored diffusion models for synthetic training data, we also introduce CALISA: a balanced, region-aware traffic-sign dataset emphasizing Canadian and U.S. road signs. Across CIFAR10, GTSRB, and CALISA, our experiments show that TEMPO-Diffusion can reliably poison class-specific synthetic data generation and induce high attack success rates in downstream classifiers trained on that data.

</details>


### [209] [Beyond Takedown: Measuring Malicious Go Module Persistence in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26291)
*Minjae Bae,Carter Yagemann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文测量了Go生态系统中基于自动化的供应链攻击。攻击者将合法Go模块重新打包到攻击者控制的仓库下，并嵌入混淆代码作为导入触发的下载器。通过手动搜索2,113个GitHub仓库和大规模扫描1230万索引条目（使用自研的AST反混淆扫描器GOAST），我们识别出2,289个恶意版本的合法Go模块。研究显示，纯GitHub搜索无法完全识别攻击范围，仅在受影响代码存在于平台上时才有效。代理测量显示，在后来无法从GitHub观测到的工件中，至少99.4%仍可通过Go代理检索。披露后，GitHub移除了684个恶意仓库，Google Go团队修复了1,377个模块版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Go生态系统中存在自动化供应链攻击，攻击者通过重打包合法模块并嵌入恶意代码来传播恶意软件，现有检测方法（如GitHub搜索）存在盲区，需要系统和全面地测量攻击的范围、持久性和影响。

Method: 采用两种互补分析方法：1) 手动搜索GitHub上2,113个仓库；2) 使用自研AST反混淆扫描器GOAST对1230万索引条目进行大规模扫描。同时通过代理测量评估takedown-remediation gap。

Result: 识别出2,289个恶意版本的合法Go模块；在GitHub无法观测的工件中，至少99.4%仍可通过Go代理检索；GitHub移除了684个恶意仓库，Google Go团队修复了1,377个模块版本。

Conclusion: 纯GitHub中心化的搜索方法不足以全面发现Go生态中的供应链攻击，且仅在恶意代码存续于平台上时有效。Takedown-remediation gap严重，绝大多数已被移除的恶意模块仍可通过Go代理获取，需要更全面的检测与响应机制。

Abstract: We measure an automation-based supply chain campaign in the Go ecosystem. The attackers repackage legitimate Go modules under attacker-controlled owners, and embed them with obfuscated code for an import-triggered downloader. Our results come from two complementary analyses: a) a manual search on GitHub across 2,113 repositories and b) a large-scale scan of 12.3M index entries using a deobfuscating AST scanner (GOAST) that we implemented. As a result, we identified 2,289 malicious versions of legitimate Go modules. We demonstrate that purely GitHub-centric searches fail to identify the full extent of the compromise and are only effective for as long as the affected code is present on the platform. Moreover, our proxy-based measurements of the takedown-remediation gap reveal that among artifacts later found to be GitHub-unobservable (i.e., removed or suspended), at least 99.4% remained retrievable via Go proxy. Following our disclosure, GitHub has removed 684 malicious repositories and the Google Go team has remediated 1,377 module versions.

</details>


### [210] [Hybrid privacy-aware semantic search: SVD-truncated document geometry and CKKS-encrypted query reranking under a restricted threat model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26373)
*Sergey Kurilenko*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合保护方案：文档向量通过SVD截断+秘密旋转进行几何保护，查询向量通过CKKS同态加密进行密码学保护，在百万级文档规模下实现亚秒级延迟，并保持排序质量。实验证明该方案能有效抵御嵌入逆向攻击，同时明确了文档保护是经验性混淆层而非密码学原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的嵌入向量保护方案存在两难：全同态加密虽然安全但在大规模文档场景下速度太慢，而添加隐私噪声则会在保护生效前就严重破坏排序质量。作者希望利用静态集合与动态查询之间的不对称性，寻找一条折中路径。

Method: 集合端：将每个向量截断到低维SVD子空间，并乘以只有所有者知道的秘密正交矩阵进行旋转。查询端：使用CKKS同态加密对查询进行重排序，使得服务器无法看到查询向量和相似度分数。CKKS参数通过小规模离线基准测试确定。

Result: 在100万文档和5种编码器上，方案保持甚至轻微提升了排序质量（对强编码器起到线性降噪作用），延迟在亚秒级。现成的逆向攻击在保护空间上崩塌至噪声水平。更强大的已知明文攻击者通过正交Procrustes方法大约需要泄漏与保留维度相等数量的配对才能恢复旋转矩阵。

Conclusion: 查询机密性达到密码学级别，但文档保护是经验性混淆层（SVD截断+秘密旋转）而非密码学原语。论文为每个声明界定了威胁模型和局限性。

Abstract: Dense embeddings power semantic search and retrieval-augmented generation, but embedding-inversion attacks can reconstruct source text from a vector: when a vector database leaks, the documents behind it leak too. The textbook defences are extremes - encrypting the whole search homomorphically is sound but too slow at million-document scale, while privacy noise degrades ranking long before it protects. We study a middle path exploiting the asymmetry between the static collection and the dynamic query. The collection is protected geometrically: each vector is truncated onto a lower-dimensional SVD subspace and rotated by a secret orthogonal transform known only to the owner. The query is protected cryptographically: it is reranked under CKKS homomorphic encryption, so an honest-but-curious server never sees the query or the scores. CKKS parameters come from a small offline benchmark.
  We prove a tight lower bound on the reconstruction error of any attacker confined to the protected subspace. On one million documents and five encoders the scheme preserves ranking quality (slightly improving it on strong encoders, as a linear denoiser) at sub-second latency, and an off-the-shelf inversion attack on the protected space collapses to the noise floor. We then test stronger adversaries: a known-plaintext attacker recovers the rotation by orthogonal Procrustes from about as many leaked pairs as the retained dimension; the public product-quantization codes preserve most nearest-neighbour structure; and random-projection, calibrated-noise and BEIR baselines show the truncation is an encoder-dependent accuracy cost, not a free denoiser. We state the limits: query confidentiality is cryptographic, but document protection is an empirical obfuscation layer (SVD truncation plus a secret rotation), not a cryptographic primitive, and we delimit the threat model for each claim.

</details>


### [211] [Verifying Intent and Harm: A Unified Defense Against LLM-Generated Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26377)
*Poojitha Thota,Yun Lei,Santhosh Thangaraj,Siddhartha Reddy Jonnalagadda,Shirin Nilizadeh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种验证为中心的防御框架，联合评估提示意图和响应危害性，在五种威胁类别上平均F1从0.90提升至0.95，攻击成功率降至4.1%，优于单侧防御和单智能体推理基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法仅分析用户提示或生成输出中的一方，但许多真实攻击利用提示中的对抗意图与响应中的实际危害之间的分离，导致单侧防御容易漏检不安全交互。

Method: 提出验证为中心的防御框架，包含专门分析提示意图和响应危害的分析器，以及用于冲突解决的法官模块，对提示-响应攻击进行联合验证。

Result: 在越狱、提示注入、钓鱼、网络滥用和有害内容五类威胁上，平均F1从0.90提升至0.95，攻击成功率降至4.1%；相比单智能体+CoT基线，F1从0.87提升至0.95，假阳性率从0.12降至0.06。

Conclusion: 提示-响应联合验证为保障LLM应用免受对抗威胁提供了实用基础，即使面对知晓验证结构的自适应攻击仍具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in interactive applications, yet they remain vulnerable to adversarial interactions that induce harmful, deceptive, or policy-violating outputs. Existing defenses typically analyze either user prompts or generated outputs, but not both. However, many real-world attacks exploit a separation between adversarial intent expressed in the prompt and actionable harm manifested only in the response. As a result, prompt-only and response-only defenses frequently miss unsafe interactions that appear benign when viewed from either side in isolation. We present a verification-centric defense framework that jointly evaluates prompt intent and response harm before an LLM response is delivered to a user. The framework employs specialized analysts for intent and harm assessment together with a Judge for conflict resolution. We formalize a threat model for prompt-response attacks and evaluate the framework across five threat categories: jailbreaks, prompt injection, phishing, cyber abuse, and harmful content. Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets show that jointly verifying prompt intent and response harm consistently outperforms single-sided defenses and single-agent reasoning baselines. Across threat categories, the framework improves average F1 from 0.90 for the strongest applicable baselines to 0.95 while reducing the average attack success rate to 4.1 percent. Compared with a Single-Agent+CoT baseline, it improves average F1 from 0.87 to 0.95 and reduces the false positive rate on benign-sensitive requests from 0.12 to 0.06. We further evaluate architecture-aware adaptive attacks in which the attacker knows the verifier structure and attempts to bypass individual verification components. Our results suggest that prompt-response verification provides a practical foundation for securing LLM applications against evolving adversarial threats.

</details>


### [212] [Lessons from the Adoption and Deprecation of the Privacy Sandbox Web APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26390)
*Yohan Beugin,Paul Barford,Patrick McDaniel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对Google Privacy Sandbox项目（2019-2025）进行了长达七年的纵向测量与分析，揭示了其API采纳率有限且不均衡，最终项目被取消。作者从激励机制、风险和浏览器差异角度解释了失败原因，并为下一代Web隐私提案提出了建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多年来多个Web参与者试图减少网络跟踪，但如何在效用和隐私之间取得理想平衡仍不明确。Google于2019年启动Privacy Sandbox以解决这一权衡，但该项目于2025年底被取消。作者希望借此机会研究生态系统对提议变化的反应，分析其失败原因。

Method: 利用历史HTTP Archive爬取数据和公开的Chrome遥测数据，对每个Privacy Sandbox API在其整个生命周期内（部分超过5年）在CrUX前10万热门网站上的采纳情况进行纵向测量，并结合Chrome用户浏览行为数据进行大规模分析。

Result: 采纳率有限且不均衡；只有少数Web参与者实现了非常特定的API，且实现方式各异；第三方Cookie限制在Chrome中仍以选择加入（opt-in）为主，而Brave、Firefox、Safari等浏览器已默认启用。

Conclusion: Privacy Sandbox的失败揭示了"在浏览器中修复"方案的局限性以及不同浏览器之间的差异。作者为下一代Web隐私提案提供了可操作的建议。

Abstract: While several web actors have been trying to reduce web tracking for years, it remains unclear how to achieve both desirable levels of utility and privacy. In 2019, Google launched the Privacy Sandbox initiative to balance that trade-off and find privacy alternatives to common use cases such as advertising. Yet, in late 2025, Google canceled the project and deprecated most of the newly introduced APIs. Despite its end, the Privacy Sandbox represents a unique opportunity to learn about how the ecosystem reacted to the proposed changes and make observations about why and how it failed. In this paper, we present a longitudinal measurement and analysis study of the Privacy Sandbox APIs to characterize their adoption and deprecation over the past seven years by different web actors. Leveraging historical HTTP Archive crawls and public Chrome telemetry data, we offer the largest study of its kind into the prevalence of each Privacy Sandbox feature, during their entire respective lifetime (5+ years for some), on popular websites (CrUX top 100k), and as experienced by Chrome users during their browsing journey. Our results showcase an adoption that remained limited and uneven across the years; only few web actors implemented very specific APIs, and in disparate manners. We motivate our interpretation of these results by considering the incentives (interest, resources, timeline, etc.) and risks (potential trade-offs, privacy violations, and legal exposure, etc.) for these actors. Finally, our analysis also yields actionable recommendations for the next generation of web privacy proposals. More broadly, the Privacy Sandbox illustrates the limitations and disparities across browsers of ``fix it in the browser'' remedies: today, tracking and third-party cookies limitations in Chrome still remain largely opt-in, while they have been enabled by default on other browsers like Brave, Firefox, or Safari.

</details>


### [213] [What Browsers Do in the Shaders: A Measurement Study of WebGPU Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26412)
*Igor Santos-Grueiro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出WGPULens框架，系统测量WebGPU在浏览器中暴露的隐私信号。研究发现持久化管线编译状态是最清晰的隐私暴露面，可通过冷/热管线探测获取跨来源、用户和浏览器配置的编译信息；原生GPU活动也可通过浏览器可见可观测信号推断。参与者研究表明WebGPU行为具有高度区分性，网页爬虫显示实际使用主要限于适配器探测。缓解措施试点表明源码级密钥分离可作为管线缓存分区评估的代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebGPU虽然通过验证编程模型保证了内存安全，但共享的浏览器、驱动、操作系统和GPU状态仍可能暴露与隐私相关的信号，因此需要系统化的测量框架来评估这些隐私风险。

Method: WGPULens框架采用多维度测量方法：1)受控场景测量（泄漏边界、缓解措施）；2)参与者实地研究（部署兼容性、可指纹识别性）；3)Tranco大规模网页爬虫（真实页面中WebGPU暴露情况）。通过冷/暖管线探测、原生GPU活动推断等实验设计进行隐私信号量化。

Result: 1)持久化管线编译状态是最清晰的隐私暴露面；2)原生GPU活动可从浏览器可见信号推断；3)WebGPU行为在参与者样本中高度区分性，确定性组件在单次运行内稳定但跨次访问稳定性较低；4)网页爬虫显示WebGPU主要用于适配器探测和静态支持代码，未观察到实际着色器/管线/队列等执行活动。

Conclusion: WebGPU隐私分析必须针对特定表面进行：浏览器需要测量哪些GPU状态跨越哪些边界、哪些浏览器可见信号暴露这些状态、以及相应缓解措施的成本。

Abstract: WebGPU lets ordinary web pages run GPU workloads through a validated programming model.
